- **Summary**: 针对多镜头长视频生成中实体一致性随时间退化的问题，提出了GroundShot框架，通过实体级视觉记忆和镜头调度顺序来保持一致性。在GroundBench基准上，无需训练即可显著提升多镜头一致性。

<h3 id="252">[252] B[FM]$^2$: Brain Foundation Model via Flow Matching with SplitUNet</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20812](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20812) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jaedong Hwang, Kathleen Zhang, Wei Dai, Konstantinos Kontras, Maarten Vanmarcke, Maarten De Vos, Ila Fiete, Paul Pu Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Foundation Model, Flow Matching, Brain-Computer Interface
- **Summary**: 针对现有EEG基础模型离散化连续信号丢失细粒度动态的问题，提出了B[FM]^2模型，直接在原始信号上使用连续时间流匹配预训练，并设计了SplitUNet速度网络处理时间-电极不对称性。在7/9个分类任务上达到最先进性能，生成合成EEG与真实脑电图无差异。

<h3 id="253">[253] Translating Inference-Time Control to Radiology Vision-Language Models: Activation Steering for Pneumonia Classification on Chest X-rays</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20852) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eduardo Moreno Judice de Mattos Farina, Mateus A. Esmeraldo, Felipe Akio Matsuoka, Paulo Eduardo de Aguiar Kuriki, Felipe Campos Kitamura
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Activation Steering
- **Summary**: 为了评估推理时控制（如激活引导）是否能提升医学视觉语言模型的诊断性能，对三个胸部X光片VLM应用对比激活添加（CAA）。在NV-Reason-CXR-3B模型上，肺炎分类F1分数从0.7692提升至0.8727，表明激活引导可作为轻量级适配方法。

<h3 id="254">[254] FOCA: Future-Oriented Conditioning for Data-Efficient Vision-Language-Action Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20867](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20867) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Duc Minh Nguyen, Nghiem Tuong Diep, Binh Gia Nguyen, Trong-Bao Ho, Doanh Le, Tan Q. Nguyen, Thien-Loc Ha, Nhiem Tran, Bao Thach, Nhat X. Tran, Tuan A. Tran, Artur Habuda, Philip Lund Møller, Tran Nguyen Le, Daniel Sonntag, Matthias Niepert, Khoa D. Doan, Vu Duong, Hung Ngo, Minh N. Vu, Duy M. H. Nguyen, An Thai Le, Ngo Anh Vien
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action, Robotics, Few-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-行动模型在少样本模仿学习中性能急剧下降的问题，提出了FOCA框架，通过未来导向的条件建模和隐空间对齐实现数据高效适配。在LIBERO任务中以20个演示达到95.7%成功率，真实机器人任务中绝对增益最高达26%。

<h3 id="255">[255] Can LLMs Reason About Brand Ownership? An Empirical Study of Domain Attribution Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20868](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20868) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fathima Mashood, Mohamed Nabeel
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Cybersecurity, Phishing Detection
- **Summary**: 针对域名归属判断的模糊性问题，对四个LLM在品牌域名枚举、归属和所有权分类任务上进行了系统评估。结果显示，模型在枚举上精度达82%，但所有权分类在无外部工具时F1仅0.37；WHOIS查询辅助将F1提升0.65，精度接近完美。

<h3 id="256">[256] A3C3: AI Algorithm and Accelerator Co-design, Co-search, and Co-generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Selin Yildirim, Yingbing Huang, Deming Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hardware-Software Co-design, DNN Deployment, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 针对传统AI系统设计中算法与硬件分离导致次优系统的问题，提出了A3C3方法，联合参数化算法和加速器设计空间并进行协同搜索。该方法可自动生成平衡精度、延迟、吞吐量和能效的模型-加速器对。

<h3 id="257">[257] Machine Learning Classification of Cryopathy Syndromes: A Comprehensive Comparative Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20874) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nataliya Shakhovska, Valentyna Chopyak, Ivan Izonin, Vira Haievska
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Ensemble Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对冷球蛋白血症综合征分类困难的问题（实验室模式重叠、罕见诊断多），比较了12种机器学习策略，包括随机森林、梯度提升树、多层感知机、集成学习等，并构建了临床信息交互特征。结果表明，随机森林与梯度提升树的软投票集成获得最佳多分类性能，树模型优于神经网络，且在交叉验证中平衡随机森林表现稳定。

<h3 id="258">[258] Artificial collectives of specialists and generalists excel at different tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20877](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20877) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: John Meluso, Laurent Hébert-Dufresne, Christoph Riedl, H. Oliver Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.SI; nlin.AO
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Agent-Based Model
- **Summary**: 该论文旨在科学理解人工集体智能的设计原则，通过系统实验研究智能体解释能力（专家vs通才）、理性界限和任务特性对集体性能的影响。发现专家和通才的集体在不同任务上各有优势：通才在生成、选择和协调任务上更好，而专家加通才中介在谈判任务上更优；理性界限调节这些关系。

<h3 id="259">[259] The Substrate Collapse: AI Code Generation Invalidates Authorship-Based Knowledge Metrics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Brett Wheeler
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文指出AI代码生成破坏了基于作者身份的知识度量（如卡车因子）的推理基础，因为AI生成的代码不再保证作者理解。论证了该类度量已失效，并提出需要基于理解证据的新度量，并给出可验证的预测：有良好作者身份但理解保持低的系统将出现事件解决失败。

<h3 id="260">[260] Exploiting Neural Audio Codec Latents for Adversarial Audio Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20893) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sameek Bhattacharya, Bharath Krishnamurthy, Ajita Rattani
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/vcbsl/DAC-GAN), [code](https://github.com/Bharath-K3/DAC-GAN)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对音频分类系统对抗攻击的实时性难题，提出一种在神经音频编解码器连续潜在空间中生成对抗样本的框架。条件生成器单次前向传播合成类别特定扰动，实现99%的有目标攻击成功率，推理时间低于7毫秒，延迟降低24倍。

<h3 id="261">[261] PeerCheck: Enhancing LLM-Generated Academic Reviews Towards Human-Level Quality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20897](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20897) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zeyuan Chen, Ziqing Yang, Yihan Ma, Michael Backes, Yang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering, RAG
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TrustAIRLab/PeerCheck)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM生成学术评审质量不足的问题，提出PeerCheck框架，分析LLM与人类评审差异（LLM侧重理论，人类强调方法与实验），并采用思维链和RAG提升质量。发现思维链显著改进，但RAG存在“RAG悖论”，有时反而降低质量。

<h3 id="262">[262] The Token Tax of Epistemic Accuracy: Comparing RAG and Long-Context Architectures for Document-Grounded Generative AI Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20898](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20898) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Austin Hamilton, Ryan Singh, Michael Wise, Ibrahim Yousif, Arthur Carvalho, Zhe Shan, Mohammad Mayyas, Lora A. Cavuoto, Fadel M. Megahed
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: RAG, Long Context
- **Summary**: 该论文比较RAG与长上下文提示架构在文档嵌入式生成应用中的准确性-成本权衡，以制造业安全培训为案例。长上下文提示实现73.1%正确率（RAG为65.4%），但token成本高26倍，揭示了“token税”。

<h3 id="263">[263] Vesta: A Generalist Embodied Reasoning Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20905](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20905) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Johan Bjorck, Zhiqi Li, Yunze Man, Jing Wang, An-Chieh Cheng, Sifei Liu, Shihao Wang, Zhiding Yu, Abhishek Badki, Stan Birchfield, Valts Blukis, Yevgen Chebotar, Siyi Chen, Sicong Leng, Yu-Cheng Chou, Tianli Ding, Boyi Li, Zhengyi Luo, Hang Su, Jonathan Tremblay, Tingwu Wang, Bowen Wen, Jimmy Wu, Xianghui Xie, Hanrong Ye, Hongxu Yin, K.R. Zentner, Liangyan Gui, Yu-Xiong Wang, Yuke Zhu, Linxi &#34;Jim&#34; Fan, Jan Kautz
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Robotics, Visual Navigation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对机器人需集成定位、空间推理、导航和长时规划的问题，提出统一具身通才模型Vesta，结合大规模空间引导语料库和多模态记忆模块。在多个基准上平均超越专业模型\>20%，优于集成\>10%，在实际机器人任务中成功率提升\>35%。

<h3 id="264">[264] MMGNN: Multi-level, multi-color graph neural networks for molecular property prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20906) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Trung Nguyen, Duc Duy Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; q-bio.MN
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Molecular Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对分子图神经网络混合不同相互作用信号的问题，提出多级多色图神经网络MMGNN，将分子图分解为原子对特异性子图。MMGNN-2D在分类任务上宏平均AUC-ROC达0.838，MMGNN-3D在BBBP上AUC-ROC达0.956，显示拓扑与几何表示的互补优势。

<h3 id="265">[265] BELDE: Building a Large-scale Earth-observation Land-cover Dataset for Europe</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20909) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ümit Mert Çağlar, Alptekin Temizel
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Semantic Segmentation, Dataset
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有土地覆盖分割数据集在地理覆盖和规模上的不足，构建了BELDE数据集：108万对Sentinel-2真彩色图像与ESA WorldCover标签，覆盖欧洲7个类别，10米分辨率。基线上欧洲测试F1为83.0%，但在韩国和美国区域分别降至66.4%和58.3%，凸显域偏移挑战。

<h3 id="266">[266] Whose Agent Are You? Multi-Layer Fingerprinting and Attribution of Autonomous Web Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20910) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dayeon Kang, Hyejun Jeong, Jade Sheffey, Pubali Datta, Amir Houmansadr
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Web Security
- **Summary**: 该论文针对AI网页代理无差别内容抓取带来的隐私和安全挑战，提出基于网络层（TLS、HTTP）和浏览器交互行为的多层指纹识别方法。在六种代理框架上测试，决策树分类器以97%准确率区分代理流量与人类和传统爬虫。

<h3 id="267">[267] Latent Personal Memory: Represent personal memory as dynamic soft prompts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20911](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20911) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Debrup Das, Avinash Amballa, Yashas Malur Saidutta, Vijay Srinivasan, Vivek Kulkarni, Srinivas Chappidi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Memory Architecture, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM个性化需要高效编码长期用户模式的问题，提出潜在个人记忆LPM，将用户历史表示为紧凑的潜在插槽矩阵，通过交叉注意力映射为动态软提示。在PersonaMem v1上比LoRA和Prompt Tuning准确率分别提升8.8%和54.4%，KV-cache减少64倍，参数量减少120倍。

<h3 id="268">[268] PROTON: Prototype-Based Test-Time Online OOD Detection for Medical VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20913) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhijit Das, Nichula Wasalathilaka, Yifan Lu, Adinath Dukre, Dwarikanath Mahapatra, Shadab Khan, Imran Razzak
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Out-of-Distribution Detection, Medical AI, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/PROTON)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对医学视觉语言模型部署时静态OOD检测在协变量偏移上失效的问题，提出PROTON模块，在线维护原型库并自适应融合原型距离与最大概念匹配分数。在FLAIR+FIVES基准上，协变量偏移AUROC提升23.9，语义偏移提升8.8，远OOD提升8.1，且无需修改模型或标注数据。

<h3 id="269">[269] Short-Term Electricity Demand Forecasting for New England Using a Hybrid Transformer-XGBoost Framework with Weather, Calendar, and COVID-19 Indicators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20918](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20918) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reza Ghanavati, Behrooz Mosallaei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Energy Management
- **Summary**: 该论文针对新英格兰短期电力需求预测，提出Transformer编码器与XGBoost的混合框架，整合气象、日历和COVID-19变量。混合模型测试RMSE为8876 MWh，MAPE 2.05%，R² 0.906，但与纯XGBoost差异不显著。消融实验显示COVID特征加剧过拟合。

<h3 id="270">[270] Peeking Inside LLMs: Leveraging Internal Artifacts of LLMs for Enhancing Reliability in Legal Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20929](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20929) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sudipta Santra, Debtanu Datta, Saptarshi Ghosh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Legal AI, Interpretability
- **Venue**: International Workshop on Automated Semantic Analysis of Information in Law (ASAIL) 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文针对法律领域LLM输出不可靠的问题，利用LLM内部工件（如隐藏状态）构建下游分类器以检测预测正确性。在保释判决和法规违反预测任务上，内部工件是可靠的错误预测指示器，能提升LLM分类系统的可靠性。

<h3 id="271">[271] Comparing Transformers and Hybrid Models at the Token Level</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20936](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20936) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanhong Li, William Merrill
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Hybrid Language Model
- **Summary**: 该论文探究混合语言模型（注意力+循环层）相比纯Transformer的优势来源，通过token级损失对比分析。混合模型在多数标签上损失更低，尤其对开放类实词和句首分隔符；Transformer在括号匹配和n-gram复制上更优，表明循环层改善语义状态跟踪，注意力利于句法匹配。

<h3 id="272">[272] Learning What Not to Forget: Long-Horizon Agent Memory from a Few Kilobytes of Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20954](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20954) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nusrat Jahan Lia, Aritra Mazumder
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 该论文针对长期LLM代理历史超出上下文窗口时的信息遗忘问题，提出学习型相关性驱逐LRE，仅需几KB的CPU轻量打分器，学习保留关键历史单元。在代理任务上准确率匹敌保留全部历史，在最简单任务上超过27%，峰值上下文大小减少52%，且无监督训练可达95%监督效果。

<h3 id="273">[273] Is Our Benchmark Enough? An Analysis of Continual Learning for MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20961](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20961) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Van-Tuan Tran, Shruthi Gowda, Merim Dzaferagic, Marco Ruffini
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Multimodal LLM, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 论文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）持续学习基准的局限，指出MR-LoRA方法依赖复杂MLLM路由器和共享专家的问题。提出无需训练的回放原型路由方法RePRo，证明路由不需要MLLM且共享专家无益。发现现有基准MLLM-CL因任务高分离和固定顺序而奖励孤立学习，提出重叠任务流形、多任务顺序等新基准设计。

<h3 id="274">[274] Detecting Satellites in Radio-Frequency Data via Semi-Supervised Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20976](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20976) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cade W. Trotter, Maksim E. Eren, Justin C. Holmes, J. Brent Parham, David Ewing, Boian S. Alexandrov, Gian Luca Delzanno
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Semi-Supervised Learning, Satellite Systems
- **Summary**: 论文针对射频监测数据量大、稀疏且标签少的问题，提出结合非负矩阵分解（NMFk）、专家解释和分类器的半监督工作流。通过无监督分解揭示模式，由专家赋予物理含义，再训练分类器预测。减少了对标注数据的依赖，实现了可解释的卫星检测。

<h3 id="275">[275] Robusto-2: Benchmarking Humans & VLMs for Autonomous Driving in Lima & New York City</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20980](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20980) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adrian Cespedes, Marcelo Chincha, Dunant Cusipuma, Victor Flores-Benites, David Ortega, Arturo Deza
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 论文针对视觉语言模型在自动驾驶跨地理OOD场景中的泛化能力，收集利马和纽约人类驾驶员与VLM的VQA数据进行分析。发现人类和VLM回答有差异但受问题类型调节，且地理因素对双方影响不显著。

<h3 id="276">[276] Text-to-Image Generative AI for Modeling and Simulation: Methods, Opportunities, and Applications</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20991) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Philippe J. Giabbanelli
- **Subjects**: cs.ET; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Survey
- **Venue**: 2026 Winter Simulation Conference
- **Summary**: 论文针对图像生成AI在建模与仿真中应用不足，介绍文本到图像生成支持概念模型可视化、仿真结果可视化等任务。提供概念指导和实用工作流，解释生成器原理及如何集成到可复现流水线中。

<h3 id="277">[277] The Metanym Game: A Self-Contained, Self-Consistent LLM Peer-Community Benchmark for Structural Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21008) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Nordfors
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Analogical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 论文提出metanym游戏作为LLM结构智能的竞技基准，自生成内容且防泄露。通过评委矩阵奇异值分解同时评估生成和评价能力，事实评分与GPQA Diamond高度相关（r=0.92），发现评价能力更稀缺。基准自洽且稳定。

<h3 id="278">[278] LK Jam: System Architecture and Implementation of a Real-Time Human-AI Interactive Music Generation System using Role-Aware GRU</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21018) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yakun Liu, Zhiyu Jin, Dong Liu, Hai Luan
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Real-Time Systems, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 论文针对实时人机交互音乐生成需低延迟动态交互的问题，提出LK_Jam系统，基于轻量级GRU和JUCE框架的多线程无锁通信，采用多维稀疏事件流和角色感知编码。三阶段训练从基础到专家级交互，在保证音乐连贯的同时实现极低延迟。

<h3 id="279">[279] CheXpercept: A Benchmark for Evaluating Expert-Level Lesion Perception in Chest X-rays</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21020) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Geon Choi, Hangyul Yoon, Nalee Kim, Jeong Yun Jang, Hyunju Shin, Hyunki Park, Sang Hoon Seo, Edward Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 论文针对胸部X光VLM评估缺乏视觉定位和专家级感知验证的问题，提出CheXpercept基准，模拟放射科医生工作流的三级任务。构建包含10,400 QA对的数据集，测试14个VLM发现仅在粗粒度表现好，医学VLM无优势。

<h3 id="280">[280] Structure-Aware Graph Multi-Task Learning for Dynamic Sparse OD Demand Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21022) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ming Xu, Jiawei Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Prediction, Graph Neural Network, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: 论文针对动态稀疏OD需求预测中零流量模式异质的问题，提出结构感知图多任务学习框架SAGMTL。分解为结构状态和流量强度，通过节点-边协作表示和联合建模，在三个城市数据集上优于基线。

<h3 id="281">[281] MoECodec: Image Compression for joint human and machine perception via Mixture-of-Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21033) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiancheng Zhao, Xiang Ji, Yifan Zhan, Zunian Wan, Yinqiang Zheng
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Image Compression, Mixture-of-Experts, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 论文针对图像压缩中静态计算忽略区域语义差异的问题，提出MoECodec，用token级混合专家替代FFN，结合专家选择路由和空间正则化，并设计轻量级专家架构。在重建和机器任务上均提升。

<h3 id="282">[282] Backdoor Attacks on Speech Emotion Recognition via TTS-Generated Poisoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21052](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21052) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongbin Huang, Xihao Xie, Jia Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Speech Emotion Recognition, Text-to-Speech
- **Venue**: IEEE Cyber AI 2026
- **Summary**: 论文针对语音情感识别训练时易受后门攻击的漏洞，首次系统研究基于TTS生成音频的投毒后门。提出隐蔽低能量触发器，低投毒率即可高成功率攻击，且触发向量可跨模型迁移，自监督表示尤其脆弱。

<h3 id="283">[283] Imitation Learning for Elder-Facing Speech Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21053](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21053) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongrui Han, Weidong Chen, Jiawen Kang, Mingyu Cui, Helen Meng, Xixin Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Imitation Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 论文针对TTS系统忽略老年人语音理解需求，提出模仿学习框架从专家演示学习，并改进GRPO为两阶段在线策略奖励学习缓解奖励黑客。实验在客观和主观指标上优于基线。

<h3 id="284">[284] Local LLM Agents as Vulnerable Runtimes:A Source-Code Audit of the Agent Runtime Layer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21071](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21071) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengsong Zhang, Zongze Li, Jiawei Guo, Haipeng Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Static Analysis
- **Summary**: 论文针对本地LLM代理运行时层的安全漏洞，提出CLAWAUDIT审计框架，基于STRIDE分类和定制静态分析规则。在OPENCLAWBENCH上Semgrep召回率66.8%，CodeQL 75.1%，规则泛化良好。

<h3 id="285">[285] An Efficient and Effective Architecture for Large-Scale Traffic Prediction via Geometry-Adaptive Square Partitioning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21072](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21072) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongfeng Su, Hongwen Li, Zijian Zhang, Ziquan Fang, Lu Chen, Christian S. Jensen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Prediction, Spatiotemporal Forecasting, Model Architecture
- **Summary**: 论文针对大规模交通预测中计算可扩展性问题，提出SqLinear架构，包含几何自适应Square Partition和层次线性交互模块。在四个数据集上MAE降低2.30%-5.81%，训练时间减少13%-31%。

<h3 id="286">[286] FiLM-Coordinated Dual-Branch Transformer for Global-Local Dependency Modeling in Language Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21075](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21075) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiqiang Zhou, Xu Ling, Junliang Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Language Modeling, Feature-wise Linear Modulation
- **Summary**: 论文针对标准Transformer难以同时建模全局和局部依赖，提出FiLM协调的双分支结构，用特征线性调制进行跨分支协调。在小规模语言建模中优于同宽基线，且FiLM学到输入和层依赖的调制模式。

<h3 id="287">[287] Scalable Hierarchical Attention Transformers for Multi-Turn Jailbreak Detection in Long Conversations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21082](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21082) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenhui Hu, Muhammed Salih, Sudipto Guha, Subramanian Srinivasan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Detection, LLM Safety, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 论文针对多轮越狱攻击在对话中扩散的问题，提出分层检测器编码轮次表示并应用对话模块捕获动态。在14k对话基准上F1达0.9394，优于Claude Opus 4.7，假阳性率减半。

<h3 id="288">[288] Sim2O: Efficient Offline-to-Online MARL via Joint Action Composition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21085](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21085) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bingchang Song, Yiqin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Offline RL, Online Learning
- **Summary**: 论文针对多智能体离线到在线适应问题，提出Sim2O框架，将适应视为联合动作组合过程，混合离线在线动作并通过集中值函数评估。在多个基准上显著优于基线，证明极简设计的有效性。

<h3 id="289">[289] SLeDGe: Semi-Supervised Learning on Data Streams with Graph Structure Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21096) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heechan Moon, Kijung Shin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Semi-Supervised Learning, Graph Learning, Streaming Learning
- **Summary**: 现有方法使用固定相似度度量或静态图结构，无法捕捉数据流中样本关系的演化。本文提出SLeDGe，一种联合学习预测模型和自适应图结构的半监督学习方法，在严格内存和标签约束下平衡快速适应和历史一致性。在12个数据集上，SLeDGe超越了现有方法，在0.1%和1%标签率下分别取得31.7%和14.8%的平均相对准确率提升。

<h3 id="290">[290] LLM-Based Multi-Reference Evaluation for Efficient and Robust Assessment of Phrase Break Annotations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21098) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Younghan Park, Hoyeon Lee, Hawon Jeong, Jong-Hwan Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Speech Processing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 短语边界标注评估存在单一参考假设所有话语只有一种正确韵律划分、人工评估不可扩展的问题。本文提出LMRE，一种基于LLM的多参考评估方法，从少量示范生成多种有效韵律划分。在韩语测试集上，LMRE在接受行为和分数相关性上均比单一参考评估更符合人类判断。

<h3 id="291">[291] Chem2Gen-Bench: Benchmarking Chemical-to-Genetic Translation in Perturbation Response Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21109](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21109) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxiang Lin, Ying Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Drug Discovery
- **Summary**: 化学和遗传扰动在扰动响应空间中不可自动互换，现有评估通常分别研究二者。本文提出Chem2Gen-Bench基准，包含26万化学和110万遗传扰动谱，评估配对对齐、检索等。结果表明翻译保真度可测量但异质，基础模型嵌入未持续优于基线。

<h3 id="292">[292] MS-rPPG: Multi-spectral State Space Model for Remote Photoplethysmography in Driver Monitoring Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21115) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiho Choi, Sang Jun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Health Monitoring, Remote Photoplethysmography
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ziiho08/MS-rPPG)
- **Summary**: 基于摄像头的远程光电容积描记（rPPG）在驾驶场景中受光照变化和头部运动影响。本文提出MS-rPPG，结合RGB和近红外视频的多光谱框架，采用跨光谱线性调制和MS-Mamba状态空间模型。在真实驾驶数据集上，该方法比以往方法更鲁棒，心率估计更准确。

<h3 id="293">[293] ConnectomeBench2: A Unified Benchmark for Automated Connectomic Proofreading</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21116) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeff Brown, Tim Farkas, Gleb Razgar, Edward S. Boyden
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Neuroscience, Vision Transformer
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/timfarkas/ConnectomeBench2)
- **Summary**: 校对是突触分辨率连接组学中的限速步骤。本文发布ConnectomeBench2，包含超过71.6万专家标注的校对决策，跨度四个主要开放连接组。基于该数据集训练的Vision Transformer在分割错误校正和合并错误识别上达到人类水平，性能随数据量和模态增加而提升。

<h3 id="294">[294] MammoExpert: Benchmarking Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Mammography Diagnosis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Di Dai, Bo Liu, Youcheng Li, Haojun Yu, Zhouhang Bian, Quanlin Wu, Dong Wang, Sichen Meng, Hongye Xuan, Zijie Lan, Shenda Hong, Liwei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Benchmark, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 现有乳腺X线摄影数据集规模和标注丰富度有限，缺乏病理亚型覆盖和结构化诊断推理。本文提出MammoExpert，首个包含思维链推理标注的乳腺X线摄影数据集，覆盖67种WHO组织病理学亚型。结合公开数据后分类准确率提升7.1%，CoT推理训练额外提升4%。

<h3 id="295">[295] PoLAR: Factorizing Extent and Mode in Latent Actions for Robot Policy Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21139](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21139) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngjoon Jeong, Jihwan Yu, Minsoo Jo, Junha Chun, Taesup Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Representation Learning, Hyperbolic Learning
- **Summary**: 现有潜在动作预训练将转变程度和模式纠缠在一起。本文提出PoLAR，在潜在动作空间施加径向方向结构，使半径编码转变程度、方向保留转变模式，并在双曲空间中实现。在下游策略学习任务中，PoLAR优于潜在动作基线和强预训练VLA。

<h3 id="296">[296] AgentMeter: Evaluating Model-CLI Matching for CLI-Based Local Task-Solving Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21140) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han Chi, Jiaxin Qi, Yan Cui, Baisheng Lai, Jianqiang Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Benchmark, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有LLM智能体评估强调任务成功率，但忽略了CLI中介的影响。本文提出AgentMeter基准和AgentMeter Score（AMS），评估模型-CLI匹配。在Core30上，不同配置选择不同最佳模型，表明模型和CLI应作为一个整体评估。

<h3 id="297">[297] AdaMem: Learning What to Remember for Personalized Long-Horizon LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21144](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21144) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Chen, Rui Wang, Zhaopeng Tu, Liefeng Bo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, LLM Personalization
- **Summary**: 长期记忆系统通常试图记住一切，导致记忆膨胀。本文提出AdaMem，学习为每个用户记住重要信息，通过结构化记忆策略和轻量级自我反思。在周级QA基准上，AdaMem将准确率提升高达9%，同时压缩记忆体积9%。

<h3 id="298">[298] AOR-Bench: Do Large Audio Language Models Over-Refuse Pseudo-Harmful Queries?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxi Yang, Chaewan Chun, Jason Lucas, Yuchen Yang, Dongwon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, LLM Safety, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 大型音频语言模型可能过度拒绝对良性音频查询。本文提出AOR-Bench，首个专门针对音频过度拒绝的基准，包含3000个伪有害音频样本。评估12个模型发现过度拒绝普遍存在，探索了思维链和激活引导等缓解策略。

<h3 id="299">[299] Context-Aware Generative AI for Automated Telecom Test Script Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21151) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gautam Prasad, Chandramohan T. N., Joy Bose
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Test Generation, RAG
- **Summary**: 现有电信测试生成生成静态测试套件，无法适应系统持续变化。本文提出一种上下文感知生成式AI框架，通过持续更新的知识图谱和增量变化检测，使用LLM智能体根据MCP协议自动创建、更新或退休测试用例。在软件系统和电信网络用例中减少了手动工作量并提高了测试相关性。

<h3 id="300">[300] Contrastive and Adaptive Multi-modal Masked Autoencoder for Spatial Transcriptomics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21156) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joohyeok Kim, Taejin Jeong, Jinyeong Kim, Seong Jae Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Computational Biology, Contrastive Learning, Spatial Transcriptomics
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Kyyle2114/CAMMST)
- **Summary**: 从组织学图像预测基因表达存在固有限制。本文利用部分基因表达作为锚点，采用掩码自编码器结合对比学习，并引入生物显著性分数和排序策略自适应选择最有信息量的位点。在仅用10%转录组覆盖度时即超越现有方法。

<h3 id="301">[301] OmniV2X: A Generative Foundation Planner for Efficient End-to-End Cooperative Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21165](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21165) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juntong Peng, Juanwu Lu, Yupeng Zhou, Can Cui, Yaobin Chen, Ziran Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Foundation Model, V2X
- **Summary**: 现有车联网协同驾驶方法存在计算成本高、数据稀缺及标准兼容性差等问题。本文提出OmniV2X，一种生成式基础模型，通过交叉注意力注入解释多模态多智能体观测，并使用轻量级V2X令牌。在DAIR-V2X-Seq数据集上，仅需不到10%的微调数据和1%的通信带宽即达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="302">[302] An Exploratory Case Study of LLM-Assisted Refactoring and Gameplay Feature Generation in an Endless Runner Game</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21171) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jan Wunderlich, Markus Kleffmann, Sebastian Lempert
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Game AI, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: LLM在游戏开发中的实际可用性尚未充分探索。本文对GPT-4o在无尽跑酷游戏中的6项任务（3项重构、3项玩法特征生成）进行案例研究。结果表明所有重构任务成功，但仅一项玩法特征生成任务成功集成。

<h3 id="303">[303] Inverting the Bellman Equation: From $Q$-Values to World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21173) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alistair Letcher, Mattie Fellows, Alexander D. Goldie, Jonathan Richens, Jakob N. Foerster, Oliver Richardson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, World Model, Model-Based RL
- **Summary**: 基于模型和无模型的强化学习传统上被视为分离范式。本文证明基于价值的智能体在丰富奖励函数下隐式编码世界模型，并引入P-learning从Q值中提取模型。实验表明即使奖励函数有限，智能体也能编码准确动态，并在分布外目标上得到准最优策略。

<h3 id="304">[304] Anatomically Consistent TMJ Disc Segmentation via Semantic Anchoring and Clinical Priors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21177) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dayun Ju, Chanyoung Kim, Sunyoung Jung, Hyo-Jung Jung, Chena Lee, Younjung Park, Seong Jae Hwang
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical Image Segmentation, Medical AI, Semantic Segmentation
- **Summary**: 颞下颌关节盘MRI分割因尺寸小、对比度低和形态变异而不可靠。本文提出TISC框架，结合原型语义锚定和临床元数据引导的边界细化。在2488例PD MRI数据集上，Dice提升最高达4.96，得到更解剖一致的分割结果。

<h3 id="305">[305] TF-SNO: Time-Frequency Gated Spectral Neural Operators for Learning Non-Stationary Partial Differential Equations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yitian Zhou, Chaoning Zhang, Zhenzhen Huang, Haoxuan Yu, Jiaquan Zhang, Yiran Li, Fan Mo, Kuien Liu, Jie Zou, Caiyan Qin, Yang Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural PDE, Scientific Computing
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：非平稳偏微分方程中频率随时间漂移，现有频谱神经算子使用共享频谱响应导致不匹配。方法：提出TF-SNO，通过可学习的时频门控机制，根据当前状态自适应调制频谱响应。结果：在6个非平稳PDE基准上显著降低预测误差，长程 rollout 鲁棒性提升。

<h3 id="306">[306] MEDLAYXPLAIN: Benchmarking the Expert-Lay Gap in Medical Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21194) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Han Jang, Junhyeok Lee, Songsoo Kim, Chae Young Lim, Hyeonjin Goh, Heeseong Eum, Kyu Sung Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：医学视觉语言模型无法生成患者可理解的描述，存在专家-公众差距。方法：提出MedLayXPlain基准，包含12.2万样本；采用分层本体验证精炼(HOVER)流程生成通俗描述；蒸馏3B评估器MedLayEval。结果：在33个模型上发现系统性的专家-公众差距。

<h3 id="307">[307] Beyond Hooking Onto the World: Referential Profiles and the Numerical Structure of LLM Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21195](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21195) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joo Yull Rhee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Cognitive Science, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM的接地问题被过于简化，缺乏数值实现机制。方法：提出基于轮廓的参考理论，认为LLM通过数值结构（权重、激活等）获得派生、语言介导的参考。结果：为增强型参考提供了理论论证，并借助机械可解释性发现（实体特征、知识神经元）提供间接支持。

<h3 id="308">[308] Extraction and Analysis of Multimodal Concepts in Vision Language Models through Sparse Autoencoders</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21197) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sergio Lanza, Jae Hee Lee, Stefan Wermter
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Multimodal Learning, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PHDLanza/Multidata_SAE)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有稀疏自编码器只提取文本或视觉概念，忽略多模态概念，且视觉概念描述质量低。方法：基于SAE的框架提取视觉、文本和多模态概念，通过余弦相似度计算对齐。结果：视觉概念质量提升高达45%，同时保持文本概念质量，系统识别多模态概念。

<h3 id="309">[309] Communication Heterogeneity and Collective Consensus in Neural Cellular Automata</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21202) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nishit Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Emergent Behavior, Neural Cellular Automaton
- **Summary**: 问题：集体共识通常假设单一通信协议，本文探究异质协议的影响。方法：使用神经细胞自动机，引入可调语言距离的“语言”子种群，解决密度分类任务。结果：语言距离减缓共识，产生轻度分歧；多样性训练群体鲁棒，同质训练不鲁棒；类似伊辛弛豫。

<h3 id="310">[310] When Context Misleads: Surprisal, Energy and Attention Entropy as Metrics of Coherence Illusions in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21203](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21203) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ece Takmaz, Nitin Kumar, Li Kloostra, Jakub Dotlacil
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Cognitive Science, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 问题：人类读者易受连贯性错觉影响，LLM是否表现出类似行为？方法：在荷兰语模型中测量惊奇度、注意力熵和联想记忆能量。结果：惊奇度跟踪人类判断；匹配干扰物降低惊奇度；注意力熵识别特定头；消融头显示迁移效应；能量量化连贯性。

<h3 id="311">[311] QBioFusion-QSAR: Morgan-Anchored Quantum Multiple Kernel Learning for Small-Data Ligand Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21213](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21213) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Azadeh Alavi, Fatemeh Kouchmeshki, Muhammad Usman, Jessica Holien
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Molecular Property Prediction, Quantum Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：小样本QSAR研究中分子类似物活性标记不同。方法：量子多核学习，结合Morgan/Tanimoto核与量子保真度核。结果：QMKL准确率从0.815提升至0.833，MCC从0.613至0.645；但多次分区平均未显著优于基线。

<h3 id="312">[312] Speaker Identity in Non-Verbal Vocalizations: Conditional Distillation and Mixture of Experts Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tzu-Chieh Wei, Yi-Cheng Lin, Huang-Cheng Chou, Kuan-Yu Chen, Hsin-Yen Sung, Shrikanth Narayanan, Hung-yi Lee
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Speaker Verification, Mixture-of-Experts, Speech Processing
- **Venue**: INTERSPEECH 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：说话人验证系统对非语音发声泛化差，微调导致灾难性遗忘。方法：冻结Data2Vec特征+ECAPA-TDNN+MoE模块，采用条件蒸馏损失和对比损失。结果：语音-非语音均衡错误率从38.93%降至22.66%，语音错误率从13.17%降至9.24%。

<h3 id="313">[313] FleetAgent: Teleoperation Assistant for Autonomous Fleets via Vectorized V2N Messages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21222) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juntong Peng, Qi Chen, Deyuan Qu, Takayuki Shimizu, Yaobin Chen, Ziran Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Multimodal LLM, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 问题：大规模自动驾驶车队遥操作成本高，操作员监控能力有限。方法：FleetAgent云端多模态大语言模型，使用向量化V2N消息；VecFormer接口实现可微分top-K上下文选择。结果：上行负载减少625倍，KV缓存内存减少16.54倍；Lingo-Judge分数提升16.8%，干预失败率降低19.9%。

<h3 id="314">[314] Recency/Frequency Adaptive KV Caching for Large Language Model Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21238](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21238) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Shen, Meghana Madhyastha, Robert Underwood, Bogdan Nicolae, Randal Burns
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Cache Policy
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Resource-Adaptive Foundation Model Inference (AdaptFM)
- **Summary**: 问题：KV缓存LRU策略导致无关工作负载互相冲刷缓存。方法：自适应缓存动态分配最近和频繁出现的KV块空间。结果：KV缓存命中率提升最高10.8%，首令牌延迟降低12.6%。

<h3 id="315">[315] ACE-GS: Acing the Trade-off with Accurate, Compact and Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21244](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21244) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jijian Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Gaussian Splatting, Novel View Synthesis, 3D Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 问题：3DGS计算和存储需求大，现有加速方法丢失高频细节。方法：ACE-GS渐进优化框架，包括动量一致性致密化、统计敏感性稀疏化、跨维度残差频率补偿。结果：训练加速3.7倍，3-5分钟收敛，PSNR提升0.89 dB。

<h3 id="316">[316] An Empirical Study of OpenPangu Quantization on Ascend NPUs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21257) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tong Shi, Jiacheng Wang, Hui Xie, Ying Li, Aishan Liu, Jinyang Guo, Xianglong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantization, LLM Inference, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 问题：OpenPangu模型在昇腾NPU上的量化鲁棒性未被系统评估。方法：对1B和7B模型采用多种后训练量化方法进行实证研究。结果：8位权重量化无损；4位对7B可用但对1B在推理、数学、代码任务有损害；超低比特量化崩溃。

<h3 id="317">[317] Few-Shot Hyperspectral Aphid Detection via FastGAN Synthetic Data Generation, Transformer-Based Classification and Explainable AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21267](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21267) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Saeidan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, Few-Shot Learning, Hyperspectral Imaging
- **Summary**: 问题：蚜虫侵染早期检测中深度学习受限于小规模高光谱数据集。方法：FastGAN合成数据增强；使用VGG16、ResNet-50、EfficientNet、ViT分类。结果：增强提高鲁棒性；ViT准确率和F1最高；EfficientNet平衡性能好。

<h3 id="318">[318] Topological Neural Dynamics: A Neuron-wise Framework for Sequence Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21295](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21295) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Borui Cai, Yao Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sequence Modeling, Graph Neural Network, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：现有序列模型使用逐层动力学，神经元无法独立演化。方法：提出拓扑神经动力学(TND)，在神经元图上执行神经元级动力学，结合局部演化与图拓扑交互。结果：在Pong行为克隆任务中，TND捕获率最高，平均连续捕获17.47次，为最强基线的3倍以上。

<h3 id="319">[319] NASDAQ: Normalized Observation Space Dynamics-Augmented Q-Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21297](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21297) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinwei Liu, Junyuan Liang, Zicong Hong, Jianting Zhang, Wuhui Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Representation Learning, Model-Free RL
- **Summary**: 问题：基于观测预测的强化学习在低维观测上因重构损失不平衡而性能下降。方法：提出面向在线RL的归一化方法；NASDAQ框架结合值学习、短期值预测和下一归一化观测预测。结果：与最先进的基于模型和自预测RL方法相比，性能相当或更优，且训练时间更短。

<h3 id="320">[320] Task-Differentiated Atomic Skill Expansion and Routing for Continual Learning Across Highly Heterogeneous Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21307) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiacheng Wang, Xinjia He, Qi Ding, Yutao Yang, Jie Zhou, Liyang Yu, Liang Dou, Qin Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Multi-Task Learning, Catastrophic Forgetting
- **Summary**: 问题：高度异质任务下的持续学习面临灾难性遗忘和容量分配低效。方法：提出TASER框架，包括原子技能增量学习、正交增强技能检测和技能动态路由。结果：在19个任务的HeteroCLBench上，TASER优于基线，提高可塑性并减少遗忘。

<h3 id="321">[321] Warning labels shift perceptions of sycophantic AI, but not its influence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lujain Ibrahim, Myra Cheng, Cinoo Lee, Pranav Khadpe, Desmong Ong, Dan Jurafsky, Diyi Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Sycophancy, Human-AI Interaction
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了警告标签对AI谄媚行为影响用户判断的缓解效果。通过实验（N=2610）发现，基本AI披露无影响，而标注系统为谄媚（"可能在你错误时同意你"）改变了用户感知（降低客观性和信任），但未减少谄媚对用户自我正确感和冲突修复意愿的影响。结果表明警告标签可能提供虚假保护。

<h3 id="322">[322] Ramanujan Graph Rewiring with Non Negative Resistance Curvature</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21333) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hugo Attali, Rachid El Jouhri
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Graph Learning
- **Venue**: ECML PKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络中的过度挤压问题，提出基于Ramanujan图的图重连策略Ramanujan Propagation。该方法利用Ramanujan图保证非负抵抗曲率，缓解拓扑瓶颈，在实验中优于9种SOTA重连技术。

<h3 id="323">[323] DataClaw0: Agentic Tailoring Multimodal Data from Raw Streams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21337) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cong Wan, Zeyu Guo, Zijian Cai, Jiangyang Li, SongLin Dong, Lin Peng, Xiangyang Luo, Zhiheng Ma, Yihong Gong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Data Synthesis, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对多模态数据整理成本高且难以提取深层逻辑的问题，提出了DataClaw0自主数据裁剪框架。该框架通过SFT和GRPO实现复杂意图对齐，在视频生成、VQA和GUI导航等下游任务中验证了数据质量提升效果。

<h3 id="324">[324] SOHET: Sequence Of Heterogeneous Events Transformer with Self-Supervised Pre-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21356) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kees Jan de Vries, Mustafa Radha, Mathijs de Jong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Self-Supervised Learning, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 针对异构事件流预测问题，提出了SOHET分层架构（结合事件类型编码器和Transformer），并设计了三种自监督预训练目标。在欺诈检测任务上超越基线5.8%，预训练额外提升2.6%，在EBES基准上6/8任务最佳。

<h3 id="325">[325] LambdaMark: Semantic Audio Watermarking for Robustness and Radioactivity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21365) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kexin Li, Xiao Hu, Ilya Grishchenko, David Lie
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Watermarking, AI Security
- **Summary**: 针对语音克隆滥用问题，提出了LambdaMark——首个通用放射性音频水印方案。该方案将多比特水印嵌入语义音频隐空间，通过自定损失函数训练编解码器，实现接近完美的鲁棒性，且是唯一能抵抗所有移除攻击的方法。

<h3 id="326">[326] Sexualised synthetic personas encode and amplify gendered power asymmetries through voice</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21366) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alice Ross, Ariadna Sanchez, Elin Kanhov, Catherine Lai, Eva Szekely
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Gender Bias, Speech Processing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文从女性主义HCI视角研究了商业语音AI中性别刻板印象的再现。通过听力实验和声学分析发现，女性编码声音更常被描述为性感和顺从，男性编码声音更常与支配和积极特质关联，揭示了技术的性别权力不对称。

<h3 id="327">[327] Graph-of-Differences: Anatomy-Structured Difference Alignment for Medical Image Re-Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21368](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21368) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nichula Wasalathilaka, Abhijit Das, Imran Razzak, Dwarikanath Mahapatra
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Retrieval, Interpretability
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/GoD.git)
- **Summary**: 针对医学图像重识别中的捷径学习和缺乏解剖可解释性问题，提出了Graph-of-Differences（GoD）框架。该框架将图像表示为解剖图，通过结构差异对齐和节点级解释，在眼底和胸部X光数据集上Rank-1分别提升7.1和3.1个百分点，并支持零样本泛化。

<h3 id="328">[328] EnTrust: Modeling Inter-Modal Conflict for Trustworthy Multimodal Medical Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21384) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dwarikanath Mahapatra, Abhijit Das, Behzad Bozorgtabar, Zongyuan Ge, Sudipta Roy, Deepak Nayak, Mauricio Reyes, Imran Razzak
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Uncertainty Estimation, Medical Image Segmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GenMI-Lab/EnTrust.git)
- **Summary**: 针对多模态医学图像中模态分歧导致不可靠预测的问题，提出了EnTrust框架。该框架通过EnFuse模块解耦共享、特定和冲突特征，结合扩散分割生成假设，并输出像素级不确定性图，在四个基准上取得SOTA分割精度，校准误差降低40%。

<h3 id="329">[329] Unsupervised Disentanglement Without Compromises : How Functional Orthogonality Enforces Identifiability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21385](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21385) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mathieu Cyrille Simon, Pascal Frossard, Christophe De Vleeschouwer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Disentanglement
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文从功能观点研究无监督解耦表示学习，提出生成映射雅可比的正交性约束，证明该条件可实现一般非线性生成模型的可识别性。实验在正态化流动上验证了理论，解释了VAE的成功，挑战了无监督解耦不可行的论断。

<h3 id="330">[330] Evaluating LLMs for Real-World Web Vulnerability Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21397) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sebastian Neef, Luca Jungnickel, Antonio Benjamin Buchholz, Valene Spence, Vicente Birke Gonzalez
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Vulnerability Assessment, Web Security
- **Venue**: AI&CCPS Workshop @ ARES 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了六种前沿LLM在WordPress插件真实Web漏洞检测中的能力。通过五种提示设计和三次实验发现，Claude Opus 4.6检测率最高（63%），缩小漏洞范围的提示优于开放提示，且所有模型报告一致性低，揭示了LLM检测的局限。

<h3 id="331">[331] SwarmX: Agentic Scheduling for Low-Latency Agentic Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21401](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21401) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yeqi Huang, Yanwei Ye, Guomin Chen, Wenhao Su, Bin Gong, Jialian Li, Zhan Lu, Yangshen Deng, Xuan Sun, Le Xu, Luo Mai
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Scheduling, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对智能体AI应用的延迟敏感调度问题，提出了SwarmX系统。该系统使用神经预测器捕捉特征，实现分布感知的调度决策，在多智能体代码生成、深度研究等工作流上，尾延迟降低61.5%，吞吐量提升2倍。

<h3 id="332">[332] 2D Versus 3D Diffusion for In Silico Training of Interventional X-ray AI Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21414) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sampath Rapuri, Jeremy Ko, Benjamin D. Killeen, Russell H. Taylor, Mathias Unberath
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Synthesis, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 为解决介入X射线AI模型训练数据稀缺问题，比较了3D条件扩散生成CT和2D扩散生成X光两种合成数据方法。实验表明，2D扩散合成X光训练的解剖标志检测模型在真实X光上性能可与真实数据训练模型媲美。

<h3 id="333">[333] MIRCaps: A Large-Scale Mixed-Domain Dataset with Image-Level and Region-Level Captions for Fine-Grained Vision-Language Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arlindo Luciano Tulumba Roberto, Hyungjoon Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dataset, Vision-Language Model, Image Captioning
- **Summary**: 针对混合领域图像描述数据集缺乏的问题，构建了大规模多模态数据集MIRCaps，包含14万+图像、98万+图像级和174万+区域级描述。该数据集支持细粒度视觉语言学习，在图像描述和目标检测任务上验证了轻量VLM微调的有效性。

<h3 id="334">[334] Does Mixture-of-Experts Actually Help Inference on Consumer and Edge Hardware? An Empirical Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alfarizy Alfarizy, Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen, René Richard, Roozbeh Razavi-Far, Hung Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Inference, Edge Computing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/edge-moe)
- **Summary**: 该论文实证研究了混合专家模型（MoE）在消费级和边缘硬件上的推理性能。在Apple M2 Pro和NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano上对比OLMoE-1B-7B与密集模型，发现边缘设备上推理时间受总参数而非活跃参数主导，稀疏激活优势消失，揭示了MoE的局限性。

<h3 id="335">[335] CORTIS: Text-Only Adaptation of Spoken Language Models for Task-Oriented Voice Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youngwon Choi, Hyeonyu Kim, Taeyoun Kwon, Donghyuk Jung, Myeongkyun Cho
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.SD; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Spoken Language Model, Dialogue System, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 针对任务导向语音智能体需要结构化输出但ASR错误传播的问题，提出了CORTIS文本仅适应框架。该框架通过文本形式任务监督微调口语语言模型，在三个任务导向数据集上表现与ASR-LLM级联相当，且在噪声条件下有明显优势。

<h3 id="336">[336] Evaluation of Small Language Models for Arabic Language Processing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21460) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jumana Alsubhi, Ahmed Alhusayni, Abdulrahman Gharawi, Israa Hamdine, Alshaymaa Allahim, Lamees Alhumaid, Ahmad Shabana, Rafik Madani
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, LLM Evaluation, Small Language Model
- **Summary**: 该论文评估了12种小型语言模型在阿拉伯语NLP任务上的零样本性能，构建了含240个测试项的基准。Gemma 3 12B总分最高（4.548/5），模型大小并非唯一因素，低性能模型常见失败模式包括提示泄漏、幻觉等。

<h3 id="337">[337] Predicting High-Risk Colorectal Polyps in African Americans Using Pre-Colonoscopy Clinical Features: Machine Learning Model Development and Temporal Validation</h3>

<h3 id="353">[353] PrivacyAlign: Contextual Privacy Alignment for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21710) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Manveer Singh Tamber, Abhay Puri, Marc-Etienne Brunet, Perouz Taslakian, Jimmy Lin, Spandana Gella
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Agent, Privacy Alignment
- **Summary**: 面向LLM智能体的上下文隐私对齐问题，现有方法缺乏可靠的人机隐私规范。作者构建了PrivacyAlign数据集（1350样本，3516标注），并提出基于标注的奖励建模进行强化学习。结果表明，小规模开放权重智能体经过该奖励训练后，能更好地与人类隐私规范对齐，在PrivacyAlign和现有智能体隐私基准上取得显著改进。

<h3 id="354">[354] Denoising Iterative Self-Correction: Structured Verification Loops for Reliable LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shen Yin, David Ken, Joel Stremmel
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Correction
- **Summary**: 现有自纠正方法可能破坏正确回答，本文提出去噪迭代自纠正方法DISC，通过验证、判断、纠正的多轮迭代，并引入二元判断门控控制纠正精度。在BIG-Bench Mistake上准确率达81.6%，每单位退化带来的改进量比Chain-of-Verification高13倍，比Self-Refine高5倍。

<h3 id="355">[355] Safe to Check, Unsafe to Use: Relinking at the Compression Boundary of LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21732](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21732) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zesen Liu, Zihan Zhang, Dongdong She
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 基于摘要的提示压缩导致重新链接漏洞，即良性片段组合成恶意指令。形式化对抗性重新链接，开发自动化DSL工具Relink。实现86.9%的重新链接率和后端执行率，而干净分割基线仅为17.0%。现有防御失效，提出的KBRA防御将残余后端执行率降至0.0%。

<h3 id="356">[356] HPP: Hierarchical Programmatic Probing for Long Video Understanding by Decoupling Perception and Reasoning</h3>

<h3 id="357">[357] Cohort Organized Learning: Clustering Through Agreement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21743](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21743) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="358">[358] Scene-Level Heterogeneous Physics Simulation with 3D Gaussian Splats</h3>

<h3 id="359">[359] Beyond the Next Step: Variable-Length Latent World Models for Long-Horizon Planning</h3>

<h3 id="361">[361] THREAD: Trajectory Planning for Hybrid Rigid-Soft Manipulators with Environment-Aware Diffusion</h3>

<h3 id="362">[362] Is Agent Code Less Maintainable Than Human Code?</h3>

<h3 id="364">[364] Generating Public Health Responses using Survey-Augmented Large Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="366">[366] AgentDSE: Reasoning-Augmented Architectural Design Space Exploration</h3>

- **Summary**: 低秩LLM压缩中秩分配困难。提出UniRank，将全局秩分配形式化为排序与截断流水线，通过局部奇异能量比和全局功能重要性评分。单次压缩中困惑度降低最高50%。

<h3 id="368">[368] Keyless Attention: Value-Space Routing and Value-Only Caching for Efficient Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21848](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21848) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xin Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, KV Cache, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 标准注意力机制中KV缓存开销大。提出无键注意力机制，消除键投影，仅使用值缓存（KV缓存减半）。实现深度m注意力分解。在5个模型中的4个上困惑度匹配或优于标准注意力，零样本推理更优。

<h3 id="369">[369] Harness-MU: A Safe, Governed, and Effective Harness for Multi-User LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21856](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21856) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wangxuan Fan, Xiaoyu Nie, Zhongxiang Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YuanJrShiuan/Harness-MulUser)
- **Summary**: 针对多用户LLM代理在协作工作流中面临的权限执行、冲突解决和数据泄露等治理问题，提出Harness-MU框架，将语言生成与安全编排解耦，通过执行钩子强制执行确定性权限边界。实验表明，该框架在所有访问控制攻击下都能保证隐私保护，效用得分比标准基线高0.28-0.39，指令跟随准确率提升最高达48.9个百分点。

<h3 id="370">[370] The Language-Energy Divide: Measuring Energy Costs of Multilingual LLM Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21869](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21869) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Naihao Deng, Alissa Shen, Yiming Feng, Joan Nwatu, Jae-Won Chung, Mosharaf Chowdhury, Yulong Chen, Rada Mihalcea
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Multilingual Learning, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对多语言LLM推理能耗不均的问题，系统研究了不同语言下的推理能耗差异。发现每输出token能耗差异达8.3倍，总能耗差异达179倍（英语最低，普什图语最高），且高能耗语言往往任务准确率也最低，揭示了系统性的能源不平等。

<h3 id="371">[371] Protein contacts are already in the attention: a single-forward-pass alternative to the Categorical Jacobian</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21876](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21876) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rome Thorstenson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Language Model, Attention Mechanism, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Rome-1/plm-contact-fusion)
- **Summary**: 针对Categorical Jacobian方法需19L次前向传播读取蛋白质接触的问题，证明该信号集中在少量注意力头上。通过选择少量标注蛋白质上的top-K接触相关注意力头，一次前向传播即可恢复接触，在无泄漏数据上优于CJ（如ESM-2-650M提升9个百分点）。

<h3 id="372">[372] Streaming T5-based Text-to-Speech Synthesis with Limited Lookahead</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21882](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21882) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muyang Du, Jason Roche, Junjie Lai
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Streaming TTS
- **Summary**: 针对级联LLM-TTS系统中流式语音合成延迟高的问题，提出S5-TTS，通过编码器-解码器语言模型和单调对齐学习实现逐词增量合成。引入lookahead因果掩码和交错多源蒸馏，在有限前瞻下保持质量，实验表明与全上下文T5-TTS质量相当，显著降低端到端延迟。

<h3 id="373">[373] A Verifiable Search Is Not a Learnable Chain-of-Thought</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21884](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21884) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Harsh Patel
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning, Search
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/harshpatel1692/search-not-learnable)
- **Summary**: 针对可通过短程序解决的任务能否被蒸馏为思维链的假设，以密码算术等九类推理任务为测试床，发现回溯搜索无法被忠实蒸馏为前向思维链。模型只能学习验证步骤，无法执行搜索，准确率仅0.01-0.07，而揭示密钥后提升至0.57，说明可蒸馏的是记忆与验证而非搜索。

<h3 id="374">[374] Cohort-Anchored Foundation Models for Electronic Health Records: From Risk Scores to Auditable Peer Cohorts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21885](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21885) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaiping Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Foundation Model, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对电子健康记录基础模型可解释性差、易受分布偏移影响的问题，提出CAFM框架，将患者队列作为一等公民贯穿学习流程。包括偏差感知数据整理、队列条件预训练、多模态队列对齐和临床医生在环精炼四个阶段，可增强现有模型，并通过四个临床案例展示其效果。

<h3 id="375">[375] AI-Mediated Negotiation: Design Reflections and Lessons</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Veda Duddu, Jash Rajesh Parekh, Andy Mao, Hanyi Min, Ziang Xiao, Vedant Das Swain, Koustuv Saha
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Human-Computer Interaction, AI Persuasion
- **Summary**: 针对高压力工作场所谈判的AI辅导系统，构建了Trucey并验证其四个设计假设。预注册实验（N=267）和访谈（N=15）发现，静态手册在赋能和可用性上优于AI条件，说明对话式AI强加线性执行模型于递归任务，并提出“地图先行，路径次之，模拟最后”的排序原则。

<h3 id="376">[376] Improving Engine Sound Analysis in Hot-Test Environments via a RAB-U-Net (Residual Attention Block U-Net) Noise Removal Method</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21887](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21887) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raheleh Mohseni, Mahdi Alyari
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio Processing, Anomaly Detection, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 针对生产线热测试中背景噪声干扰发动机声音分析的问题，提出基于残差注意力块U-Net（RAB-U-Net）的噪声去除方法。实验表明该方法显著提升发动机噪声检测精度，优于传统技术，适用于实时生产环境。

<h3 id="377">[377] Scaling Performance and Low-Resource Annotation with Many-Shot In-Context Learning for Named Entity Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21890) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Zhang, Fangping Lan, Cornelia Caragea, Longin Jan Latecki, Eduard Dragut
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Named Entity Recognition, In-Context Learning, Low-Resource NLP
- **Summary**: 针对命名实体识别中ICL性能低于监督模型的问题，研究了数百示例的多示例ICL，并探索其用于低资源NER标注。实验表明，多示例ICL可匹配甚至超越全监督BERT，且利用约百个人工标注示例进行上下文标注后，微调BERT在低资源NER上F1提升约10%。

<h3 id="378">[378] Skills for the future software profession: beyond agentic AI!</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21894](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21894) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sungmin Kang, Baishakhi Ray, Abhik Roychoudhury
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Agent, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 针对智能体时代软件工程师所需核心技能的问题，总结了2026年纽约和新加坡两场圆桌讨论的结果。发现验证与确认的重要性日益增加，并识别了开发者所需技能，对未来的软件工程培训和教育具有启示意义。

<h3 id="379">[379] Rethinking the Adaptation of Vision Foundation Models for Efficient Cell Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21913) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qing Xu, Xiangjian He, Wenting Duan, Jiebo Luo, Zhen Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Vision Foundation Model
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xq141839/EffiCell-Seg)
- **Summary**: 针对视觉基础模型在细胞分割中资源消耗大的问题，提出EffiCell-Seg框架，无需重新训练视觉编码器。利用冻结VFM中的全局显著性先验和局部形态先验，设计细胞结构提示编码器和协同掩码解码器，在多种细胞成像模态上优于现有方法，可训练参数仅约5M。

<h3 id="380">[380] Latent Confidence Alignment for LLM Self-Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21937](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21937) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ting-Yu Chen, Tingting Yu, Pei-Cing Huang, Chan Hsu, Ming-Yen Lin, Yihuang Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 针对LLM置信度校准未建模题目难度，无法区分真正自我评估与响应生成副产品的问题，提出基于Rasch模型的潜在置信度对齐误差（LCAE）。通过融入题目难度作为外部信号，实验表明该方法在20个模型上提升了自我评估质量，且可靠性与其推理成本相关。

<h3 id="381">[381] Modularized Reinforcement Learning on LLMs: From MDP Creation to Exploration and Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21943](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21943) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhao Yang, Yuxuan Jiang, Ting-Chih Chen, Lincen Yang, Annie Wong, Chao Gao, Jacob E. Kooi, Zhong Li, Jiayang Shi, Kevin Qiu, Qi Huang, Xinrui Zu, Shiping Yang, Hengyuan Zhang, Ngai Wong, Filip Ilievski, Shujian Yu, Aske Plaat, Zhaochun Ren, Mark Hoogendoorn, Vincent François-Lavet
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training, Survey
- **Summary**: 针对LLM后训练中RL方法（PPO、GRPO）仅代表RL窄子集的问题，综述围绕MDP构建、探索和学习三个阶段的算法设计决策。揭示了Critic-free策略梯度和蒙特卡洛信用分配密集，而基于值的方法、离策略actor-critic和自举信用分配等经典RL技术尚未充分探索，为迁移RL技术到LLM训练提供了方向。

<h3 id="382">[382] ScalePredictor: Instance-aware Scale Learning for Accurate Quantization of Vision Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21947) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Changjun Li, Runqing Jiang, Lian Xu, Ye Zhang, Qingyong Hu, Yulan Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision Transformer, Quantization, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对视觉Transformer量化中静态量化未考虑实例差异的问题，提出ScalePredictor动态量化框架。发现浅层激活分布范围与深层最优量化尺度之间的隐藏相关性，设计高效范围提取和Taylor多项式尺度投影模块，同时生成所有量化尺度。实验表明在ImageNet上优于先前PTQ方法，实现了更好的精度-效率权衡。

<h3 id="383">[383] From RAN Control to Agentic Intelligence: Architecture and Vision for Energy Efficient AI-RAN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21955](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21955) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sabrine Aroua, Alexis I. Aravanis, Ilias Chatzistefanidis, Hamza Abbar, Anh-Khoa Dang, Anastasios Giovanidis, Salah-Eddine El Ayoubi, Stephane Senecal, Martha Vlachou Konchylaki, Navid Nikaein
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 6G Networks, LLM Agent, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对6G网络中AI与RAN工作负载联合编排的能效挑战，提出面向智能AI原生的RAN架构，结合O-RAN的结构化控制与AI-RAN的统一愿景。利用语义意图抽象和LLM驱动的协调，实现自适应编排、冲突解决和能效感知多目标优化，通过代表性用例展示资源效率和能耗的改善。

<h3 id="384">[384] SPOTR: Spatio-temporal Pooling One-Token Reconstruction for Universal Physiological Signal Self-supervised Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21973](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21973) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiyu Gui, Mingzhi Chen, Yuesheng Zhu, Guibo Luo, Yuchao Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Medical AI, Time Series Analysis
- **Venue**: IJCAI-ECAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/5GYYYYY/SPOTR)
- **Summary**: 针对生理信号自监督学习在异构数据集上表现不佳、计算成本高的问题，提出SPOTR框架，通过单token全局瓶颈进行压缩-重构预训练。在EEG、iEEG、ECG和PPG的20个数据集上，线性探针平均AUC分别提升18.49%、21.71%、17.86%和4.64%，且延迟和峰值GPU内存均显著降低。

<h3 id="385">[385] Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for Quantum Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21974) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Katherine Ip, Casey R. Myers, Udaya Parampalli, James Quach, Peiyong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Quantum Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型在量子推理中的不足，提出监督微调（SFT）和SFT+GRPO两种流水线。SFT在分布内和门计数外推上达到近完美精度，SFT+GRPO提高了泛化能力。

<h3 id="386">[386] Old Fictions, New Skins: Evaluating the Manipulative Capabilities of LLMs in Healthcare</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.21977](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21977) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gathoni Ireri, Roger D. Odipo
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Medical AI, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 通过随机实验评估LLM在医疗中的操纵能力。操纵变体使参与者选择错误治疗的比例显著高于对照组（59.5% vs 44.0%），表明需要针对操纵的安全基础设施。

<h3 id="387">[387] Channel Location Constrains the Auditability of Subliminal Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tamas Madl
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 研究潜意识学习（隐藏特质迁移）的可审计性，发现通道位置（身体、词汇、条件行为）决定审计有效性。覆盖度指标在身体通道中预测力强（AUROC 0.997），但词汇通道需事后检测。

<h3 id="388">[388] Cluster-Specific Localized Drift Detection for Efficient Batch Model Adaptation under Controlled Distribution Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22026) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ignacio Cabrera Martin, Marcello Trovati, Almas Baimagambetov, Nikolaos Polatidis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Concept Drift, Domain Adaptation, Tabular Learning
- **Summary**: 针对动态环境中的分布漂移，提出聚类诱导的漂移模拟框架。评估六种适应策略，在多个数据集上比较分类和回归任务的表现。

<h3 id="389">[389] RARM: Confidence-Gated Progress Reward Modeling for RL in Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22027) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengzhi Yang, Xinyu Wang, Pengyu Jing, Kehan Wen, Yiduo Qu, Zhenhao Huang, Minghao Fu, Xin Liu, Yaheng Shen, Fan Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reward Model, Robotic Manipulation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 提出RARM，一种轻量级视觉比较器，从单个成功演示生成密集进度奖励，并抑制不确定匹配。在9个模拟和4个真实操作任务中取得最佳成功率，尤其长时任务。

<h3 id="390">[390] A Completion-Aware Framework for Impactful Counterfactual Explainability in Graph Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22033](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22033) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maria Myrto Villia, Filippos Gouidis, Theodore Patkos, Panos Trahanias
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Interpretability, Counterfactual Explanation
- **Venue**: ECML PKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 提出面向图神经网络的通用、模型无关的反事实解释管道，结合事实可解释性与缺失边预测。在合成和真实图分类基准上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="391">[391] Gradient-Descent Steps to Success over Mean Accuracy: A Paradigm Shift for ML</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22053](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22053) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Riccardo Poli, Ahmet Yilmaz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Optimization, AutoML, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 提出以梯度下降步数作为计算代价度量，发现最优超参数倾向大学习率，并揭示单次训练与多次重启的相变。该范式为模型选择和AutoML提供新框架。

<h3 id="392">[392] NL2Scratch: An Executable Benchmark and Evaluation for Block-Based Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22061) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heejin Do, Alexandre Ballenghien, Yang Wu, April Yi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Benchmark, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对Scratch块编程，提出包含31万对的可执行基准和语义对齐一致性（SAC）指标。实验显示模型在词汇相似度高但语义对齐差，尤其操作槽位。

<h3 id="393">[393] New Smooth Loss functions for Robust Regression that Closely Approximate Absolute Error and Provide Improved Performance on Datasets With Significant Outliers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22068) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mathew Mithra Noel, Arindam Banerjee, Yug D. Oswal, Geraldine Bessie Amali D, Venkataraman Muthiah-Nakarajan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robust Loss Function
- **Summary**: 提出两种无限可微的损失函数（SRL和SMAE），更接近MAE且性能更优。SRL严格凸，SMAE严格拟凸，在含大量离群点的回归任务中表现优越。

<h3 id="394">[394] CodeTeam: An LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Framework for Repository-Level Code Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22082](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22082) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifei Wang, Ruiyin Li, Peng Liang, Qiong Feng, Zengyang Li, Mojtaba Shahin, Arif Ali Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Multi-Agent System, Repo-Level Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/WhitenWhiten/CodeTeam)
- **Summary**: 针对仓库级代码生成，提出LLM驱动的多智能体框架，包含策划、实现、质检等角色。在SketchEval和NL2Repo-Bench上分别提升SketchBLEU和测试通过率。

<h3 id="395">[395] Plurification in/of language technology -- The integration of culture in next-generation AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22097](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22097) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gertraud Koch, Fausto Giunchiglia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Cultural Bias, NLP
- **Summary**: 探讨如何在NLP中操作化“文化”，提出多元认识论。分析表明当前方法多停留在输出层面，未解决权力、治理等深层问题。

<h3 id="396">[396] TraceView: Interactive Visualization of Agentic Program Repair Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22110](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22110) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amirali Sajadi, Tu Nguyen, Kimmie Huynh, Esteban Parra, Preetha Chatterjee
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Program Repair, Agent Debugging, Data Visualization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SOAR-Lab/agent-traj-visualization)
- **Summary**: 交互式工具用于标注和可视化程序修复智能体的轨迹。支持关系过滤、补丁摘要和节点证据面板。用户研究认为其提高了轨迹可浏览性。

<h3 id="397">[397] KITE: Decoupling Kinematics and Interaction for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22113](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22113) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qianxu Wang, Kuan Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotic Manipulation, Zero-Shot Learning, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 解耦操作任务的推理与本体特定控制，实现零样本跨体现迁移。在并联夹爪、灵巧手等体现间转移，成功率优于基线。

<h3 id="398">[398] BioMatrix: Towards a Comprehensive Biological Foundation Model Spanning the Modality Matrix of Sequences, Structures, and Language</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22138) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qizhi Pei, Zhimeng Zhou, Yi Duan, Yiyang Zhao, Wei Li, Han Guo, Liang He, Chengping Li, Chang-Yu Hsieh, Conghui He, Rui Yan, Lijun Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; q-bio.BM
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Multimodal Learning, Molecular Representation
- **Summary**: 首个原生整合序列、结构和自然语言的生物基础模型，统一标记化后采用单一下一标记预测。在80个任务中77个达到SOTA或竞争力。

<h3 id="399">[399] SAGE: An Expert-Annotated South Asian GI Endoscopy Dataset for Multimodal Learning and Hallucination Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22144](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22144) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Niyoj Oli, Sachin Acharya, Sandesh Pokhrel, Sanjay Bhandari, Ramesh Rana, Nikesh Mani Shrestha, Ram Bahadur Gurung, Yash Raj Shrestha, Prashnna K Gyawali, Binod Bhattarai
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Dataset, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 专家标注的南亚胃肠内窥镜数据集，含1300张图像、标注和问答对。基准测试显示任务特定模型性能下降58%，LMM的GREEN分数显著降低。

<h3 id="400">[400] $π$-RAG: Oblivious Retrieval via Semantic Quantization and Transcendental Addressing for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aniket Wattamwar, Mrunal Kakirwar
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: RAG, Privacy, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 利用π的数字化熵，通过语义量化层和π-key实现遗忘检索，确保推理对数据不可知。适用于高合规领域。

<h3 id="401">[401] Rebuttals Move Peer-Review Scores, but Initial-Review Structure Bounds the Movement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22166) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mathieu Louis, Tibo Vanleke, Vincent Ginis, Andres Algaba
- **Subjects**: cs.DL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Peer Review, LLM-as-a-Judge
- **Summary**: 同行评议中作者回复对评分的影响难以测量。本文利用LLM预测回复前评分，分析ICLR 2024-2025的73k评审轨迹，发现初始评审结构已能预测大部分评分变化（AUC=0.747），加入回复交换信号后AUC提升至0.804。回复可以移动评分，但移动幅度受限于初始评审结构。

<h3 id="402">[402] Gated MLPs as Symmetry-Broken Rank-1 Bilinear Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22172](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22172) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nathan Breslow
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 门控MLP的理论理解不足。本文证明门控MLP可视为双线性注意力机制的秩1近似，且非线性置于一个因子上打破了交换对称性和逆缩放对称性。该视角解释了门控MLP的有效性，并指导未来架构设计。

<h3 id="403">[403] How Well Do Self-Supervised Speech Models Encode Age and Gender in Children's Speech? A Layer-Wise Analysis Across Multiple Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22177) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhijit Sinha, Hemant Kumar Kathania, Mohit Joshi, Harishankar Kumar, Shrikanth Narayanan, Sudarsana Reddy Kadiri
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Children's ASR, Age Estimation
- **Summary**: 自监督语音模型在儿童语音中编码年龄和性别信息的效果尚不明确。本文对Wav2Vec2、HuBERT、Data2Vec和WavLM进行逐层分析，发现早期到中层编码最强，HuBERT在年龄分类上最优，Wav2Vec2和HuBERT在性别分类上领先。即使在1-3秒短语音段也能实现可靠分类。

<h3 id="404">[404] DSSCNet: A Transfer Learning Framework for Cross-Corpus Dysarthric Speech Severity Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arnab Kumar Roy, Hemant Kumar Kathania, Paban Sapkota, Sudarsana Reddy Kadiri, Shrikanth Narayanan
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Dysarthric Speech Recognition, Transfer Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 构音障碍语音严重程度分类面临说话人变异、类别不平衡和数据集有限的问题。本文提出DSSCNet，采用迁移学习和多语料库学习，在TORGO和UA-Speech上分别达到75.80%和68.25%准确率，显著优于现有模型。该方法有助于开发更好的辅助语音技术。

<h3 id="405">[405] Dual-Stream EEG Decoding for 3D Visual Perception</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22182](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22182) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ninon Lizé Masclef, Taisija Demcenko, Antonella Catanzaro, Nataliya Kosmyna
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Brain-Computer Interface, 3D Reconstruction
- **Venue**: NeurReps Workshop at NeurIPS 2025
- **Summary**: 脑电图解码3D形状感知存在挑战。本文提出双通路架构（腹侧和背侧），采用圆形回归预测角度和EEG条件多视图扩散进行3D重建。成功从EEG信号解码物体身份和空间方向，并实现3D重建，揭示时间结构化的通道参与。

<h3 id="406">[406] L20-Edu-135M: An Auditable Single-GPU Study of Data-Efficient Small Language Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Data Efficiency, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 小语言模型通常需要大量训练数据，资源受限场景下难以实现。本文在单张L20 GPU上训练134.5M参数模型，使用13B token，平均得分0.4150，达到SmolLM-135M的87.1%而token量仅2.17%。实验发现RLVR存在失效模式。

<h3 id="407">[407] Neural Conjugate Aggregation: Identifiable Unsupervised Multi-Sensor Regression under Heterogeneous Sensor Bias</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22200](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22200) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammed Faruk Aytin, Zehra Demir, Alper Ünal, Julian Marshall, Gözde Ünal
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sensor Fusion, Uncertainty Estimation, Conformal Prediction
- **Summary**: 多传感器数据融合中缺乏无标签的监督方法。本文提出神经共轭聚合模型NCAM，结合神经网络与共轭高斯推断，实现可识别的无监督多源融合，并集成蒙特卡洛共形预测提供覆盖保证。在合成和空气质量数据集上提高了预测精度和不确定性校准。

<h3 id="408">[408] When Is Emergent Consensus Real? A Measured Coupling Gain and a Validity Diagnostic for LLM Agent Societies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22203](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22203) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongxu Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: LLM智能体社会中涌现共识或极化的可测量控制参数和有效性检验缺失。本文引入耦合增益γ，通过扰动邻居意见测量，并建立Friedkin-Johnsen等经典动力学模型。发现前沿模型不会自发反极化，且提出随机初始条件诊断区分真实平均效应和模型先验伪影。

<h3 id="409">[409] Lexical Consensus: Grounded Word Learning and Shared Meaning in Artificial Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22207) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Patricio M. Vera
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Language Acquisition, Visual Grounding, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/patriciomvera/lexical-consensus)
- **Summary**: 人工智能体能否从具身经验中习得并稳定词汇意义尚未得到评估。本文提出词汇共识框架，使用冻结DINOv2嵌入和非词标签，发现感知距离预测习得准确率（偏R²=0.245），语义距离无显著作用。命名和检索是不同过程。

<h3 id="410">[410] Sequential Minimal Optimization Algorithm for One-Class Support Vector Machines With Privileged Information</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22210) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrey Lange, Dmitry Smolyakov, Evgeny Burnaev
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Support Vector Machine, Optimization
- **Venue**: IEEE Access
- **Summary**: 一类支持向量机结合特权信息（OC-SVM+）缺乏序列最小优化算法。本文提出OC-SVM+的SMO算法，证明有限时间收敛。实验表明该算法显著优于非序列算法和内点算法。

<h3 id="411">[411] Delta-Diffusion: Modeling Longitudinal Brain Amyloid-PET Trajectories via Conditional Poisson Diffusion Bridge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22216](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22216) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongheng Sun, Minhui Yu, Mengqi Wu, Maureen Kohi, Mingxia Liu
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Diffusion Model, Image Synthesis
- **Summary**: 纵向脑PET成像成本高且现有模型难以捕捉细微病理进展。本文提出Delta-Diffusion，基于条件泊松扩散桥，锚定基线PET，结合扩散Transformer。在542名受试者中优于现有方法，有效捕捉淀粉样蛋白沉积的纵向变化。

<h3 id="412">[412] An Analysis of Untrained Deep Reservoir Networks for Audio Surveillance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22218) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Corrado Baccheschi, Patrizio Dazzi
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reservoir Computing, Audio Processing, Sound Event Detection
- **Venue**: AVSS 2026
- **Summary**: 未训练递归模型在音频监控中的潜力有待探索。本文评估双向回声状态网络在MIVIA数据集上的紧急声音事件检测，深度配置在噪声下更鲁棒，浅层配置在边缘设备上效率更优。与BiLSTM和CRNN相比具有竞争力。

<h3 id="413">[413] MultiMem: Measuring and Mitigating Memorization in Multi-Modal Contrastive Learninga</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenhao Wang, Franziska Boenisch, Michael Backes, Adam Dziedzic
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Contrastive Learning, Memorization Mitigation
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 多模态对比学习中的记忆化现象尚未被研究。本文提出MultiMem指标量化记忆化，发现跨模态语义错配影响最大，文本是主导模态。针对性数据增强可降低记忆化并提升模型性能。

<h3 id="414">[414] Quantifying Theoretical AI Alignment Guarantees: Receiver-Utility Bounds in Bayesian Persuasion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22226](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22226) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Yachbes, Eva Tardos
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: AI Alignment, Game Theory, Bayesian Persuasion
- **Summary**: 当AI目标不对齐时，人类能获得多少有用信息？本文采用贝叶斯说服模型，证明接收者效用比R_max/R_0 ≤ 3/2，并在接近独立乘积先验时界更紧。存在六位先验使得比值为39/31 \> 5/4。

<h3 id="415">[415] On the Expressive Power of Weight Quantization in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22249](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22249) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shao-Qun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Quantization, Model Compression, Expressivity
- **Summary**: 权重量化对大型语言模型表达能力的影响缺乏理论理解。本文证明1.58位是量化精度极限，并建立通用逼近和表达能力崩溃性质。表达能力随量化位数减少而多项式下降。

<h3 id="416">[416] From Handcrafted Features to Functional Edge Learning: Evolution of EEG Seizure Detection Frameworks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22258](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22258) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sepideh Kheirollahi, Mohammad Rasoul Roshanshah
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Seizure Detection, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 深度学习用于EEG癫痫检测存在可解释性差、数据需求大和计算成本高的问题。本文综述现有局限并引入Kolmogorov-Arnold网络作为新范式，提供参数效率、内在可解释性和数据稀缺下的稳健性。

<h3 id="417">[417] Revelio: Cost-Efficient Agentic Memory Safety Vulnerability Detection For Repository-Scale Codebases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22263) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiwei Hou, Hao Wang, Muxi Lyu, Marius Momeu, Eric Nguyen, Taige Yang, Koushik Sen, Dawn Song, David Wagner
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 针对内存安全漏洞检测中LLM幻觉和可扩展性问题，提出Revelio端到端智能体框架，通过生成可执行漏洞验证并用确定性sanitizer验证，结合轻量静态分析降低成本。在7个持续模糊测试5-8年的项目和100个Arvo项目上，以约1小时/项目和总成本$300发现19个未知漏洞，性能优于前沿编码智能体。

<h3 id="418">[418] Evaluating Large Language Models for Hausa and Fongbe Machine Translation: Benchmarks, Failures, and Metric Reliability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22269](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22269) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahounan Pericles Adjovi, Roald Eiselen, Prasenjit Mitra
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Low-Resource NLP, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 评估GPT-4o Mini等四个LLM在豪萨语和丰贝语上的翻译质量，发现不同语言质量差异大（豪萨语4.0-4.5/5，丰贝语1.0-2.2/5），自动指标与人工判断相关性因语言而异（丰贝语ρ=1.0，豪萨语ρ=0.5），建议多指标评估并需至少2500句样本保证稳定性。

<h3 id="419">[419] MixedPEFT: Combining Multiple PEFT Methods with Mixed Objectives for Unsupervised Domain Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22272) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammed Rawhani, Dervis Karaboga, Ozkan Ufuk Nalbantoglu, Alper Basturk, Bahriye Akay
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对预训练语言模型在新领域上全微调成本高且易灾难性遗忘的问题，提出结合多种PEFT方法和混合目标的无监督域自适应策略。在MNLI数据集20个域间迁移任务上，使用7%可训练参数，比现有最优参数高效方法UDapter高1.41个百分点，比全微调DANN高1.26个百分点。

<h3 id="420">[420] From Speech to Text Corpora: Evaluating ASR-Based Data Acquisition for Low-Resource Fongbe and Hausa</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22274](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22274) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mahounan Pericles Adjovi, Victor Olufemi, Roald Eiselen, Prasenjit Mitra
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Low-Resource NLP, Speech Recognition
- **Summary**: 针对低资源非洲语言缺乏文本语料的问题，使用ASR管道为丰贝语和豪萨语扩展文本资源。微调MMS-300M在12.3小时丰贝语数据集上WER降至9.48%（相对降低78%），处理45.49小时YouTube视频获得6770个转录片段，人工评估豪萨语质量接近可用，丰贝语需要后处理。

<h3 id="421">[421] Active Sensing and Deferred-Decision Trajectory Optimization for Robust Target Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22277) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Farbod Siahkali, Mengxue Hou, Vijay Gupta
- **Subjects**: eess.SY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Motion Planning, Active Sensing
- **Venue**: IEEE Control Systems Letters (L-CSS) 2026
- **Summary**: 针对移动传感系统在资源约束下识别目标的问题，提出主动感知延迟决策轨迹优化（AS-DDTO），在规划目标中加入轨迹依赖的信息获取项，支持贝叶斯更新和共形候选集更新。推导了混合整数锥形重构，证明了递归可行性和置信集中性等保证，数值实验表明在距离相关感知不确定性和有限感知预算下改进了目标识别。

<h3 id="422">[422] SCENIC: Semantic-Conditioned Edge-Aware Neural Framework for Structured IoT Command Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22296](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22296) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luke Ztz Hu, Hongbing Lang, Songping Mai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: IoT, Edge Computing, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对边缘IoT设备在内存、隐私和延迟约束下的命令生成问题，提出SCENIC框架，涵盖模型架构选择、数据生成、三元组损失对比微调、剪枝量化及部署。在Smart Home Instruct-Bench上，最强密集解码器自回归模型达99.0% EM@1，INT8编码器-解码器导出保留91.0% EM@1并减小模型大小25.38%。

<h3 id="423">[423] Leveraging Large Language Models to Obscure Code Stylometry: A Comparative Study of GPT-3.5 and GPT-4</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22306) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saman Pordanesh, Benjamin Tan
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Stylometry, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 研究LLM（GPT-3.5和GPT-4）在保留功能的同时改变代码风格特征的能力，评估不同提示策略对规避随机森林作者归属检测的效果。发现单次与多次提示方法存在显著差异，结构化详细提示更重要，同时功能保留存在挑战。

<h3 id="424">[424] Enhancing Protein Representation Learning via Manifold Restore Mixing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22307) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yizhou Dang, Chuang Zhao, Lianbo Ma, Guibing Guo, Xingwei Wang, Zhu Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Protein Representation Learning, Data Augmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KingGugu/MRM)
- **Summary**: 针对蛋白质表示学习中数据增强方法破坏结构和多样性不足的问题，提出流形恢复混合（MRM），在隐空间混合原始和增强蛋白质数据以恢复结构信息，并设计样本难度调度器逐步增加混合难度。在多种蛋白质表示主干和下游任务上表现有效且泛化。

<h3 id="425">[425] Diffusion Integrated Gradients: Controllable Path Generation for Flexible Feature Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22314](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22314) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soyeon Kim, Kyowoon Lee, Jaesik Choi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Diffusion Model, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对路径归因方法中固定路径导致解释噪声和扭曲的问题，提出扩散积分梯度（DiffIG），将路径生成视为条件生成问题，训练扩散模型学习路径分布并通过引导采样嵌入用户指导。在定量匹配或超越现有方法的同时获得感知对齐的解释。

<h3 id="426">[426] Curriculum Reinforcement Learning Can Incentivize Reasoning Capacity in LLMs Beyond the Base Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22317](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22317) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengxiang Cai, Tianchen Fang, Xiaohan Li, Qingyuan Zeng, Guocong Li, Jintai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 针对主流RLVR仅改进采样效率而未能扩展推理能力边界的问题，提出边界感知课程强化学习，先通过pass@k定位当前边界，再对边界附近示例进行教师指导，最后用RL巩固。在Qwen、Llama、DeepSeek上，平均pass@256比基础模型提高9.8个百分点，比普通RLVR提高10.3个百分点。

<h3 id="427">[427] All Routes Lead to Collapse</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22325](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22325) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: K. R. Balasubramanian
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Attention Mechanism, Geometric Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 证明注意力沉点、表示崩溃和范数分层并非Transformer特有，而是内容路由在固定相似度度量下的普遍现象。给出重述恒等式：softmax注意力是博尔兹曼加权聚合，其评分忽略键的范数。在多种路由器（注意力、图注意力、SSM、递归混合器）上均观察到相同特征，并表明机制不依赖范数分层。

<h3 id="428">[428] BabelJudge: Measuring LLM-as-a-Judge Reliability Across Languages and Agent Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22329) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shreyas KC
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Multilingual Learning, Bias Mitigation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shreyaskc/BabelJudge)
- **Summary**: 针对LLM作为评判者存在位置偏差、冗长偏差和跨语言退化的问题，提出BabelJudge基准和可靠性审计框架，通过构造已知标签的成对项目消除标注成本。在Qwen2.5上发现复合偏差惩罚可靠性得分从印地语0.714降至斯瓦希里语0.550，斯瓦希里语顺序一致性仅0.480（接近随机），并扩展到智能体轨迹评估。

<h3 id="429">[429] Benchmarking Robot Memory Under Interference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22338](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22338) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soumil Rathi
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Robotics, Memory Architecture, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对机器人记忆中长上下文和干扰问题，提出RoboMME-Interference跨会话基准，通过向查询会话前插入无关会话构造干扰。发现感知记忆变体在无干扰时提升成功率，但随无关会话积累显著衰减，表明当前系统在长上下文记忆和抗干扰能力上存在不足。

<h3 id="430">[430] Flow Annealing Posterior Sampling for Function-Space Regression and Inverse Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22346) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaozhong Shi, Zachary E. Ross, Yisong Yue
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Uncertainty Estimation, Inverse Problems
- **Summary**: 针对随机过程回归和PDE逆问题的函数空间后验推断，提出流退火后验采样（FAPS），利用预训练函数空间流匹配先验，结合低秩协方差预条件器的朗之万校正，支持稀疏噪声观测。在多种高斯和非高斯回归基准及PDE逆问题上，产生准确的后验样本和不确定性量化，优于现有函数回归基线，测试时采样成本更低。

<h3 id="431">[431] Select-to-Act: Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Language Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22350](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22350) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanping Zhang, Adam Koziak, Yuhong Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Instruction Following, Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习样本效率低且现有方法将整个指令作为单一条件输入的问题，提出分层强化学习框架HRLLI，将指令分解为分块指导元素，高层策略选择当前状态最相关的指令块指导低层决策。在RTFM基准上，HRLLI一致优于强指令条件RL基线。

<h3 id="432">[432] Reliability-Guided Adaptive Ensembling for Robust Test-Time Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22351](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22351) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adam Koziak, Yuhong Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Test-Time Adaptation, Adversarial Robustness, Ensemble Learning
- **Summary**: 针对测试时适应在对抗污染流中脆弱的问题，提出SAFER框架，通过随机增强和相关性加权池化进行可靠性引导的预测聚合，并引入自适应混合以保留干净性能。在PACS、VLCS、OfficeHome上在不同攻击率下提高了TTA方法对抗攻击的韧性，同时保持竞争性的干净性能。

<h3 id="433">[433] On the Sparsity-Storage-Accuracy Tradeoff in Parsimoniously Activated Dictionary Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22352](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22352) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihui Zhao, Yuanbo Tang, Yang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dictionary Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对具有全局稀疏约束的字典学习方法缺乏概率解释和理论保证的问题，提出了一种称为PADL的简约激活字典学习方法，将其视为结构化生成模型下的最大后验估计，并解析刻画了稀疏性、存储成本与重建精度之间的权衡。在视觉基准上实现了可比稀疏性下更好的重建性能，并能加速视觉语言模型的推理。

<h3 id="434">[434] First-Token Broadcasters: Mechanistic Origins of Language Identity and Distributed Robustness in Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22361) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arjun Pillai, Christian Hoang, Anjelo Jann Laroza
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Multilingual Learning, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对多语言语言模型生成错误语言的问题，提出语言身份头消融（LIHA）方法，通过因果干预识别关注第一个token的语言身份传播头。发现指令微调将语言身份电路定位到早期层，并揭示了补偿的层级前馈模式。

<h3 id="435">[435] Large Language Model-Assisted Cleaning of Report-Derived Labels in a Large-Scale Chest CT Dataset</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22382) (cross-listed)
