- **Authors**: Yosuke Yamagishi, Atsushi Takamatsu, Mototsugu Sato, Tomohiro Kikuchi, Shouhei Hanaoka, Takeharu Yoshikawa, Osamu Abe
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Data Cleaning, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 针对大型胸部CT数据集CT-RATE中标签与报告不一致的问题，利用LLM（GPT-5.4）辅助清洗标签。通过比较LLM生成的标签与原有标签并进行放射科医生裁决，发现LLM标签在多数争议中更可靠，多LLM多数投票达到最高F1和kappa。

<h3 id="436">[436] Structured Hyperedge Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Vision Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22383](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22383) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Edwin Kwadwo Tenagyei, Lei Wang, Ugochukwu Ejike Akpudo, Jun Zhou, Yongsheng Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Vision Transformer, Hypergraph Learning
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有适配器式参数高效微调对视觉Transformer中token独立处理而忽略空间关系的问题，提出HyperAdapter超图适配器，通过超边空间进行结构化组感知适配。在多种视觉基准上优于强基线，尤其在结构化推理任务上提升显著。

<h3 id="437">[437] Reinforcement learning to improve large language model-based automated code compliance systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22402) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jack Wei Lun Shi, Minghao Dang, Wawan Solihin, Leong Hien Poh, Justin K.W. Yeoh
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Reinforcement Learning, Instruction Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对基于LLM的建筑规范自动化合规系统生成错误和幻觉规则的问题，提出P4IR两阶段框架，先用监督微调注入领域知识，再用组相对策略优化（GRPO）提升中间代码骨架的准确性。相比SFT基线，树编辑距离降低23.8%，Levenshtein距离降低38.6%。

<h3 id="438">[438] Gold Points Sniper: Self-guided Visual Reasoning in VLM for Fine-grained Action Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22409](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22409) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haodi Liu, Xinhang Yang, Kunda Yan, Sen Cui, Zeyu Zhang, Changshui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, Human-Robot Interaction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Haodi-Liu/GPS-Gold-Point-Sniper)
- **Summary**: 针对机器人从广角视图理解细粒度人类动作的需求，提出Gold Points Sniper (GPS)框架，赋予轻量级VLM自引导多模态推理能力。通过关键点提取、选择性苏格拉底提问和语义蕴含评估，使轻量级VLM在准确率上接近GPT-4o。

<h3 id="439">[439] Words as Difference Makers: How Large Language Models Determine Causal Structure in Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22430) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wolfgang Pietsch
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM如何从文本中学习因果结构的问题，论证LLM采用基于差异制造逻辑的变分归纳法，并分析token嵌入和自注意力的作用。发现其与实验方法平行，通过系统变化确定因果关系。

<h3 id="440">[440] MMGist: A Comprehensive Multimodal Benchmark for 2027</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22437](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22437) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenzhen Yuan, Jiacheng Ruan, Wutao Xiong, Chengping Zhao, Ting Liu, Yuzhuo Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对现有视觉语言基准存在不依赖视觉、性能饱和及异常项的问题，提出MMGist基准，包含7,262项覆盖7个能力维度。通过文本消融、跨模型饱和和异常检测过滤，保留模型排名的高保真度（Spearman ρ=0.98），减少69%评估项并提高78%区分度。

<h3 id="441">[441] DreamUV: Unwrap Artist-like UV by End-to-End Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quanyuan Ruan, Jiabao Lei, Xingyi Du, Xifeng Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 针对UV参数化中几何失真目标与艺术家偏好之间的差距，提出DreamUV框架，将UV展开视为生成式流匹配问题。采用边界感知训练和模型在环微调，生成更直边界和更紧致轴对齐岛屿，在用户研究中得到专业艺术家认可。

<h3 id="442">[442] CASPER in the Machine: Insights into Character Variety in LLM-Generated Stories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22454](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22454) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anneliese Brei, Abhisheik Sharma, Nicholas Sanaie, Lu Wang, Snigdha Chaturvedi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Creative Writing, Narrative Understanding
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成故事与人类故事在角色上的差异，借助叙事学定义分析八个角色维度。自动推断LLM和人类故事中角色类别并比较，发现有趣的异同点。

<h3 id="443">[443] Not All Claims Are Equally Risky: FACTOR for Adaptive Verification in Factual Long-Form Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22474](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22474) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Areeba Hassan, Arooj Kausar, Syeda Kisaa Fatima, Gibrail Islam, Mehwish Fatima
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Hallucination, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对LLM长文本生成中事实性错误且验证策略无差别的问题，提出FACTOR自适应验证模型，结合不确定性估计、自适应语言推理验证和候选重排序。在FactScore基准上提高事实性并降低验证成本。

<h3 id="444">[444] All Green, Still Broken: Real-Flow Verification Lessons from an LLM-Integrated, Multi-Market Web Application</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Bilal, Ali Hassaan Mughal
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Software Testing, Software Engineering, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对集成LLM、多市场和前端浏览器的现代Web应用难以测试的问题，分析252个缺陷修复提交，提出四裂缝框架。发现44%的修复属于组件级单元测试无法观察到的裂缝，并给出实践建议。

<h3 id="445">[445] Human and AI collaboration for pulmonary nodule segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22486) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongqiao Dong, Wenhao Chi, Ruobing Liang, Xiaokui Yang, Wenhua Liang, Peng Hou, Wenjun Pu, Yipeng Zhao, Ping Chen, Haiping Liu, Jianxing He, Bo Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 针对医学专家标注稀缺且纯AI不可靠的问题，提出Hi-Seg人机交互分割框架，基于SAM和人类迭代提示。在12中心1179例CT上达到约85% Dice，优于5个SOTA深度学习模型，非医学标注者达到初级医学生水平。

<h3 id="446">[446] An LLM-Orchestrated Agent for Directional-Coupler Design with Self-Consistent Eigenmode and FDTD Validation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22493) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saumya Biswas, Amrit De, Md Tauhidul Islam
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, EDA, Simulation
- **Summary**: 针对硅基定向耦合器设计问题，提出由LLM编排的设计代理，通过本征模求解器和FDTD验证迭代优化。最终实现50/50分束器，FDTD测得交叉比为0.498，残差0.0017。

<h3 id="447">[447] Enabling Cloud-Level Accuracy in Edge AI through IoT Data Preprocessing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22496](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22496) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aygün Varol, Katarzyna Kołodziej, Łukasz Sobczak, Michał Romaszewski, Przemysław Głomb, Naser Hossein Motlagh, Mirka Leino, Johanna Virkki
- **Subjects**: cs.PF; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Edge Computing, IoT, Prompt Engineering, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对边缘AI中本地LLM对原始传感器数据数值推理弱的问题，提出结构化提示构建框架，通过富文本表示（原始值、阈值感知描述、环境摘要标志）提升准确率。在室内外数据集上，本地准确率从50.9%提升至81.7%（室内），63.7%提升至89.3%（室外）。

<h3 id="448">[448] Fed-CausalDiff: Decoupled Synchronization for Federated Do-Simulation and Policy Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pengfei Li, Mohammad Khalil
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Causal Inference, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习仅拟合历史观测而无法进行干预推断和政策评估的问题，提出Fed-CausalDiff联邦因果扩散框架，通过解耦同步（DSS）分离全局因果得分和局部混杂得分。在四个数据集上实现更优的ATE和政策价值估计精度。

<h3 id="449">[449] Generative Robust Optimisation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22536](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22536) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhui Yin, Vassilis M. Charitopoulos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY; math.OC
- **Tags**: Robust Optimization, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 该论文针对传统鲁棒优化中不确定性集合固定且无法表示真实数据复杂依赖的问题，提出生成鲁棒优化框架，利用深度生成模型定义不确定性集合。通过五维评估框架和Wasserstein对抗自编码器实现，实验证明该方法在表达性、校准性和优化可处理性上均优于传统方法。

<h3 id="450">[450] Training-Free Semantic Correction for Autoregressive Visual Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22550) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junhao Chen, Chanyu Zhu, Zheqi Lv, Keting Yin, Shengyu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Autoregressive Model, Multimodal Learning, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对自回归视觉模型（AVM）中语义错误难以检测和纠正的问题，提出Gazer框架，通过多模态大语言模型反馈在生成过程中进行语义校正。包含反思性诊断和语义修正两个阶段，实验表明无需额外训练即可提升图像和视频的语义对齐与构图准确性。

<h3 id="451">[451] Look Light, Think Heavy: What Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Can and Cannot Do</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22565](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22565) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuoran Jin, Kejian Zhu, Hongbang Yuan, Yupu Hao, Pengfei Cao, Yubo Chen, Kang Liu, Jun Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Multimodal Learning, Visual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文系统探究多模态思维链推理的能力边界，发现CoT对感知任务可能产生副作用，而对推理任务有效；现有开源多模态推理模型提升有限，且视觉推理仍是瓶颈，模型表现出“轻视觉、重语言”的模式。

<h3 id="452">[452] Concept-Constrained Prompt Learning for Few-Shot CLIP Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22567) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Na Sang, Ding Ma, Rui Sang, Yuxuan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Few-Shot Learning, CLIP, Prompt Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/richael-sang/concept-constrained-prompt-learning)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对少样本提示学习中类别提示优化易过拟合基类的问题，提出概念约束提示学习（CCPL），通过将可学习的类别提示锚定到冻结的概念级文本原型上，实现轻量级正则化。实验表明CCPL在多个数据集上提升了基类到新类的调和平均性能。

<h3 id="453">[453] The Power of Light: Improving Synthetic-to-Real Domain Adaptation through Physically-Based Indirect Illumination</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22574](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22574) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hooman Tavakoli Ghinani, Tatjana Legler, Martin Ruskowski
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Object Detection, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 该论文针对合成数据到现实域的领域差距问题，系统研究了光照配置和背景复杂度对目标检测性能的影响。提出了SmartSDG管线，基于物理渲染的间接照明显著提升了视觉提示丰富度，减少了假阳性并加速了模型收敛。

<h3 id="454">[454] Context-Aware Distillation and Ablation for Text2DSL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22578](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22578) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander V. Kozachok, Alexander M. Nazimov, Shamil G. Magomedov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Knowledge Distillation, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: 该论文扩展了Text2DSL工作，通过上下文感知蒸馏（使用结构化上下文：BNF语法、API规范和标识符词汇）生成了更大且验证过的语料库。消融实验显示，结构化上下文是关键机制，其中词汇对语义质量贡献最大，API和BNF对结构有效性贡献显著。

<h3 id="455">[455] From CVE to CWE: Syscall-Based HIDS Generalisation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22581) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander V. Kozachok, Stanislav G. Vyugov, Shamil G. Magomedov
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 该论文实证研究了基于系统调用的主机入侵检测系统在相同CWE类内不同CVE之间的泛化能力。使用孤立森林和One-Class SVM，发现跨CVE迁移强烈依赖于源正常轮廓的广度，且校准假阳性率是诚实报告的先决条件。

<h3 id="456">[456] On the Position Bias of On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22600) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yan Xie, Sijie Zhu, Tiansheng Wen, Bo Chen, Yifei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Policy Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文发现在线策略蒸馏（OPD）存在位置偏差：后面位置的token因偏离教师分布而监督质量下降。基于此提出重要性加权在线策略蒸馏（IW-OPD），根据累积差异调整权重，在AIME-2025上性能提升高达6.9分。

<h3 id="457">[457] Federated Learning for Global Carbon Emission Forecasting: A Hybrid Time-Series Approach with Statistical and Neural Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22618](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22618) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Attia Qammar, Qazi Haseeb Yousaf, Ali Azam, Ammar Ahmed, Abdenacer Naouri, Tianrui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Time Series Forecasting, Climate Science
- **Summary**: 该论文提出一种联邦混合预测框架，集成ARIMA-GARCH、LSTM-Attention和XGBoost用于全球碳排放预测，在保护隐私的同时实现协作学习。在14个客户端上平均R2为0.73，MAPE为6.5%，证明其准确且合规。

<h3 id="458">[458] Orthogonal Representation Editing: Decoupling Semantic Entanglement in Batch Knowledge Editing of LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22627](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22627) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenhao Yu, Zhicong Lu, Bo Lv, Fangyin Ma, Kaiwen Wei, Shihao Yang, Nayu Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, Model Editing, Representation Learning
- **Venue**: Findings of ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YVVH/ORE)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对批量知识编辑中语义表示纠缠导致的性能下降，提出正交表示编辑（ORE），通过构建通用语义子空间并施加正交约束来解耦表示。实验表明ORE在批量编辑和跨语言编辑场景中优于现有方法。

<h3 id="459">[459] Foundation Models for Epileptogenic Zone Identification in Drug-Resistant Epilepsy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22657](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22657) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thi Kieu Khanh Ho, Thomas Lai, Petr Klimes, Jan Cimbalnik, Martin Pail, Milan Brazdil, Birgit Frauscher, Narges Armanfard
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Foundation Model, Epilepsy Detection
- **Summary**: 该论文开发了EpiiSLM，一个用于药物难治性癫痫中致痫区识别的双基础模型系统，结合信号基础模型和语言基础模型。仅需一夜发作间期睡眠数据，在接触级阳性预测值上达到0.978，显著优于基线。

<h3 id="460">[460] Confidently Wrong: Severity-Aware Calibration of Prompt-Injection Detectors under Attack Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22659](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22659) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Anas Biswas
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Prompt Injection, LLM Security, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了提示注入检测器在攻击分布偏移下的置信度校准，提出严重性度量S。发现检测器漏报时几乎总是高置信度，且标准校准误差无法反映该问题；内容锁定而非注入结构是主要原因。

<h3 id="461">[461] Data Evolution by Wittgenstein's Rule Following</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aydin Ghojogh, Benyamin Ghojogh
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Data Synthesis, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文受维特根斯坦规则遵循和家族相似性启发，提出数据演化框架WRF，通过结构描述符外推和目标平均来生成新数据集。在合成和图像数据集上验证了其生成有意义数据延续的能力。

<h3 id="462">[462] RigorBench: Benchmarking Engineering Process Discipline in Autonomous AI Coding Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22678) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Meher Bhaskar Madiraju, Meher Sai Preetam Madiraju
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Agent Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文提出RigorBench，首个衡量AI编码代理过程纪律的基准，评估规划、验证、恢复等五个维度。实验表明结构化过程纪律不仅提升过程质量（平均41%），还提高最终结果正确性（17%）。

<h3 id="463">[463] Only Ask What You Don't Know: Grounded Delta Planning for Efficient Multi-step RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22681](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22681) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei-Chieh Chou, Xuanjun Chen, Jian-Ren Lin, Claire Lin, Hung-yi Lee, Jyh-Shing Roger Jang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Multi-Hop Reasoning, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 该论文提出GDP-RAG，一种基于计划的多跳RAG框架，仅针对信息缺失部分进行检索。通过初步检索和间隙条件规划，在多个数据集上以更低成本实现了最高准确率（60.63%）。

<h3 id="464">[464] The Geometry of Refusal: Linear Instability in Safety-Aligned LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shivam Ratnakar, Kartikeya Vats
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Jailbreak Attack
- **Venue**: TrustNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文引入对比逻辑引导（CLS），揭示安全对齐LLM中拒绝行为的线性不稳定性。CLS通过输出分布操作实现越狱，在多个模型上达到高攻击成功率，同时也能用于增强安全。

<h3 id="465">[465] SCRUB-FL: Sanitizing and Cleansing Representations via Unlearning of Backdoors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22700](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22700) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Osama Wehbi, Sarhad Arisdakessian, Omar Abdel Wahab, Azzam Mourad, Hadi Otrok
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Backdoor Detection, Machine Unlearning
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习后训练阶段后门无法彻底消除的问题，提出SCRUB-FL两阶段解决方案，利用频谱分析、激活聚类和WGAN-GP捕获触发分布，并通过机器遗忘消除后门关联。在CIFAR-10和GTSRB上，攻击成功率降至3.88%，正常任务准确率保持91%以上。

<h3 id="466">[466] Libretto: Giving LLM Agents a Sense of Musical Structure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22708) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yichen Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Music Generation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对生成音乐难以检查和编辑的问题，提出Libretto框架，通过LLM原生语法显式定义音槽、声部和小节组织，并基于统计空间支持检索、诊断和迭代自修正。该框架将符号音乐从原始序列转化为可测量和可编辑的对象。

<h3 id="467">[467] Beyond Simpson's Paradox: A Cascade of Confounders in AI Agent Pull-Request Co-Authorship</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22711](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22711) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoran Yu, Xiaochong Jiang, Lifei Liu, Su Wang, Pin Qian, Yihang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Causal Inference
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0)
- **Summary**: 研究发现AI代理合著PR的合并率低于纯自主PR，但这一聚合结果存在辛普森悖论，分层分析后各代理内部效果方向逆转。进一步控制仓库和PR结构后，未发现任何代理有明确的合著因果效应，表明跨截面关联多为选择偏差。

<h3 id="468">[468] Leakage-Aware Benchmarking of LLM Forecasting: Real-Time Nowcasts as the Decision-Time Input for Macro Factor Ranking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22719](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22719) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mao Guan, Qian Chen
- **Subjects**: q-fin.ST; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, RAG, Quantitative Finance
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on AI Forecasting
- **Summary**: 针对LLM预测基准中信息泄露问题，提出泄漏控制的因子排名框架，仅使用决策时信息（滞后宏观变量、事件摘要等）。在2023-2026年间中位数Spearman秩IC为+0.154，但统计上不显著，且kNN宏观模拟可恢复类似信号。

<h3 id="469">[469] Subspace-Constrained Federated Learning with Low-Rank Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Neranjan Senarath, Rohit Muralitharan, Sadia Asif
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LoRA, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sadia-sigma-lab/Subspace-Constrained-Federated-learning-with-Lora)
- **Summary**: 针对联邦LoRA中异构客户端数据导致子空间未对齐的问题，提出子空间正则化联邦LoRA目标，鼓励局部更新靠近共享全局参考子空间。在RoBERTa-large上达到0.454的均值最佳准确率，子空间重叠度接近0.9999，验证了几何对齐假设。

<h3 id="470">[470] Interpretable Uncertainty Routing Separating Emotion Ambiguity from Distribution Shift in Facial Expression Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22725](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22725) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Keito Inoshita, Takato Ueno
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Affective Computing, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 针对单一不确定性分数混淆偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的问题，提出不确定性感知路由（UAR），利用DINOv2深度集成分离两种不确定性。偶然性与标注者分歧的斯皮尔曼相关达0.66，认知性检测分布偏移的AUROC为0.699。

<h3 id="471">[471] When Confidence Takes the Wrong Path: Diagnosing Retrieval-State Lock-In in RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22728) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sahib Julka
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Uncertainty Estimation, LLM Hallucination
- **Summary**: 针对RAG系统中置信度因检索状态锁定而失效的问题，提出将置信度分解为答案、证据和检索状态三个对象。在可检查的KG-RAG中，决策规则达到91.9%精度但覆盖率仅7.7%，表明置信度是对象特定的。

<h3 id="472">[472] AI Fiction in the Wild</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22748) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Neel Gupta, Maria Antoniak, Melanie Walsh
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Creative Writing, Text Generation, LLM Application
- **Venue**: MFS Cultural AI Conference
- **Summary**: 论文分析50万条ChatGPT对话，发现超过三分之一涉及小说创作（原创故事、角色扮演、同人小说等）。用户尤其倾向于同人小说和情色文学，并表现出无限故事需求等模式，引发对“唯我论读者—作者”和文化工业的思考。

<h3 id="473">[473] Evolutionary Optimization Reveals Structural Constraints on Reservoir Architecture for Spatiotemporal Chaos</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22765) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nima Dehghani
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG; nlin.CD
- **Tags**: Reservoir Computing, Evolutionary Computation
- **Summary**: 针对标准储层计算中固定随机网络的问题，采用进化优化对储层架构（规模、连接度等）进行演化。在时空混沌预测中，进化减少了预测误差，并揭示了可解释的结构约束，如保持特征谱包络和优化模块化性。

<h3 id="474">[474] Noise is Signal: Density-Based Outliers as Leading Indicators of Occupational Emergence in Labor Market Text</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22769](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22769) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shreyash Rawat
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Labor Market Analysis, Text Classification, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 认为标准NLP流程丢弃的低密度异常值实际上预示着新职业的出现。在84,988条招聘信息上验证了“涌现-密度反转”假设，高EOS异常组形成稳定聚类的速度更快（1.4季度 vs 4.1季度）。改进的EOS评分将聚类形成预测F1从0.61提升至0.74。

<h3 id="475">[475] GeoRouteNet: Geometry-Enhanced Non-Autoregressive Neural Solver for the Traveling Salesman Problem</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22776](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22776) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiang Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Combinatorial Optimization, Reinforcement Learning, Combinatorial Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对非自回归神经求解器缺乏几何归纳偏置的问题，提出GeoRouteNet，集成中心节点特征、径向基函数、距离感知图注意力和多候选自比较强化学习。在TSP50上最优性差距0.32%，TSP100上1.26%，TSPLIB上从17.12%降至3.60%。

<h3 id="476">[476] Explainable AI for Mental Health Prediction in Drug-Affected Populations with Dragonfly Algorithm and GAN Oversampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22780) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahnaf Atef Choudhury, Shahriar Siddique Ayon, Md. Ebrahim Hossain, Abdullah Al Mamun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Mental Health AI, GAN
- **Venue**: 16th International Conference on Advanced Computer Information Technologies (ACIT 2026)
- **Summary**: 针对药物影响人群心理健康AI预测中类别不平衡、低可解释性和超参数优化问题，提出结合PCA-IG特征选择、GAN过采样和龙蝇算法优化XGBoost的框架。准确率94.17%，加权F1 93.80%，并通过SHAP提供实例级解释。

<h3 id="477">[477] Scaling Audio Models Efficiently: A Joint Study of Compute Constraints and Optimization Behavior</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22790) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vyom Agarwal, Mokshda Gangrade, Siddharth Pal, Jerry Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Speech Emotion Recognition, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 研究语音处理任务中计算分配与模型性能的权衡，提出统一框架分析模型规模、输入长度和表示分辨率三个维度。实验表明增大模型收益递减，SER最优音频时长约4秒，降低编码器令牌分辨率可有效减少计算量（WER仅增\<3%）。

<h3 id="478">[478] Bagpiper-TTS: Natural Language Guided Universal Speech Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22811) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinchuan Tian, Haoran Wang, Siddhant Arora, Takashi Maekaku, Keita Goto, Jin Sakuma, Yusuke Shinohara, Chao-Han Huck Yang, Shinji Watanabe
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, LLM Agent, Singing Voice Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对经典TTS输入格式僵化的问题，提出Bagpiper-TTS通用语音合成系统，通过自然语言提示推理用户意图并生成丰富描述，再合成语音。在Seed-TTS-Eval基准上词错误率1.7%，支持多说话人、意图转语音、角色扮演等任务。

<h3 id="479">[479] DBT-Bleed: Dual-Branch Temporal Modeling with Key-Frame Selection for Surgical Bleeding Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22829) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sudhanshu Mishra, Jialang Xu, Jensen Ang, Evangelos B. Mazomenos, Beng Ti Ang, Yueming Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Video Understanding, Surgical Video Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对手术中出血不良事件检测因视觉相似残留血而困难的问题，提出DBT-Bleed双分支时间建模框架，使用熵驱动关键帧选择（HiRED）处理长视频。在MultiBypass数据集上F1提升6.53%，并在零样本跨手术场景下展现强迁移性。

<h3 id="480">[480] OrthoMotion:Disentangling Camera and Subject Motion via Geometry Semantics Orthogonal Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22835](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22835) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijie Meng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Disentanglement, Controllable Generation
- **Venue**: SCA2026
- **Summary**: 针对可控视频生成中2D条件束缚相机和物体运动的问题，提出OrthoMotion，通过几何通道（RoPE旋转）和语义通道（门控值注入）正交分离两种运动。在保持保真度的同时将串扰降低2.4倍以上，达到SoTA。

<h3 id="481">[481] CLIP-guided Diffusion Model for Backdoor Generation in Sensor-based Human Activity Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22837](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22837) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Toby Briston, Illya Kosyk, Kuniyih S
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Human Activity Recognition, Backdoor Attack
- **Summary**: 针对传感器人类活动识别（HAR）模型数据不足的问题，提出了一种基于CLIP引导的扩散模型后门攻击技术IMU-DM-CLIP。该方法通过扩散模型生成带触发器的合成数据注入后门。实验表明，即使在10%的后门注入率和10%的数据引导率下，攻击依然成功。

<h3 id="482">[482] The Unseen Hand: Manipulating Model Fairness and SHAP with Targeted Identity Re-Association Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22858](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22858) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sannaan Khan, Muhammad U. S. Khan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Adversarial Robustness, Explainable AI
- **Venue**: NeurIPS Workshops 2025
- **Summary**: 针对机器学习模型公平性和可解释性审计机制易受操纵的问题，提出了目标身份重关联攻击（TIRA）家族，包括概率微洗牌（PMiS）和概率秩移位微扰动（PRSMP）。攻击无需访问模型内部，可有效推动公平性指标向理想值靠近并完全混淆SHAP解释，且比先前攻击更隐蔽。

<h3 id="483">[483] Discovering Crystal Structure Prediction Algorithms with an AI Co-Scientist</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22866](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22866) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kiyoung Seong, Nayoung Kim, Sungsoo Ahn
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Crystal Structure Prediction, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 针对从化学成分生成晶体结构的算法发现问题，提出了人机共同发现系统HACO，通过跨领域搜索和稀疏人类引导，将视觉领域的MaskGIT框架迁移至晶体结构预测，并扩展了对称性令牌、分层采样等模块，得到MaskGXT模型。在MP-20多态分裂上达到79.06%的METRe准确率，优于最强基线70.87%。

<h3 id="484">[484] VideoLatent: Video-Language Learning via Latent Self-Forcing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22870](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22870) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zi-Yuan Hu, Zicong Tang, Shijia Huang, Yanyang Li, Michael R. Lyu, Liwei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multimodal Learning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对现有基于思维链的多模态大模型在视频理解中需要大量标注和计算开销的问题，提出了VideoLatent模型，通过潜在注入模块和潜在自强制训练范式（包括对齐和多样性目标），仅依赖标准视频问答三元组进行视觉潜在推理。在14个基准上优于现有模型，且训练/推理开销分别降低约6倍和68倍。

<h3 id="485">[485] SpotAttention: Plug-In Block-Sparse Routing for Pretrained Long-Context Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22874) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huzama Ahmad, Se-Young Yun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文Transformer中预填充和解码计算成本高的问题，提出了SpotAttention，一种轻量级注意力分布选择器，通过KL散度蒸馏学习估计预训练模型的注意力分布，并采用top-p规则分配每查询每层预算。在Qwen3和Qwen3.5上匹配密集精度，128K上下文解码比FlashAttention快3.9倍。

<h3 id="486">[486] Priority-Aware Learning-Unlearning Correction for Dynamic Decentralized LoRA Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22878](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22878) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nuocheng Yang, Yechen He, Sihua Wang, Zihan Chen, Tony Q. S. Quek, Changchuan Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LoRA, Machine Unlearning
- **Summary**: 针对去中心化联邦微调中设备动态加入/离开导致的更新纠偏瓶颈，提出了基于正交LoRA的优先级感知学习-遗忘校正框架，通过拓扑调整、局部校正、近端阻尼和同步调度等策略处理主导残差项。实验表明框架能有效处理设备加入和离开事件，不同残差区域需要不同的校正动作。

<h3 id="487">[487] Explanation-Guided Medical Named Entity Recognition with Stability and Boundary Awareness for Atopic Dermatitis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22886) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xueguang Li, Di Lin, Xue Jiang, Yanxi Li, Yugang Chi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Named Entity Recognition, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 针对中文特应性皮炎临床文本中医学命名实体识别的可靠性和鲁棒性问题，提出了基于解释引导的NER框架，通过扰动分析评估解释稳定性和边界敏感度，并采用自适应融合策略结合局部与全局解释，将解释信号融入训练中的稳定、边界感知和一致性约束。在多个NER模型上取得一致性能提升。

<h3 id="488">[488] Explainable AI in Speaker Recognition -- Attention Map Visualisation and Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22901) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yanze Xu, Mark D. Plumbley, Wenwu Wang
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Speaker Recognition, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 针对说话人识别网络中注意力机制可视化评估不足的问题，提出了改进的RISE-eval评估算法，并系统比较了GradCAM和LayerCAM两种方法。改进算法解决了原版缺陷，实验表明两种方法在不同实验条件下各有优势。

<h3 id="489">[489] From Fragments to Paths: Task-Level Context Recovery for Large Industrial Codebases</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22906](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22906) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiawei He, Weisong Sun, Mengyu Shi, Jie Jia, Tong Bian, Xikai Yang, Dong Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Understanding, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对大型工业代码库中现有搜索方法仅检索局部片段、无法恢复任务级上下文的问题，提出了DeepDiscovery，采用两阶段定位-推理框架，在多关系仓库结构中恢复任务相关上下文。在SWE-bench Verified上达到78.6%解决率，优于基线8.2个百分点。

<h3 id="490">[490] Graph-Enhanced Large Language Models for Spatial Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22909) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicole R. Schneider, Kent O&#39;Sullivan, Hanan Samet
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, LLM Reasoning, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型空间推理能力不足的问题，提出了图增强推理的搜索引擎愿景，利用存储在图中的空间数据推进LLM回答复杂空间问题。论文为展望性质，尚未给出定量结果。

<h3 id="491">[491] Cross-lingual Retrieval-Augmented Classification for Dysarthria Severity Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22910](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22910) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Taeyoung Jeong, Insung Lee, Du-Seong Chang, Myoung-Wan Koo
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Medical AI, Cross-Lingual
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 针对标签病理语音数据稀缺的问题，提出了跨语言检索增强分类（CRAC），通过监督对比学习构建严重性嵌入空间，并从不同语言的语料库中检索参考特征，利用交叉注意力融合输入和参考。在韩语和意大利语数据集上分别达到87.3%和86.7%的均衡准确率，比单语言基线提高8.4和20.0个百分点。

<h3 id="492">[492] Intend, Reflect, Refine: An Adaptive Multimodal Reflection Framework for Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22913) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zisheng Chen, Yuping Qiu, Jianhua Han, Tao Tang, Xiuwei Chen, Likui Zhang, Ying-Cong Chen, Hang Xu, Xiaodan Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Vision-Language-Action Model, End-to-End Driving
- **Summary**: 针对现有端到端自动驾驶VLA模型直接生成轨迹、缺乏对后果检查的问题，提出了IRR-Drive自适应多模态反思框架，先生成初步意图并预测未来BEV表示，形成文本+BEV双模态反思空间进行自我纠正，并设计自适应反思奖励以平衡性能和效率。在NAVSIM基准上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="493">[493] Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Sparse-Reward Search in Commutative Algebra</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22922) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giorgi Butbaia, Paul Orland, Coco Huang, Davide Passaro, Lucas Fagan, Michele Tarquini, Hailong Dao, David Eisenbud, Ali Shehper, Sergei Gukov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.AC; math.CO
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Hierarchical RL, Mathematical Reasoning, Graph Neural Network
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对应用机器学习解决数学猜想时奖励极端稀疏的问题，以Kalai代数Hirsch猜想为例，将其转化为图上的稀疏奖励强化学习问题，提出了基于约束选项的层次强化学习框架，采用等变图神经网络策略学习时间抽象。实验表明该方法在广泛度数上优于经典RL和贪婪搜索。

<h3 id="494">[494] BEV-Denoise: Learning Intrinsic Noise for Accurate Bird's-Eye-View Semantic Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22931) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dooseop Choi, Kyounghwan An, Kyoung-Wook Min
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Semantic Segmentation, BEV Perception
- **Summary**: 针对鸟瞰图语义分割中学习到的BEV特征包含内在噪声的问题，提出了BEV-Denoise框架，利用DDPM的噪声估计能力设计UNet模块估计噪声并从特征中减去，采用任务分解顺序学习范式进行监督。应用至四个现有模型，在nuScenes数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="495">[495] Provable Benefits of RLVR over SFT for Reasoning Models: Learning to Backtrack Efficiently</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22938) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stanley Wei, Juno Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 针对为何强化微调比监督微调提升推理能力的问题，将思维链推理建模为图上的路径搜索，理论证明了SFT因缺乏负样本无法学会高效回溯，而RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）能学会回溯，导致推理时计算量指数级分离。进一步表明RLVR的推理轨迹可蒸馏至基础模型。

<h3 id="496">[496] Attacking the Trusted Imagination: Oracle-Level Integrity Attacks on Imagine-then-Act World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22966) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Linghan Chen, Kaiyan Ji, Minyu Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Vision-Language-Action Model, Adversarial Robustness, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对想象-然后行动VLA策略中信任想象作为攻击面的问题，发现扰动观察可轻易破坏潜在轨迹。采用能力威胁模型，通过投影梯度下降攻击观测到想象的映射。评估三个模型，非目标破坏比随机强60倍并被完美检测，目标控制受限，自适应攻击者只能放弃破坏。

<h3 id="497">[497] Understanding Parallel Samplers in Masked Diffusion via Random Walks on Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22976](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22976) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vansh Bansal, Cho Cholyeon, Syamantak Kumar, Sujay Sanghavi, Purnamrita Sarkar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Diffusion Sampling, Masked Diffusion
- **Summary**: 该论文针对掩码扩散模型中并行采样策略缺乏可验证沙盒的问题，提出利用图上的随机游走作为沙盒，发展了一种二分采样器。理论证明最低熵并行采样并非普遍最优，而二分采样器在完美训练下是精确的。实验表明二分采样器在速度和质量之间取得了更好的权衡。

<h3 id="498">[498] StatABench: Dataset and Framework for Evaluating Statistical Analysis Capabilities of LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22977](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22977) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youxin Zhu, Yixuan Ding, Peng Lai, Longyue Wang, Bingyi Jing, Guanhua Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Statistical Analysis
- **Summary**: 现有LLM统计分析基准在范围和格式上有限。该论文引入StatABench，包含Stat-Closed和Stat-Open两部分，使用LangChain MCP框架和LLM-as-Judge评估。实验表明GPT-5.1在封闭部分仅达68.6%，最佳开源模型60.6%，揭示了当前LLM与可靠统计分析的差距。

<h3 id="499">[499] Neural Architecture Search of Sample Reweighting Networks for Complex Distribution Shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22991](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22991) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Keisuke Sugawara, Kento Uchida, Shinichi Shirakawa
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture Search, Class Imbalance, Noisy Label Learning, Sample Reweighting
- **Venue**: PRICAI 2025
- **Summary**: 针对样本重加权网络Meta-Weight-Net在同时存在标签噪声和类别不平衡时性能下降的问题，该论文引入神经架构搜索，使用树结构Parzen估计器探索最优隐藏层和节点数。在CIFAR-10/100上的实验证明了其有效性。

<h3 id="500">[500] Group-Graph Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.22995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22995) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunan Wang, Minghui Song, Zihan Zhang, Shaohan Huang, Haizhen Huang, Furu Wei, Weiwei Deng, Feng Sun, Qi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Credit Assignment
- **Summary**: 长期域智能体强化学习面临奖励稀疏和延迟问题，现有步骤级方法信用分配粗粒度。该论文提出组图策略优化G2PO，将轨迹转化为全局状态转移图，使用组聚合状态值估计和边中心优势估计。在WebShop等基准上相比GRPO提升高达22.2%。

<h3 id="501">[501] ScalingAttention: Discovering Intrinsic Sparse Attention Topology for Video Diffusion Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruiliang Zhou, Xuecheng Wu, Kang He, Guangyun Han, Bin Liu, Qinqin Chen, Wende Xu, Qingjie Zhao, Chengru Song
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Transformer, Sparse Attention
- **Summary**: 视频扩散Transformer中3D全注意力导致计算瓶颈。该论文提出ScalingAttention，无训练框架，通过权重编码稀疏拓扑和保真度感知敏感性调优，结合硬件对齐的块稀疏内核。在Wan2.1上实现1.90倍加速且保持高质量。

<h3 id="502">[502] Physics-Guided Spatiotemporal State Space Modeling for Lookahead Molten Pool Segmentation in Laser Wire-Feed Welding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23028](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23028) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sen Li, Haichao Cui, Changhao Yin, Chendong Shao, Yaqi Wang, Xinhua Tang, Fenggui Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Image Segmentation, Physics-Informed Learning
- **Summary**: 激光送丝焊接中实时熔池感知存在延迟。该论文提出物理引导的时空状态空间网络WeldMamba，利用历史图像、工艺参数和电信号预测未来熔池语义。在43序列数据集上500毫秒预测达到74.63% mIoU。

<h3 id="503">[503] EvoRubrics: Dynamic Rubrics as Rewards via Adversarial Co-Evolution for LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23038](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23038) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongxin Ding, Baixiang Huang, Yue Fang, Weibin Liao, Zheng Li, Jinyang Zhang, Zhijing Wu, Junfeng Zhao, Yasha Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Reward Model, Curriculum Learning
- **Summary**: 静态评分准则在强化学习中逐渐失效。该论文提出EvoRubrics，策略LLM和评分生成器对抗性共进化，自动生成课程。实验表明一致优于静态和动态评分基线，即使无监督变体也有收益。

<h3 id="504">[504] The Model as One Rater Among Several: Measuring Political Positions in Data-Sparse Regions with a Language-Model Panel</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tarek Gara
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Computational Social Science, LLM Evaluation, Political Measurement
- **Summary**: 现有政治立场测量工具在西方政党体系外失效。该论文将LLM作为小组中一个易错评分者，引入适用性规则和透镜系统。实验表明添加定义提高评分者一致性，Krippendorff alpha达0.86，分歧具有信息性。

<h3 id="505">[505] Prime Fourier Embeddings: A Principled Basis for Modular Arithmetic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23044](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23044) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyunsang Hwang, Suhyun Bae, Donghun Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Modular Arithmetic, Number Theory
- **Summary**: 标准神经嵌入无法暴露数字的代数结构。该论文提出素数傅里叶嵌入PFE，将整数编码为素数索引的正余弦对。理论证明线性映射块对角化，每个素数独立。实验显示任务相关通道与无关通道专业化比率超过500倍。

<h3 id="506">[506] Domain Adaptation Under Wireless Network Constraints: When Does It Become Green?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23047](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23047) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Illyyne Saffar, Aurélie Boisbunon, Shruti Bothe
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Domain Adaptation, Energy Efficiency, Wireless Networks
- **Summary**: 无监督域自适应UDA可能比重新训练更耗能。该论文比较UDA和单任务的能耗，提出确定UDA比重新训练更节能的最小目标域数量。结果从能耗和标注角度澄清了UDA的适用条件。

<h3 id="507">[507] HALAS: A Human-Annotated Dataset of Hallucinations of Modern ASR Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23048](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23048) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mateusz Barański, Jan Jasiński, Julitta Bartolewska, Marcin Witkowski, Konrad Kowalczyk
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Dataset, ASR Hallucination
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 现有ASR幻觉检测方法在非语音或人工音频上评估。该论文引入HALAS，首个自然发生幻觉的人工标注数据集，涵盖7个ASR模型在真实财报电话会议上的记录。分析显示跨模型词汇重叠，最佳检测方法F1仅53.1%。

<h3 id="508">[508] Training Open Models for Agentic Phone Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23049](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23049) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengyang Tang, Xin Lai, Pengyuan Lyu, Xinyuan Wang, Tianyi Bai, Chenxin Li, Yiduo Guo, Huawen Shen, Yuxuan Liu, Junyi Li, Zhengyao Fang, Yang Ding, Yi Zhang, Weinong Wang, Xingran Zhou, Liang Wu, Fei Tang, Sunqi Fan, Shangpin Peng, Zheng Ruan, Anran Zhang, Benyou Wang, Ji-Rong Wen, Rui Yan, Chengquan Zhang, Han Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, GUI Automation, Mobile AI
- **Summary**: 训练开放模型进行手机操作面临环境慢、有状态等问题。该论文提出PhoneBuddy，结合真实应用环境和模拟应用环境PhoneWorld，经过监督微调和强化学习。在150任务人工评估中成功率从36.67%提升至45.33%。

<h3 id="509">[509] Physics-governed executable modelling of triboelectric nanogenerators</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23051](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23051) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongfa Zhao, Baiqiao Wang, Tiancong Zhao, Chun Jin, Hanlin Zhou, Mingrui Shu, Minyi Xu, Liwei Lin, Wenbo Ding, Zhong Lin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MS
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Scientific Computing, Energy Systems
- **Summary**: 摩擦纳米发电机TENG的预测建模碎片化。该论文提出电荷定义建模框架并实现TENG-CLAW，建立自洽静电层次结构。该框架将用户请求转化为物理可行仿真任务，提供可追溯的研究基础设施。

<h3 id="510">[510] From Text Metrics to Model Internals: A Study of Whisper ASR Hallucination Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23060](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23060) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jan Jasiński, Mateusz Barański, Julitta Bartolewska, Marcin Witkowski, Konrad Kowalczyk
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, ASR Hallucination, Probing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: ASR模型幻觉检测仍然具有挑战性。该论文在Whisper large v3上研究基于文本、LLM和内部解码器状态探针的检测方法。实验表明无需参考转录的解码器探针性能最强，结合文本和内部状态的元分类器达到最佳效果。

<h3 id="511">[511] MotionHalluc: Diagnosing Kinematic Hallucinations in Fine-Grained Motion Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23061) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Weile Guo, Shenghong He, Danying Mo, Chengdong Xu, Xuexun Liu, Chao Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Multimodal Learning, Motion Hallucination
- **Summary**: 跨视频比较中的运动指令生成存在运动幻觉。该论文引入MotionHalluc基准，包含1540个细粒度问题，评估方向、归因和时间幻觉。提出PPV基线，通过注入测量信息平均提升性能10.6%。

<h3 id="512">[512] Attention-Spectrum Regularization for Replay-Free Continual Multimodal LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23063) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chuangxin Zhao, Canran Xiao, Siyuan Ma, Mengyao Lyu, Yanbiao Ma, Jun Xia, Guiguang Ding, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Multimodal LLM, Attention Mechanism
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Creative-zcx/attention-spectrum-replay)
- **Summary**: 多模态大模型持续微调导致遗忘。该论文提出注意力谱正则化ASR，无回放，通过保持跨模态注意力结构来防止遗忘。在多个VQA和指令微调基准上持续改进最终性能并减少遗忘。

<h3 id="513">[513] Safety in Self-Evolving LLM Agent Systems: Threats, Amplification, and Case Studies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23075](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23075) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruixiao Lin, Xinhao Deng, Qingming Li, Jianan Ma, Yunhao Feng, Yuqi Qing, Zhenyuan Li, Yechao Zhang, Shiwen Cui, Changhua Meng, Tianwei Zhang, Xingjun Ma, Qi Li, Ke Xu, Shouling Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, Agent Evolution
- **Summary**: 自进化LLM智能体系统面临新的安全威胁，如攻击持久化与自我放大。本文通过模块-生命周期攻击面（MLAS）矩阵系统分析威胁，发现17个关键威胁和7种跨放大效应。实验表明，进化原生设计激活3.5倍攻击面单元格，攻击持久率100%，而静态防御仅阻止2.5%的攻击。

<h3 id="514">[514] AdaReP:Adaptive Re-Planning under Model Mismatch for Neural World-Model Predictive Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23079](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23079) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yutian Cheng, Xiaojian Ma, Xianhao Wang, Min Yang, Rongpeng Su, Hangxin Liu, Xi Chen, Shuai Li, Qing Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model-Based RL, Robotics, Inference Optimization
- **Venue**: ICANN 2026
- **Summary**: 神经网络世界模型结合模型预测控制（MPC）需每步重规划，计算开销大。AdaReP作为免训练封装，通过在线调整重规划容忍度减少查询次数。在物理机器人实验中，查询次数减少80%以上，且任务性能相当。

<h3 id="515">[515] FLFL: Federated Latent Factor Learning for Private Recovery of Spatio-Temporal Signals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23091](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23091) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chengjun Yu, Di Wu, Yi He, Jia Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Privacy, Data Imputation
- **Summary**: 无线传感器网络中数据缺失问题需集中式处理，存在隐私风险。本文提出联邦潜在因子学习（FLFL）模型，在传感器级联邦学习框架中融入时空相关正则化。在四个真实数据集上，FLFL在恢复精度和隐私保护方面显著优于八种基线方法。

<h3 id="516">[516] ReNIO: Reweighting Negative Trajectory Importance for LLM On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23104](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23104) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chen Lin, Kedi Chen, Wei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Reasoning, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/BDML-lab/ReNIO)
- **Summary**: 在线蒸馏（OPD）平等对待所有学生输出，但错误输出更富信息。ReNIO通过学生-教师概率比加权负轨迹，无需最终答案正确性。在数学推理和代码生成任务中，ReNIO相对提升高达10.00%。

<h3 id="517">[517] Self-Evolution for Multi-Turn Tool-Calling Agents via Divergence-Point Preference Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23112](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23112) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaqiang Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 多轮工具调用智能体需协调长序列，现有方法分离推理与学习。ToolGraph结合模式拓扑与过渡权重，并利用分歧点偏好学习（DPO）优化。在tau2-bench上，加权平均奖励从0.304提升至0.355（+16.8%），主要收益来自航空和零售领域。

<h3 id="518">[518] PRIDE: Privileged Information-enhanced Distillation for Empathetic Dialogue Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23124](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23124) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaqiang Wu, Zhouan Zhu, Shangfei Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Knowledge Distillation, Affective Computing
- **Summary**: 大型模型用于共情对话计算量大，知识蒸馏难以传递细微共情。PRIDE利用特权信息（如心理标注）训练，结合共情推理提示、多源注意力和双对齐损失。在多种数据集上，PRIDE匹配甚至超越更大教师模型的准确率和语义相关性。

<h3 id="519">[519] AI-Empowered UAV-Assisted Backscatter Localization and ISAC for Zero-Energy IoT: A Comprehensive Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23125](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23125) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruhul Amin Khalil
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Survey, IoT, Wireless Networks
- **Summary**: 零能耗IoT设备在反向散射通信中面临局限。本文综述了AI赋能的无人机辅助反向散射定位和通感一体化（ISAC）技术，提供了结构化分类和量化趋势分析。指出了现实信道建模、能效、可重复性等未来挑战。

<h3 id="520">[520] LLM-Aided A* Search in Non-Geometric Network Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23136](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23136) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nouf Alabbasi, Esraa Ghourab, Omar Alhussein
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Route Planning, Combinatorial Optimization, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 非几何网络图中最短路径搜索缺乏几何启发函数。本文提出LLM辅助A*算法，利用地标距离指导LLM生成中间航点。在多达2000个节点的多种图结构中，扩展节点数减少约50%，路径成本增加极小。

<h3 id="521">[521] Interpretable Probabilistic Medical Image Segmentation via Gaussian Process with Explicit Modelling of Annotation Bias and Variability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23177) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Li, Yuliang Huang, Shaheer U. Saeed, Qianye Yang, Vasilis Stavrinides, Zachary M. C. Baum, Dean C. Barratt, J. Alison Noble, Tom Vercauteren, Yipeng Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Segmentation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Venue**: MICCAI 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/QiLi111/GPS-Var)
- **Summary**: 医学图像分割中多标注者偏差通常隐式编码。本文提出基于高斯过程的对数空间概率分割框架，显式建模标注者偏差和方差。在多标注者数据集上，该模型在保持分割精度的同时改善了不确定性校准。

<h3 id="522">[522] Memory Contagion: Cross-Temporal Propagation of Evaluator Bias via Agent Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23195](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23195) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zewen Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Memory, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: LLM智能体记忆系统可能传播评估者偏差。本文形式化记忆传染现象，证明即使在完美记忆整合下，偏差也能跨时间传播。实验显示，在污染率仅0.2时即可检测到偏差传播。

<h3 id="523">[523] When Does Intrinsic Self-Correction Help? A Task-Sensitive Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23196) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elroy Stav, Dvir Berlowitz, Maayan Orner, Sarit Kraus
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Self-Correction, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 内在自我纠正（SC）的可靠性存疑。本文采取任务敏感视角，分析SC在不同任务中的机制。结果表明，当任务结构便于修订时SC可带来稳定增益，而非统一可靠。

<h3 id="524">[524] The Correct Answer Trap: Pedagogically-Grounded Detection and Feedback for Hidden Misconceptions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23205](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23205) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Moiz Imran, Sahan Bulathwela
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Education Technology, AI Tutoring, Misconception Detection
- **Venue**: AIED PEAF 2026
- **Summary**: 基于答案正确性的自动反馈会忽略隐藏误解。本文提出分级评估量表和检测-验证-升级流水线。开放权重推理模型检测率达84%，但假阳性率高，因此需要诊断性追问。

<h3 id="525">[525] Where Is My Physics Wrong? Localized and Identifiable Discovery of Model Discrepancy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Symbolic Regression, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 混合模型拟合全局校正会掩盖局部错误。LISDD框架通过定位故障区域、稀疏符号识别和有限样本检验，实现局部可标识的模型差异发现。实验显示物理参数偏差仅为0.002，定位F1达0.80，正确恢复符号形式的概率为1。

<h3 id="526">[526] MuPPET: A Benchmark for Contextual Privacy of LLM Assistants in Multi-Party Conversations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23217](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23217) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elena Sofia Ruzzetti, Cornelius Emde, Sangdoo Yun, Seong Joon Oh, Martin Gubri
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Privacy, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有上下文隐私基准仅考虑单对话者场景。本文提出多对话者隐私测试基准MuPPET。实验表明，模型在多对话者设置中泄漏更多，开源小模型更易受攻击，现有防御措施效果有限。

<h3 id="527">[527] RS-Gen: A Multi-Stage Agentic Framework for Reasoning and Search-Augmented Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23221](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23221) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Feifei Bian, Zhimin Zheng, Wei Deng, Daiguo Zhou, Jian Luan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, LLM Agent, RAG
- **Summary**: 图像生成模型缺乏深度推理和实时外部知识。RS-Gen提出即插即用、免训练的多阶段智能体框架，通过“提问-求解”闭环实现逻辑推理与知识弥补。在WISE和RISEBench上，将Qwen-Image和Qwen-Image-Edit性能分别提升0.313和19.70，达到开源SOTA。

<h3 id="528">[528] SteerVTE: Seamless Video Text Editing with Style and Glyph Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23254](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23254) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kai Zeng, Moran Li, Zhengwei Wang, Yingchen Yu, Yiheng Lin, Ruichuan An, Ming Lu, Qi She, Wentao Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, Video Editing
- **Summary**: 视频文字编辑面临跨帧精度和风格一致性的挑战。SteerVTE基于冻结视频扩散模型，附加风格编码器和双粒度字形编码器，并通过字形感知损失和三阶段渐进训练实现精细编辑。在文字准确度、风格一致性和时间连贯性上显著优于基线。

<h3 id="529">[529] P-JEPA: Procedural Video Representation Learning via Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23256) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Felix Tristram, Stefano Gasperini, Benjamin Killeen, Marcel Walch, Christian Benz, Nassir Navab, Ghazal Ghazaei
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Action Segmentation
- **Summary**: 针对程序性视频中长程依赖难以建模的问题，提出P-JEPA框架，通过将视频压缩到密集的帧对齐动作空间并预测掩码潜向量，实现30分钟以上视频的表示学习。在EgoExo4D、EgoProceL和Assembly101上的实验表明，该方法在细粒度动作分类上达到SOTA，参数比基于LLM的方法少一个数量级，且支持实时推理。

<h3 id="530">[530] Dynamic multi-agent deep reinforcement learning-based pricing and incentivization approach in multimodal transportation networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23257) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Khadidja Kadem, Mostafa Ameli, Carlos Lima Azevedo, Mahdi Zargayouna, Latifa Oukhellou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Transportation Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对多模式交通网络中共享出行需求不均、利益冲突等问题，提出多智能体深度强化学习框架，包含公共部门激励策略和共享出行定价智能体。在早高峰模拟中，激励策略降低通勤成本约20%、排放约10%，并提升公交利润近一倍，实现了公平与效率的平衡。

<h3 id="531">[531] Exposing the Illusion of Erasure in Knowledge Editing for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23276) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Advik Raj Basani, Anshuman Chhabra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, LLM Safety, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 揭示知识编辑（KE）方法并未真正擦除知识，而是将知识重新分布到表示空间中，且编辑后的知识位于狭窄各向异性区域，易受对抗攻击。通过机制分析证明KE算法本质上可被绕过，呼吁重新评估事后更新在LLM中的应用。

<h3 id="532">[532] Towards a Bathroom-Centered Human-Building Digital Twin Framework for Indoor Safety Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23292) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanzhi Su, Huiying
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Digital Twin, Human Activity Recognition, Indoor Safety
- **Summary**: 针对老年人浴室安全这一人-环境耦合问题，提出以浴室为中心的人-建筑数字孪生框架，集成语义环境表示、骨架人体表示、空间语义耦合和交互行为分析。基于Unity的原型验证了框架可行性，为隐私敏感、交互感知的适老化数字孪生提供了方法论基础。

<h3 id="533">[533] VideoAgent: All-in-One Framework for Video Understanding and Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23327](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23327) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hengji Zhou, Lingxuan Huang, Jian Wang, Bing Zhou, Si Wu, Lianghao Xia, Chao Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Editing, Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HKUDS/VideoAgent)
- **Summary**: 针对现有视频编辑系统无法处理长视频和多任务的问题，提出VideoAgent一体化智能体框架，通过镜头规划智能体和跨模态检索实现镜头创建，并利用多智能体编排超过30个专业编辑智能体。在VideoEdit基准上编排成功率87-95%，API成本降低60%，人工评估接近人类水平。

<h3 id="534">[534] The Watermark Shortcut: How Provenance Marking Sabotages Audio Deepfake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicolas M. Müller, Pascal Debus
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Watermarking, Spurious Correlation
- **Summary**: 发现语音水印会导致音频深度伪造检测器过度依赖“水印=\>伪造”的捷径，造成泛化下降、逃避和误报三重失效。实验显示，水印训练后标记真实语音使等错误率从16%升至75%。通过在两类数据上都添加水印可修复该问题。

<h3 id="535">[535] Field-level weak lensing cosmology with $<100$ simulations using multifidelity simulation-based inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23346) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alex A. Saoulis, Kiyam Lin, Niall Jeffrey, Maximilian von Wietersheim-Kramsta, Davide Piras, Alessio Spurio Mancini, Ana M. G. Ferreira, Benjamin Joachimi
- **Subjects**: astro-ph.CO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Fidelity Learning, Simulation-Based Inference, Cosmology
- **Summary**: 针对场级弱引力透镜宇宙学推断需要大量计算昂贵的高保真模拟问题，提出多保真模拟推断方法：先用快速对数正态模拟预训练，再用少量N体模拟微调。在KiDS-Legacy模拟中，仅用60-100个高保真模拟即可获得良好校准的后验，将模拟成本降低一个数量级。

<h3 id="536">[536] Adaptive Hard-Soft Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Robust Boundary-Constrained PDE Solving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23359) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Duc Tien Nguyen, Trinh Minh Tuan, Nguyen Duc Manh, Vu Linh Nguyen, Dinh Gia Ninh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, PDE Solving
- **Summary**: 针对传统PINN收敛慢、边界条件不准确的问题，提出自适应硬-软物理信息神经网络（HSPINN），通过解析提升精确施加Dirichlet和周期边界条件，其余项设为软约束，并用逆共享softmax动态平衡损失权重。在泊松、Burgers等方程上实现更快收敛、更高精度和稳定性。

<h3 id="537">[537] Rethinking Molecular Graph Backdoors under Chemistry-aware Admission</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23361](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23361) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thinh T. H. Nguyen, Sze Jue Yang, Khoa D. Doan, Chee Seng Chan, Kok-Seng Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Graph Neural Network, Molecular Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 指出分子图后门攻击忽略实际学习管道中的化学有效性检查（ChemGuard），并形式化了该检查协议。提出ChemBack攻击，构造化学可行的基序-锚点附件，通过Tanimoto相似度排序候选。实验表明ChemBack能绕过化学检查实现高攻击成功率，揭示了化学后门持续存在威胁。

<h3 id="538">[538] Measuring & Mitigating Over-Alignment for LLMs in Multilingual Criminal Law Courts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23375](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23375) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arthur Wuhrmann, Gaetan Stein, Daniel Brunner, Andrei Kucharavy
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Legal AI, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多语言刑法场景中LLM过度对齐导致拒绝合法任务的问题，提出TF-RefusalBench基准，包含5200条多语言提示。评估发现过度对齐是多方面现象，而abliteration（消除拒绝方向）能在最小化任务性能损失的同时消除拒绝。

<h3 id="539">[539] Energy-Based Transformers as Predictors of Reading Difficulty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23382) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jakub Dotlacil, Ece Takmaz
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Energy-Based Model, Cognitive Science, Psycholinguistics
- **Summary**: 探索能量基Transformer在阅读难度预测中的应用，发现能量度量是阅读时间的稳健预测因子，能吸收注意熵和惊奇度的影响，并在关系从句处理中捕捉主/宾语不对称。为认知建模提供了统一的预测指标。

<h3 id="540">[540] Distribution-Aware Diffusion-LLM for Robust Ultra-Long-Term Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23391) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Falguni Ghosh, Vahid Hashemi, Bernhard Kainz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Diffusion Model, LLM Application
- **Venue**: ICANN 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM用于时间序列预测时缺乏概率校准和多模态对齐问题，提出Diffusion-LLM，将条件扩散模型集成到LLM框架中，学习未来数据的条件分布并增强语义对齐。在六个长期预测基准上显著优于现有LLM方法，尤其在超长期和少样本场景中。

<h3 id="541">[541] Automated Semantic Fault Localization in SysML v2: A Human-in-the-Loop Framework Using Knowledge-Graph Augmented LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23395) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haitham Al-Shami, Rohail Malik, Riku Ala-Laurinaho, Jari Vepsäläinen, Raine Viitala
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Fine-Tuning, Software Engineering, Model-Based Systems Engineering
- **Venue**: INCOSE International Symposium 2026
- **Summary**: 针对SysML v2模型中语义错误无法被编译器检测的问题，提出人机协作框架，结合微调小语言模型与领域知识图谱，生成合成训练数据并指导修复建议。在1184个测试样本上，语义错误修复率从\<3%提升至\>91%，补丁输出减少token长度60%。

<h3 id="542">[542] ReasoningLens: Hierarchical Visualization and Diagnostic Auditing for Large Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23404) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun Zhang, Jiasheng Zheng, Boxi Cao, Yaojie Lu, Hongyu Lin, Jia Zheng, Xianpei Han, Le Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Interpretability, Data Visualization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/icip-cas/ReasoningLens)
- **Summary**: 针对大推理模型长思维链中关键逻辑被淹没的问题，提出ReasoningLens开源框架，通过层次化交互式可视化、智能体审计自动错误检测和系统推理画像，将非结构化文本转化为可操作洞察，支持推理过程的解释、调试和优化。

<h3 id="543">[543] HyperQuant: A Rate-Distortion-Optimal Quantization Pipeline for Large Language and Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23406](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23406) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuval Domb, Hadar Sackstein, Tomer Solberg
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, LLM Inference, KV Cache
- **Summary**: 提出HyperQuant量化流水线，结合随机Hadamard变换、最优格量化、Rice编码和偏差校正，实现LLM和扩散模型权重与KV缓存的高效量化。在H100上以4bps压缩线性权重约3.9x、KV缓存约3.79x，性能接近无损，并成功量化19B参数DiT视频模型。

<h3 id="544">[544] UnBias-Plus: Detect, Explain, and Rewrite Bias</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23412](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23412) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Y. Radwan, Ahmed ElKady, Sindhuja Chaduvula, Mohamed Hafez, Amrit Krishnan, Shaina Raza
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Bias Detection, Explainable AI, Fairness
- **Summary**: 针对自然语言中偏见检测缺乏细粒度定位、解释和中性改写工具的问题，提出UnBias-Plus开源工具包，支持段落级多类偏见分类、偏见跨度定位、中性改写和决策推理。提供Python、CLI、REST API和Web接口，便于访问。

<h3 id="545">[545] Detecting Malicious Agent Skills in the Wild using Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23416) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bacem Etteib, Daniele Lunghi, Tégawendé F. Bissyandé
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security
- **Summary**: LLM智能体加载第三方技能时面临恶意技能威胁，现有提示注入防御失效。提出Locate-and-Judge两阶段检测器，利用注意力机制定位高关注度跨度并由法官模块详细检查。相比LLM扫描，成本降低一个数量级，高精度检测数十个恶意技能，生成标注数据集。

<h3 id="546">[546] GRINQH: Graded Input-based Quantization Hierarchy for Efficient LLM Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23419) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jette Oberländer, Jan Finkbeiner, Catherine M. Schöfmann, Emre Neftci
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Quantization, Model Compression, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: LLM自回归解码受内存带宽限制，现有量化方法未区分预填充与解码阶段的不对称性。提出GRINQH，基于激活幅度动态分配权重通道到不同精度级别，实现灵活的位宽。在3/4位设置下优于现有基线，实现有效2位生成，建立新的帕累托前沿。

<h3 id="547">[547] Rethinking Object-Centric Representations for Video Dynamics Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23436) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Amaury Wei, Ismail Nejjar, Olga Fink
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Object-Centric Learning, Video Understanding, Unsupervised Learning
- **Summary**: 基于槽的无监督视频目标跟踪中，外观和位姿纠缠导致槽锁定在静态区域。提出STAITUS，将槽解耦为外观和几何位姿，强制帧内空间分离并仅在外观空间进行时间对齐，自适应门控调整槽数量。在合成和真实基准上显著优于现有方法，分割质量和跟踪稳定性提升。

<h3 id="548">[548] What Does a Chemical Language Model Know About Molecules?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23443](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23443) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christian Kenneth, Etowah Adams, Liam Bai, Gerard JP van Westen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Interpretability, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 化学语言模型被认为仅学习语法模式而非分子语义。对MolFormer应用稀疏自编码器，逐层分析表示。早期层跟踪位置，后期层编码原子-子结构和药理学特征；非规范SMILES导致较大表示偏移。

<h3 id="549">[549] War in the Abstract: The Rise and Consequences of Militarized Language in Scientific Communication</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23462) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sovesh Mohapatra, David Lydon-Staley, Dani S. Bassett
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.DL
- **Tags**: Computational Social Science, Content Analysis, Survey Research
- **Summary**: 量化科学摘要中战争语言的使用及其影响。分析2140万篇论文并开展实验。2010-2025年间战争术语出现频率上升48%（OpenAlex）和32%（PubMed）；战争框架降低了可信度、资助意愿和政策支持。

<h3 id="550">[550] DVL-DeepONet: A Physics-Guided Operator Learning for Resilient Underwater Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23502) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arup Kumar Sahoo, Itzik Klein
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Autonomous Systems, Sensor Fusion
- **Summary**: AUV在多波束缺失或噪声下速度估计困难，低成本平台无惯性传感器。提出DVL-DeepONet，物理引导的神经算子框架，利用一致性约束学习速度估计。在真实AUV实验中比基线方法提升40%。

<h3 id="551">[551] SQLConductor: Search-to-Policy Learning for Step-wise Text-to-SQL Orchestration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23537](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23537) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yizhang Zhu, Zhangyang Peng, Boyan Li, Yuyu Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, Reinforcement Learning, Program Synthesis
- **Summary**: Text-to-SQL系统固定流水线缺乏自适应性，编排方法在执行业前未利用中间反馈。提出SQLConductor，将子任务建模为动作，通过MCTS搜索策略并采用稳定性加权SFT和课程强化学习训练。在BIRD-Dev上达到73.2%执行准确率，优于训练更大骨干网络的方法。

<h3 id="552">[552] The Energy Consumption of Transformer Fine-Tuning: A Roofline-Inspired Scaling Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23546](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23546) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mansour Zoubeirou a Mayaki
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.AR; cs.CL; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Energy Efficiency, LLM Training, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: Transformer训练能耗快速增长，需要精确预测。提出基于roofline模型的能耗建模框架，包含张量并行和FSDP的硬件效率因子。推导出缩放律，可准确预测异构配置下的训练能耗。

<h3 id="553">[553] Scheduling Thoughts: Learning the Order of Thought in Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23567) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Xu, Minghui Liu, Aakriti Agrawal, Yifan Chen, Furong Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Language Modeling, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 掩码扩散语言模型解码顺序启发式选择，影响生成质量。推导序列解码不匹配的上界，提出SAS方法，利用GRPO学习顺序策略。数独准确率从82.0%提升至91.8%（微调后97.5%）；GSM8K pass@1从64%提升至76%。

<h3 id="554">[554] Solve for the Hyperparameter, Skip the Search: Kolmogorov-Optimal Scaling Laws for Spline Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23575](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23575) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yong Yi Bay, Kathleen A. Yearick
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.NA; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Hyperparameter Optimization, Tabular Learning, Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bay-yearick-lab/kore)
- **Summary**: 样条回归超参数调优需要网格搜索，计算成本高。利用近似理论推导最优分辨率闭式解，通过KORE算法实现。以约8倍少模型拟合匹配全交叉验证；在36个表格数据集上排名第一。

<h3 id="555">[555] Kamera: Unified Position-Invariant Multimodal KV Cache for Training-Free Reuse</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23581](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23581) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bole Ma, Jan Eitzinger, Harald Koestler, Gerhard Wellein
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: KV Cache, Multimodal Learning, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 多模态智能体重复编码相同帧，前缀缓存仅支持固定位置复用，跨块绑定丢失。提出位置无关的KV缓存，附加低秩条件补丁恢复跨块绑定。rank-m补丁恢复完整任务准确率，减少KV内存开销，在SGLang内核中重建至bf16精度。

<h3 id="556">[556] Decentralized Autonomous Traffic Management through Corridor Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23585](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23585) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jasmine Jerry Aloor, Aadarsh Govada, Hamsa Balakrishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.RO; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Autonomous Systems, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: ATRDS2026
- **Summary**: 高密度自主飞行器集中管理不足，需分散方法。扩展多智能体强化学习方法至走廊网络，零样本迁移。策略良好迁移至不同密度、几何和异构性能，保持系统性能。

<h3 id="557">[557] A Generative Model for Closed-Loop Microsimulation of Signalized Intersections</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23588](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23588) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yash Ranjan, Rahul Sengupta, Anand Rangarajan, Sanjay Ranka
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Simulation, Behavior Prediction, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 交通微观仿真使用手工模型忽略交互，学习模型闭环不稳定。提出Enactor，以智能体为中心的生成模型，使用极坐标和双注意力Transformer，闭环课程训练。在速度和时间分布上KL散度比基线低一个数量级；违反红灯减少一个数量级。

<h3 id="558">[558] Polycepta: Object-Centric Appearance Estimation for Multi-Object Tracking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23604](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23604) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohamed Nagy, Naoufel Werghi, Jorge Dias, Majid Khonji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Object Tracking, Computer Vision, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 多目标跟踪中外观描述符帧独立，实时系统常放弃外观线索。Polycepta将外观建模为递归估计问题，为每个目标独立更新外观状态。减少身份切换，在KITTI上达到92.27% MOTA，运行频率90.57 Hz。

<h3 id="559">[559] Scaling Linear Mode Connectivity and Merging to Billion Parameter Pretrained Transformers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23607) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Li, Zhiqiang Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Foundation Model, Vision Transformer
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VILA-Lab/Dual-Learned-Matching)
- **Summary**: 现有线性模式连通性方法仅从单端点优化，不适用于十亿参数模型。提出双学习方法，两个模型共同学习向共享线性插值路径的转换。在WikiText上实现近零损失屏障；ViT-L沿插值路径保持\>69% ImageNet top-1准确率。

<h3 id="560">[560] Discovering Latent Groups for Robust Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ankur Garg, Ulrich Aïvodji, Samira Ebrahimi Kahou, Vincent Michalski
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Interpretability, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 机器学习模型利用虚假相关性，在少数子群上表现差。提出神经分类树，在树结构中编码子群结构，通过路由样本至易/难节点并使用路径作为伪标签。树拓扑具有强可解释性，隔离少数子群，与最先进方法竞争力相当。

<h3 id="561">[561] Data Selection Through Iterative Self-Filtering for Vision-Language Settings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23611](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23611) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrei Liviu Nicolicioiu, Sarvjeet Singh Ghotra, Morgane M. Moss, Aaron Courville
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Data Curation
- **Summary**: 论文针对大规模视觉-语言数据集噪声大、人工难以监督的问题，提出一种自举的自过滤方法（Self-Filtering），通过迭代训练CLIP模型并基于模型选择平衡干净样本与多样样本的数据混合。该方法无需额外数据或预训练模型，即可提升下游任务性能。

<h3 id="562">[562] RECALL: Recovery Experience Collection for Active Lifelong Learning in Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23617) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ulas Berk Karli, Tesca Fitzgerald
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Active Learning, Catastrophic Forgetting
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）被动模仿学习效率低的问题，提出主动持续学习范式RECALL，通过不确定性驱动的恢复数据收集和重放式数据混合与弹性权重巩固来平衡塑性记忆。实验表明主动数据收集更高效，但仅用恢复数据微调会导致灾难性遗忘，需要权衡学习与保留。

<h3 id="563">[563] DiT-Reward: Generative Representations for Text-to-Image Reward Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23626) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanming Yang, Guoqing Ma, Bo Wang, Yuan Zhang, Wei Tang, Chenyi Li, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Reward Model
- **Summary**: 为利用生成式表示评估生成图像质量，提出DiT-Reward模型，将预训练文本到图像扩散Transformer转换为奖励模型，通过处理近干净潜变量并聚合跨层表示。在相同训练数据下，DiT-Reward在HPDv2和HPDv3上分别达到85.6%和77.6%的准确率，优于HPSv3。

<h3 id="564">[564] Learning Process Rewards via Success Visitation Matching for Efficient RL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23640) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raymond Tsao, Andrew Wagenmaker, Sergey Levine
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Reward Shaping
- **Summary**: 针对强化学习稀疏奖励导致信用分配困难的问题，提出通过学习判别器区分成功/失败轨迹，并利用该判别器激励策略匹配成功轨迹的状态-动作访问，从而提供密集过程奖励。该方法在不改变最优策略的前提下，显著加速机器人操作任务的微调。

<h3 id="565">[565] Tapered Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23670) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Reza Bayat, Ali Behrouz, Aaron Courville
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Language Model, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对语言模型各层参数均匀分配但贡献不均的问题，提出锥形语言模型（TLM），在固定预算下按余弦调度单调递减MLP宽度。在三种规模、四种架构上，TLM均优于均匀宽度基线，无额外参数或计算开销，提升困惑度和下游任务性能。

<h3 id="566">[566] Open Problem: Is AdamW Effective Under Heavy-Tailed Noise?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23676](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23676) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dingzhi Yu, Hongyi Tao, Yuanyu Wan, Luo Luo, Lijun Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Optimization, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 针对AdamW在重尾噪声下缺乏收敛理论的问题，将其表述为开放问题，并证明了加权度量基准和下限机制。研究指出符号优化器（如Lion、Muon）在重尾噪声下达到锐利速率，但AdamW的理论仍缺失。

<h3 id="567">[567] AIR: Adaptive Interleaved Reasoning with Code in MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23678) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cong Han, Xiaohan Lan, Haibo Qiu, Yujie Zhong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal LLM, Code Generation, Tool Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/CongHan0808/AIR.git)
- **Summary**: 为增强多模态大语言模型在数值计算任务中的代码交叠推理能力，提出包含冷启动数据构建、数据过滤和基于分组约束奖励的自适应工具调用策略的解决方案。强化学习训练后，评估基准平均提升6.1个百分点，交叠推理准确率提升9.9个百分点，工具调用成功率超过95%。

<h3 id="568">[568] Semantic Browsing: Controllable Diversity for Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23679) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sara Dorfman, Maya Vishnevsky, Omer Dahary, Or Patashnik, Daniel Cohen-Or
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Controllable Generation, Output Diversity
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像生成多样性不足且缺乏结构的问题，提出语义浏览方法，在文本层面利用视觉语言模型和智能体工作流诱导受控的语义变化，而非依赖随机噪声。该方法生成结构化的、可导航的图像画廊，每种变化对应用户可理解的语义决策。

<h3 id="569">[569] CoorDex: Coordinating Body and Hand Priors for Continuous Dexterous Humanoid Loco-Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.23680](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23680) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sikai Li, Shuning Li, Zhenyu Wei, Yunchao Yao, Chenran Li, Mingyu Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Humanoid Robotics, Dexterous Manipulation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对人形机器人操作中断、末端执行器自由度低的问题，提出CoorDex学习管线，将高维身体和灵巧手控制转化为协调的潜在残差控制，通过特权运动跟踪教师、蒸馏和残差强化学习实现。在Unitree G1人形机器人上实现了运动中灵巧操作（如抓取瓶子、开门）。

<h3 id="570">[570] A criterion for Artificial General Intelligence: hypothetic-deductive reasoning, tested on ChatGPT</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2308.02950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2308.02950) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Louis Vervoort, Vitaliy Mizyakov, Anastasia Ugleva
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: General Intelligence, AI Evaluation, Causal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 指出假设-演绎推理是通用人工智能的关键能力，并设计了因果推理测试。对ChatGPT的测试表明，其在复杂问题上假设-演绎和因果推理能力有限。若AI能在广泛上下文中掌握该推理，则可视为AGI。

<h3 id="571">[571] Emergent Communication in Continuous Worlds: Self-Organisation of Conceptually Grounded Vocabularies at Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2401.08461](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08461) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jérôme Botoko Ekila, Lara Verheyen, Jens Nevens, Katrien Beuls, Paul Van Eecke
