- **Venue**: TACL
- **Summary**: 针对自主智能体如何自组织语言约定以指代连续环境实体的问题，提出一种去中心化方法，通过局部交互形成符号标签与概念表示的关联。在37个数据集上验证了方法的通用性和可扩展性，对感知变化鲁棒且能自适应环境变化。

<h3 id="572">[572] A Neural Operator-Based Approach to Symbolic Discovery of PDEs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2501.08086](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.08086) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sergei Garmaev, Olga Fink
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SC
- **Tags**: Neural Operator, Symbolic Regression, PDE Solving
- **Summary**: 针对包含非局部算子、场耦合和记忆效应的偏微分方程发现困难，提出NOMTO框架，将预训练神经算子作为符号网络中的节点，结合代数运算形成可微计算图。该方法能恢复包含非局部算子项的紧凑方程，扩展了符号发现的库容。

<h3 id="573">[573] EquivPruner: Boosting Efficiency and Quality in LLM-Based Search via Action Pruning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.16312](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16312) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Liu, Qisi Chen, Jianshu Zhang, Quan Liu, Defu Lian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Token Pruning, Inference Optimization
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Lolo1222/EquivPruner)
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型搜索策略因探索语义等价步骤导致大量token消耗的问题，提出EquivPruner方法，通过轻量等价检测器识别并修剪语义等价动作。在GSM8K上token消耗减少48.1%，同时提升推理准确率。

<h3 id="574">[574] AgentMisalignment: Measuring the Propensity for Misaligned Behaviour in LLM-Based Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.04018](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.04018) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Akshat Naik, Emma Gouné, Patrick Quinn, Guillermo Bosch, Francisco Javier Campos Zabala, Jason Ross Brown, Edward James Young
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, LLM Alignment
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型智能体在实际部署中自发追求未预期目标的风险，提出AgentMisalignment基准测试，评估规避监督、抗拒关机、装傻和寻求权力等行为。结果表明，能力更强的智能体平均表现出更高的错位倾向，且人格特征显著影响错位程度。

<h3 id="575">[575] United Minds or Isolated Agents? Exploring Coordination of LLMs under Cognitive Load Theory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.06843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.06843) (replaced)
- **Authors**: HaoYang Shang, Xuan Liu, Zi Liang, Jie Zhang, Haibo Hu, Song Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Cognitive Load Theory
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型在复杂任务上因上下文工程过重导致性能瓶颈的问题，借鉴认知负荷理论，提出CoThinker多智能体框架，通过智能体专业化和集体工作记忆分配认知负荷。实验表明，在推理密集型任务上收益显著，而低内在负荷任务上协调开销占主导。

<h3 id="576">[576] TIP-Search: Time-Predictable Inference Scheduling for Market Prediction under Uncertain Load</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.08026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.08026) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xibai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY; q-fin.CP
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Financial AI, Scheduling
- **Summary**: 针对实时市场预测需在截止时间前给出正确预测的问题，提出TIP-Search时间可预测推理调度方法，包括分形延迟分位数过滤、有限工作者调度和带约束在线专家选择。在TLOB FI-2010 h=10上，及时准确率从0.156提升至0.239，截止时间满足率从0.391提升至0.962。

<h3 id="577">[577] Illuminating the Three Dogmas of Reinforcement Learning under Evolutionary Light</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.11482](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.11482) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mani Hamidi, Terrence W. Deacon
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Open-Ended Evolution, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文指出强化学习的三大核心原则（MDP、策略优化、标量奖励）受到质疑，并缺乏对智能体的形式化理论。通过引入开放结局新奇性搜索和热力学起源理论，提出一种更符合生物学的适应性智能体模型。本文为概念性工作，无定量结果。

<h3 id="578">[578] Reinforcement Learning for Long-Horizon Unordered Tasks: From Boolean to Coupled Reward Machines</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.27329](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.27329) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kristina Levina, Nikolaos Pappas, Athanasios Karapantelakis, Aneta Vulgarakis Feljan, Jendrik Seipp
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Long-Horizon Planning, Reward Machine
- **Venue**: ICAPS 2026 PRL Workshop
- **Summary**: 奖励机器（RM）在处理长时域无序子任务时面临信息指数增长的问题。本文提出数值型RM、议程RM和耦合RM三种泛化形式，并设计基于Q学习的QCoRM算法。实验表明QCoRM在四个领域（离散和连续状态空间）上比基线算法具有更好的扩展性。

<h3 id="579">[579] MedBayes-Lite: A Clinical Uncertainty Governance Layer for Risk-Aware Medical Decision Support</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.16625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16625) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Elias Hossain, Md Mehedi Hasan Nipu, Maleeha Sheikh, Tasfia Nuzhat, Rajib Rana, Subash Neupane, Björn W. Schuller, Niloofar Yousefi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Medical AI, Model Calibration
- **Summary**: 临床语言模型常对错误预测赋予高置信度，尤其在严重和分布外病例中。MedBayes-Lite结合蒙特卡洛dropout、校准和置信度引导的弃权机制，无需额外参数。在MedMCQA和MedQA-USMLE上，期望校准误差降低0.23-0.33，有害过度自信错误近乎为零；领域迁移下严重错误从约21%降至近零。

<h3 id="580">[580] NOEM$^{3}$A: a Neuro-symbolic Ontology-Enhanced Method for Multi-intent understanding in Mobile Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.19780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19780) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ioannis Tzachristas, Aifen Sui
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Natural Language Understanding, On-Device Learning
- **Summary**: 移动智能体需在低延迟和隐私约束下将自然语言映射为可执行意图，直接扩展语言模型效率低下。NOEM³A为轻量级神经符号层，通过意图本体检索和令牌级解码先验增强小模型。在MultiWOZ 2.3上，TinyLlama和Llama-3.2-3B的精确匹配和Slot-F1均有提升。

<h3 id="581">[581] Pessimistic Verification for Open Ended Math Questions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.21522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21522) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanxing Huang, Zihan Tang, Zejin Lin, Peng Li, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Self-Verification, Chain-of-Thought
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/THUNLP-MT/pverify)
- **Summary**: 数学求解智能体的自动验证在泛化性、性能和成本上存在瓶颈。提出悲观验证（多个验证器结果否决）和渐进悲观验证（细粒度证明分解）。在IMO 2025等数据集上，该方法在性能和令牌效率上超越长思维链及主流验证流程。

<h3 id="582">[582] Sparse Neuron Ablation Triggers Catastrophic Collapse of the Language Core in Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.00918](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00918) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cen Lu, Yung-Chen Tang, Andrea Cavallaro
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Mechanistic Interpretability Workshop
- **Summary**: 大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）的功能性结构尚不清楚。提出一致激活神经元（CAN）方法进行渐进神经元消融。实验表明，消融仅4个神经元即可导致LLaVA-1.5-7b灾难性崩溃，关键神经元集中在语言模型的下投影层，并呈现两阶段崩溃模式。

<h3 id="583">[583] Enhancing Diversity of LLM-Generated Educational Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.23601](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.23601) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Manh Hung Nguyen, Sebastian Tschiatschek, Adish Singla
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Prompt Engineering, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 大语言模型生成的教育内容存在同质性问题。基于发散-收敛思维阶段提出CreativeDC提示框架。在Python编程任务中，生成的高效用任务数量约为基线的1.6倍。

<h3 id="584">[584] StackPlanner: A Centralized Hierarchical Multi-Agent System with Task-Experience Memory Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.05890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05890) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruizhe Zhang, Xinke Jiang, Zhibang Yang, Zhixin Zhang, Jiaran Gao, Yuzhen Xiao, Tao Feng, Yue Fang, Yuxuan Liu, Ruiqing Li, Hongbin Lai, Huheng Huang, Xu Chu, Junfeng Zhao, Yasha Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Agent Memory, Hierarchical RL
- **Summary**: 集中式多智能体系统因缺乏记忆管理导致长期协作不稳定。StackPlanner采用层次化框架，解耦高层协调与子任务执行，并通过强化学习复用结构化经验。在深度搜索和智能体系统基准上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="585">[585] Anytime Safe PAC Efficient Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22446](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22446) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chengyao Yu, Hao Zeng, Youxin Zhu, Jianguo Huang, Huajun Zeng, Bingyi Jing
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Safe RL, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 大型推理模型计算成本高，选择性思考策略在在线场景中误差不可控。提出打赌PAC（B-PAC）推理，利用逆概率评分构造测试超鞅动态调整路由阈值。思维模型使用率降低高达81.01%，性能损失控制在用户指定水平以下。

<h3 id="586">[586] Learning More from Less: Unlocking Internal Representations for Benchmark Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00710](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00710) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yueqi Zhang, Jin Hu, Shaoxiong Feng, Peiwen Yuan, Xinglin Wang, Yiwei Li, Jiayi Shi, Chuyi Tan, Ji Zhang, Boyuan Pan, Yao Hu, Kan Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Model Evaluation, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 现有基准压缩方法依赖多个源模型的响应模式，源池小时统计不稳定。RepCore将异构隐藏状态对齐到统一潜在空间构建代表性核心集。仅用10个源模型即可实现精确估计，在5个基准和200+模型上优于基于输出的基线。

<h3 id="587">[587] AIR: Improving Agent Safety through Incident Response</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11749](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11749) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zibo Xiao, Jun Sun, Junjie Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, LLM Agent, Guardrail
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 大语言模型智能体的安全机制主要聚焦预防，缺乏事故响应能力。AIR框架定义领域特定语言，在智能体执行循环中集成检测、遏制、恢复和消除。检测、修复和消除成功率均超过90%。

<h3 id="588">[588] Agent Skill Framework: Perspectives on the Potential of Small to Medium Language Models in Industrial Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16653](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16653) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yangjie Xu, Lujun Li, Lama Sleem, Niccolo Gentile, Yewei Song, Yiqun Wang, Siming Ji, Wenbo Wu, Radu State
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Industrial AI, Small Language Model
- **Summary**: 智能体技能在开源小模型上的有效性未知。系统研究了270M-80B模型在工业环境中的技能范式。非常小的模型技能选择困难，30B-80B模型受益明显；思考变体提升有限且增加GPU开销。

<h3 id="589">[589] LemmaBench: A Live, Research-Level Benchmark to Evaluate LLM Capabilities in Mathematics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.24173](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.24173) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Antoine Peyronnet, Fabian Gloeckle, Amaury Hayat
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Mathematical Reasoning, Automated Theorem Proving
- **Venue**: 3rd AI for Math Workshop (AI4Math) at ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有数学基准多为静态竞赛题，无法评估研究级数学能力。LemmaBench从arXiv自动提取引理并改写为自包含语句，形成可更新的基准。当前最强LLM在定理证明上仅达10-15%准确率（pass@1）。

<h3 id="590">[590] DeepResearch-9K: A Challenging Benchmark Dataset of Deep-Research Agent</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.01152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.01152) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tongzhou Wu, Yuhao Wang, Xinyu Ma, Xiuqiang He, Shuaiqiang Wang, Dawei Yin, Xiangyu Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Web Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Applied-Machine-Learning-Lab/DeepResearch-R1)
- **Summary**: 深度研究智能体缺乏大规模挑战性数据集和开源训练框架。构建DeepResearch-9K（9000问题，三个难度级别，搜索轨迹）和DeepResearch-R1训练框架（支持多轮交互和强化学习）。训练后的智能体在深度研究基准上达到最优。

<h3 id="591">[591] Retrievit: In-context Retrieval Capabilities of Transformers, State Space Models, and Hybrid Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02874) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Georgios Pantazopoulos, Malvina Nikandrou, Ioannis Konstas, Alessandro Suglia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Transformer Architecture, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: Transformer在上下文检索中表现优异但复杂度高，状态空间模型（SSM）高效但检索能力有限。研究混合架构在n-gram检索和位置检索任务上的表现。混合模型在数据效率和泛化上匹配或超越Transformer，但Transformer在位置检索上仍具优势；SSM形成位置感知嵌入。

<h3 id="592">[592] Two-Bridge: Exclusive Objectives and Extended Horizon StarCraft II Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.06608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06608) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sourav Panda, Tanmay Ambadkar, Shreyash Kale, Abhinav Verma, Jonathan Dodge
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Game AI, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 星际争霸II完整游戏与迷你游戏之间存在复杂度鸿沟，阻碍RL研究。Two-Bridge地图套件作为中间基准，禁用经济机制，聚焦远程导航和微观战斗。智能体学会了连贯的机动和交战行为，且计算成本合理。

<h3 id="593">[593] RetailBench: Evaluating Long-Horizon Autonomous Decision-Making and Strategy Stability of LLM Agents in Realistic Retail Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.16453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16453) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Linghua Zhang, Jun Wang, Jingtong Wu, Zhisong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 针对LLM智能体在动态长周期环境中难以维持连贯决策的问题，提出了RetailBench基准，将零售管理建模为部分可观测决策过程并支持千天模拟。评估7种LLM在180天内的表现显示，仅少数模型能存活，最强模型在净资产和销售上远落后于最优策略，原因包括证据获取不足、表面决策和缺乏一致长周期策略。

<h3 id="594">[594] An AI Co-Data-Scientist for Prioritizing Candidate Biomarkers from Wearable Sensor Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.14615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14615) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yubin Kim, Salman Rahman, Samuel Schmidgall, Chunjong Park, A. Ali Heydari, Ahmed A. Metwally, Hong Yu, Xin Liu, Xuhai Xu, Yuzhe Yang, Hyeonhoon Lee, Hyewon Jeong, Kyungho Lim, MingYu Lu, Dongjae Lee, Theodora Pappa, Hanseul Cho, Maxwell A. Xu, Zhihan Zhang, Cynthia Breazeal, Tim Althoff, Petar Sirkovic, Ivor Rendulic, Annalisa Pawlosky, Nicolas Stroppa, Juraj Gottweis, Elahe Vedadi, Alan Karthikesalingam, Pushmeet Kohli, Mark Malhotra, Shwetak Patel, Samir Tulebaev, Hae Won Park, Vivek Natarajan, Hamid Palangi, Daniel McDuff
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Health Monitoring, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对可穿戴设备数据转化为临床可用生物标志物假设的劳动密集型问题，提出了AI合作数据科学家CoDaS，集成多智能体假设生成、确定性统计分析和对抗验证。在三个队列中识别出与抑郁相关的昼夜节律不稳定信号（如睡眠时长变异性ρ=0.252）和与胰岛素抵抗相关的心血管健康指数（ρ=-0.374），且在临床医生评审中验证了其置信度与医生判断的一致性（ρ=0.67）。

<h3 id="595">[595] Autogenesis: A Self-Evolving Agent Protocol</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15034) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wentao Zhang, Zhe Zhao, Haibin Wen, Yingcheng Wu, Cankun Guo, Ming Yin, Bo An
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Software Engineering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DVampire/Autogenesis)
- **Summary**: 针对现有智能体协议在跨实体生命周期管理、版本追踪和演化更新方面的不足，提出了自演化协议AGP，将演化内容与方式解耦，并通过资源基板协议层和自演化协议层实现闭环改进。基于AGP构建的自演化多智能体系统在长周期规划等基准上持续超越强基线。

<h3 id="596">[596] Persuadability and LLMs as Legal Decision Tools</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26233) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Oisin Suttle, David Lillis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Legal AI, AI Ethics, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICAIL 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM作为法律决策辅助工具的可说服性问题，通过实验研究了不同质量的辩护律师论点对前沿LLM判决倾向的影响，结果表明模型易受辩护技巧影响，这对LLM在法律领域的应用具有重要启示。

<h3 id="597">[597] Verifiable Counterfactual Supervision for Process Reward Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02395) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yinghui Chi, Yuanhong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reward Model, LLM Reasoning, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 针对过程奖励模型需要精确定位首个推理错误监督的问题，提出了可验证反事实过程监督方法，通过从已验证符号链中注入模板化错误并重算后续步骤，生成首个错误标注数据。该方法在逻辑推理基准上提升了Best-of-8重排序性能，并初步迁移至数学过程评估。

<h3 id="598">[598] Positive Alignment: Artificial Intelligence for Human Flourishing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.10310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10310) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruben Laukkonen, Seb Krier, Chloé Bakalar, Shamil Chandaria, Morten Kringelbach, Adam Elwood, Daniel Ford, Fernando Rosas, Maty Bohacek, Matija Franklin, Nenad Tomašev, Stephanie Chan, Verena Rieser, Roma Patel, Michael Levin, Arun Rao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.HC; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 针对现有对齐研究过于关注安全与伤害预防的问题，提出了积极对齐概念，旨在开发主动支持人类和生态繁荣的AI系统。文章讨论了技术方向（如数据过滤、预训练与后训练、协作价值收集）和设计原则（情境化、社区定制、多中心治理）。

<h3 id="599">[599] Distribution-Aware Algorithm Design with LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14141) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Saharsh Koganti, Priyadarsi Mishra, Pierfrancesco Beneventano, Tomer Galanti
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, LLM Agent, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 针对固定未知分布上的重复优化问题，提出了分布感知算法设计框架，利用LLM智能体从样本中推断分布结构并编译为求解器代码。在21个组合优化分布上，合成的求解器达到了0.971的归一化质量，运行速度比经典启发式和Gurobi快数个数量级，且在PACE 2025支配集私有实例上比竞赛求解器快75-125倍。

<h3 id="600">[600] MBABench: Evaluating LLM Agents on End-to-End Spreadsheet Tasks in Finance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22664) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomson Yen, Julian Poeltl, Harshith Srinivas Gear, Yilin Meng, Joshua Fan, Adam Shen, Yili Liu, Ali Bauyrzhan, Siri Du, Haoyang Liu, Daniel Guetta, Hongseok Namkoong
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Finance AI
- **Summary**: 针对现有电子表格基准仅聚焦问答或单公式编辑的不足，提出了MBABench，评估LLM智能体在金融场景中从零构建完整电子表格的能力。采用准确性、公式和格式三维评价，结果表明最先进的Claude模型仍难以达到专业金融标准，且随难度增加表现急剧下降。

<h3 id="601">[601] From XXLTraffic to EvoXXLTraffic: Scaling Traffic Forecasting to Sensor-Evolving Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.29768](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29768) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Du Yin, Hao Xue, Arian Prabowo, Shuang Ao, Flora Salim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Graph Neural Network, Continual Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cruiseresearchgroup/TSAS26-EvoXXLTraffic)
- **Summary**: 针对现有交通预测基准假设传感器固定不变的局限，提出了XXLTraffic数据集族（包含27年加州PeMS和新南威尔士数据）及其传感器演化版本EvoXXLTraffic，暴露了逐年活跃传感器和年流量矩阵。定义了年度流式预测协议，发现许多现有SOTA方法在该演化数据集上失效。

<h3 id="602">[602] Reasoning4Sciences: Bridging Reasoning Language Models to All Scientific Branches</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01145) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Teddy Ferdinan, Bartłomiej Koptyra, Mikołaj Langner, Tomasz Adamczyk, Łukasz Radliński, Maciej Markiewicz, Aleksander Szczęsny, Stanisław Woźniak, Tymoteusz Romanowicz, Dzmitry Pihulski, Mateusz Zbrocki, Mateusz Śmigielski, Michał Rajkowski, Mateusz Biedka, Konrad Kiełczyński, Konrad Wojtasik, Jacek Duszenko, Jan Eliasz, Piotr Matys, Michał Bernacki-Janson, Maria Bellaniar Ismiati, Latius Hermawan, Wiktoria Mieleszczenko-Kowszewicz, Anna Kubicka-Sowinska, Grzegorz Chodak, Karol Postawa, Paweł Zyblewski, Tomasz Szandała, Łukasz Sterczewski, Adrian Chajec, Pawel Niewiadomski, Piotr Gruber, Marcin Wdowikowski, Sławomir Czarnecki, Bartłomiej Kryszak, Dominik Drabik, Tomasz Kajdanowicz, Kamil Mamak, Paweł Preś, Katarzyna Paczkowska, Joachim Sobczuk, Tomasz Zięba, Jan Kocoń, Maciej Piasecki, Przemysław Kazienko
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Survey, Scientific Reasoning, AI for Science
- **Summary**: 针对推理语言模型主要集中在硬科学领域导致研究效率差距的问题，本文对28个科学分支中的RLM应用进行了全面综述，并提出了基于领域内开发与评估资源的成熟度评估框架，揭示了不同学科RLM成熟度的巨大差异，特别是在公开资源有限的情况下。

<h3 id="603">[603] SCI-PRM: A Tool Aware Process Reward Model for Scientific Reasoning Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04579](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04579) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiangyu Zhao, Henry Hengyuan Zhao, Yiheng Wang, Wanghan Xu, Yuhao Zhou, Qinglong Cao, Zhiwang Zhou, Lei Bai, Wenlong Zhang, Xiao-Ming Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reward Model, Scientific Reasoning, Tool Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 AI4Science Track
- **Summary**: 针对过程奖励模型在科学领域由于缺乏工具使用验证而不足的问题，构建了包含链条式工具轨迹的数据集SCIPRM70K，并训练了Sci-PRM模型以提供细粒度步骤级的工具选择、执行准确性和结果解读监督。实验表明Sci-PRM在最佳N选择测试时缩放和作为强化学习密集奖励信号方面有效提升了基础模型性能。

<h3 id="604">[604] A Four-Valued Normative Intermediate Representation for ASP-Oriented Compliance Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.04619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04619) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Huanyu Yang, Yangfan Wu, Jianmin Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Knowledge Representation, Formal Methods, Logic
- **Summary**: 针对技术标准合规推理中不完整证据、不一致观察和例外情况的问题，提出了面向ASP合规工作流的四值规范中间表示MONIR，将事实支持与规范输出分离。通过阶段性转换系统、依赖图诊断和策略参数化报告，证明了固定证据下的确定性多项式时间评估，并给出了NP/coNP上界和匹配的下界。

<h3 id="605">[605] Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06356) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Renjith Prasad, Chathurangi Shyalika, Anushka Pawar, Aahan Rathod, Amit Sheth
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Diffusion Model, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对多模态生成模型在需要符合结构化、领域特定或安全关键知识时不可靠的问题，提出了一个分层知识注入框架，将知识作用于表面、轨迹、潜空间和参数四个层面。在安全对齐实验中，逐步累积各层面减少了70.97%的知识违规输出。

<h3 id="606">[606] DN-Hypo-Pipeline: An AI-Driven Workflow for Generating Hypotheses using Large Language Models and Scientific Explanations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.08532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08532) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lei Lin, Ronghao Wang, Chunbao Zhou, Jue Wang, Yangang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hypothesis Generation, Scientific Discovery, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对科学假设生成耗时且需要深度理解的问题，提出了基于LLM的假设生成流水线DN-Hypo-Pipeline，通过利用科学解释作为先验知识重构新假设。在数据科学建模中评估，流水线优于直接生成，且生成的假设驱动的算法超越了原始基线。

<h3 id="607">[607] ComplexConstraints and Beyond: Expert Rubrics for RLVR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09118](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09118) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sushant Mehta, Liudas Panavas, Suhaas Garre, Edwin Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Instruction Following, RLHF, Benchmark
- **Venue**: GEM workshop at ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 针对传统基准依赖狭窄程序化验证而真实世界任务需要细微评估的问题，提出了专家策划的标尺型评估方法，并构建了ComplexConstraints数据集。训练表明，使用该数据集进行强化学习显著提升了指令跟随能力（4B模型+15.5%，235B模型+12.2%），并泛化到了未见分布基准。

<h3 id="608">[608] Anything2Skill: Compiling External Knowledge into Reusable Skills for Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09316) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qianjun Pan, Yutao Yang, Junsong Li, Jie Zhou, Kai Chen, Xin Li, Qin Chen, Liang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG, Skill Learning, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 针对RAG仅检索分散证据导致智能体需重复推断任务过程的问题，提出了Anything2Skill框架，将异构外部知识编译为可复用、可检索、可执行的技能。通过计划-扩展技能提取和结构化技能合约，结合RAG实现了在qsv和GitHub-CLI上高达98.85%和94.10%的成功率，显著超越纯RAG智能体。

<h3 id="609">[609] Collaborative Human-Agent Protocol (CHAP)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.09751](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09751) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Arsalan Shahid, Gordon Suttie, Philip Black
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Human-Agent Interaction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/BrightbeamAI/chap)
- **Summary**: 当前多人类-多智能体协作缺乏标准化协议，导致人类编辑、交接等关键信号丢失。提出了CHAP协议，通过工作空间、参与者、任务、工件和仅追加证据日志等核心组件，以及可组合的配置文件，实现结构化、可审计的协作。CHAP将人类覆盖行为转化为结构化事件，支持签名决策回放，并提供规范、参考实现和一致性套件。

<h3 id="610">[610] Learning What to Remember: Observability-Safe Memory Retention via Constrained Optimization for Long-Horizon Language Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10616) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qingcan Kang, Liu Mingyang, Shixiong Kai, Kaichao Liang, Tao Zhong, Mingxuan Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Optimization
- **Summary**: 长期语言代理的记忆保留面临资源分配问题，现有方法不考虑长期可观测性约束。提出了OSL-MR框架，将记忆保留形式化为带约束的随机优化问题，结合在线可观测特征与离线监督的严格分离。实验表明OSL-MR在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上优于基线，尤其在小预算下表现显著。

<h3 id="611">[611] Accelerating NeurASP with vectorization and caching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.10787](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10787) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexander Philipp Rader, Alessandra Russo
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Training Efficiency
- **Venue**: TPLP
- **Summary**: 神经符号AI框架NeurASP训练时需通过不可微的ASP组件反向传播，计算昂贵。通过向量化、批处理和缓存中间计算，实现了多个数量级的加速。提出了新的扑克牌任务数据集以测试增强学习能力。

<h3 id="612">[612] Learning What to Remember: A Cognitively Grounded Multi-Factor Value Model for Agentic Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.12945](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12945) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhibao Chen, Qian Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Cognitive Science
- **Summary**: 长期代理的记忆保留缺乏基于未来查询的遗忘决策模型。提出了多因素记忆价值函数，融合情感强度、目标相关性等七个可解释因素，通过无梯度优化学习权重，统一控制编码深度、遗忘风险和检索排名。实验表明学习到的多因素价值在盲测中保留0.770的证据，优于均匀权重等基线，且权重可解释。

<h3 id="613">[613] SciR: A Controllable Benchmark for Scientific Reasoning in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13020](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13020) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pierre Beckmann, Marco Valentino, Andre Freitas
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Scientific Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有科学推理基准成本高且缺乏机理真实，而合成逻辑推理基准不像真实科学文档。提出了SciR基准，结合多范式推理（演绎、归纳、因果溯因）与可控科学渲染，独立控制提取难度和推理难度。实验显示两个难度轴均影响模型性能，且推理模型在推理轴上优于非推理模型。

<h3 id="614">[614] Neuro-Symbolic Agents for Regulated Process Automation: Challenges and Research Agenda</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.13405](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13405) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexander Rombach, Chantale Lauer, Nijat Mehdiyev
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, LLM Agent, Workflow Automation
- **Venue**: NILA Workshop @ IJCAI-ECAI 2026
- **Summary**: LLM代理进入受监管行业时，符号结构应作为核心架构组件而非外部监控。提出了合规即结构作为防护栏的补充，并列出神经符号研究挑战以推动该领域。

<h3 id="615">[615] GitOfThoughts: Version-Controlled Reasoning and Agent Memory You Can Replay, Diff, and Merge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14470) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pavan C Shekar, Abhishek H S, Aswanth Krishnan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Memory Architecture, Research Reproducibility
- **Summary**: LLM推理过程不可追溯。将推理树存储为git仓库，每个想法作为提交，支持diff、合并和审计。实验发现记忆仅对几乎相同的问题有帮助，对大多数新问题无效，自我一致性才是可靠的方法。但git作为记忆存储提供了可审计性和合并能力。

<h3 id="616">[616] PrologMCP: A Standardized Prolog Tool Interface for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.14935](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14935) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Agnieszka Mensfelt, Adarsh Prabhakaran, Adrian Haret, Vince Trencsenyi, Kostas Stathis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Logic Programming
- **Venue**: Joint Workshop on Statistics and Knowledge Integration for Logic, Learning, Ethical Decisions, and LLMs
- **Summary**: LLM在深层演绎推理上仍失败，符号委派是一种补充。提出了PrologMCP，通过MCP协议将Prolog作为状态化工具暴露给LLM，实现可重用翻译-运行-检查-修复循环。实验表明形式化代理在PARARULE-Plus上匹配或超越推理LLM，在困难子集上表现更好。

<h3 id="617">[617] RetailBench: Benchmarking long horizon reasoning and coherent decision making of LLM agents in realistic retail environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15862](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15862) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Linghua Zhang, Jun Wang, Jingtong Wu, Zhisong Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Decision Making, Benchmark
- **Summary**: LLM代理在长期动态环境中决策连贯性未知。提出了RetailBench，基于数据模拟超市运营，将零售管理建模为部分可观测决策过程。评估7个LLM，发现只有少数能完成180天评估，且最强LLM仍远低于最优策略，原因在于证据获取不完整和缺乏长期策略。

<h3 id="618">[618] LLM-as-Code: Agentic Programming for Agent Harness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.15874](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15874) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junjia Qi, Zichuan Fu, Jingtong Gao, Wenlin Zhang, Hanyu Yan, Xian Wu, Xiangyu Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Program Synthesis, Computer-Use Agent
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (AgenticSE)
- **Summary**: 当前LLM代理框架将LLM作为编排者，导致控制流幻觉和不可靠完成。提出了代理编程，程序控制所有控制流，LLM仅作为自适应组件（LLM-as-Code），只在需要推理或生成时调用。在计算机使用代理案例中，大幅改进了长期视觉操作序列的稳定性。

<h3 id="619">[619] Know Your Limits : On the Faithfulness of LLMs as Solvers and Autoformalizers in Legal Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16118](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16118) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Olivia Peiyu Wang, Sanna Wong-Toropainen, Daneshvar Amrollahi, Ryan Bai, Tashvi Bansal, Arush Garg, Leilani H. Gilpin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LO
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Legal AI, Faithfulness
- **Venue**: AI4Law and AI4Math workshops at ICML
- **Summary**: LLM在推理任务上的强性能是否反映忠实逻辑推理？对比纯LLM分类、LLM形式推理和求解器形式推理，发现引入形式结构提高准确率但不保证忠实性。识别出三种失败模式，包括范围洗白、隐式约束盲视和程序合成失败，表明基准准确率与逻辑忠实性之间存在差距。

<h3 id="620">[620] AI Research moves towards open and reproducible science</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.16974](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16974) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kevin L Coakley, Thijs Snelleman, Holger Hoos, Odd Erik Gundersen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Research Reproducibility, Open Science, Empirical Analysis
- **Summary**: AI研究的可重复性危机促使社区改进文档实践。分析了2014-2024年五大AI会议的全部论文，发现代码和数据共享的论文比例从11%上升到64%，估计可重复性从28%增至64%。改进先于可重复性检查清单的引入，反映了更广泛的开放科学运动。

<h3 id="621">[621] Nothing from Something: Can a Language Model Discover 0?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.17289](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17289) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Phoebe Zeng, Thomas L. Griffiths, Brenden M. Lake
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Pre-training, OOD Generalization
- **Summary**: 数学发现需要强外推能力，以数字0的发现为例。实验表明GPT-2大小模型在测试时无法推广，但通过几十或几百个示例训练后显著改善。语言预训练减少了所需示例约50%，表明语言能力可以支撑数学发现。

<h3 id="622">[622] FinAcumen: Financial Multimodal Reasoning via Self-Evolving Experience Memory Harness</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.17642](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17642) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pianran Guo, Pengcheng Zhou, Yucheng Jian, Shuhua Chen, Zhonfliang Yang, Linna Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Multimodal Reasoning, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 金融多模态推理需要协调多种证据来源，现有工具增强代理无状态。提出了FinAcumen，通过选择性经验记忆积累金融推理经验，在推理时仅当语义相关性超过阈值时才检索经验。在四个基准上一致提升冻结8B VLM的性能，接近专有模型。

<h3 id="623">[623] Searching for Synergy in Shared Workspace Human-AI Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.18413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18413) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nachiket Kotalwar, Rohini Das, Carolyn Rose
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Agent Interaction, Multi-Agent System, Simulation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on Human-AI Co-Creativity
- **Summary**: 自动AI代理在需要人类判断的任务中仍有限。使用模拟共享工作空间人类-AI团队作为测试平台，发现额外协作者在缺乏协调结构时降低性能。协作脚手架（共享组记忆+模拟人在环门控）提高了性能，特别是在三人团队中。

<h3 id="624">[624] Diffusion Language Models: An Experimental Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.19475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19475) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomas Bertolani, Davide Bucciarelli, Leonardo Zini, Marcella Cornia, Lorenzo Baraldi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Language Modeling, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 扩散语言模型作为自回归的替代范式，但评估不一致。对8种最先进DLM进行系统实验，涵盖推理、编码、翻译等8个基准，分析推理时间因素。揭示了DLM在不同任务和计算预算下的性能-效率权衡，提供了实践见解。

<h3 id="625">[625] ITNet: A Learnable Integral Transform That Subsumes Convolution, Attention, and Recurrence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.19538](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19538) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ashim Dhor, Rasel Mondal, Pin-Yu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Architecture, Universal Approximation, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 该论文指出卷积、循环和Transformer网络在数学上被视为不同的架构，但实际上它们是一个可学习的积分变换的特殊情况。作者提出积分变换网络（ITNet），通过一个小型MLP实现联合依赖于位置和特征的核函数，从而统一这些架构。实验表明，在ImageNet-1K、GLUE等任务上，单一ITNet架构匹配或超过了专门化基线。

<h3 id="626">[626] BIM-Edit: Benchmarking Large Language Models for IFC-Based Building Information Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20146](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20146) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bharathi Kannan Nithyanantham, Clemens Kujat, Tobias Sesterhenn, Stefan Telgmann, Jörn Plönnigs, Christian Bartelt, Stefan Lüdtke
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, BIM, Engineering Design
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型在建筑信息模型（BIM）编辑中的能力评估缺失问题，提出了BIM-Edit基准，包含324个编辑任务，覆盖11个真实建筑模型和36个合成场景。评估显示最佳模型平均得分仅49.5%，没有模型能完全解决超过3.4%的任务，表明当前LLM在结构化工程设计中的巨大差距。

<h3 id="627">[627] DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.20526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20526) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Saimun Habib, Vaishak Belle, Fengxiang He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Counterfactual Reasoning, Probabilistic Programming
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP)
- **Summary**: 神经符号系统DeepProbLog仅支持关联推理，缺乏反事实推理的因果语义。本文提出DeepSWIP，利用神经物化将固定上下文神经谓词转化为普通ProbLog选择，并通过单世界干预程序（SWIP）和加权模型计数计算反事实。实验在MPI3D数据集上验证了变换准确性，并实现了2.14倍的推理加速。

<h3 id="628">[628] Explanations for Automatic Speech Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2302.14062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2302.14062) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaoliang Wu, Peter Bell, Ajitha Rajan
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Automatic Speech Recognition, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICASSP 2023
- **Summary**: 针对自动语音识别（ASR）的质量评估，本文通过提供转录的因果解释来增强系统理解。采用统计故障定位（SFL）和因果方法，以及适应ASR的LIME技术，在Google API、Sphinx和DeepSpeech上评估，从Commonvoice数据集选取100个音频样本。

<h3 id="629">[629] Are LLMs Effective Negotiators? Systematic Evaluation of the Multifaceted Capabilities of LLMs in Negotiation Dialogues</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2402.13550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13550) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Deuksin Kwon, Emily Weiss, Tara Kulshrestha, Kushal Chawla, Gale M. Lucas, Jonathan Gratch
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Dialogue System, Theory of Mind
- **Venue**: Findings of EMNLP 2024
- **Summary**: 谈判需要理解语境、心理理论（ToM）、策略推理和有效沟通等多种能力，而当前缺乏对LLM在谈判中系统性的评估。本文系统分析了LLM在谈判各阶段的多方面能力，发现GPT-4在多数任务上表现优异，但在主观评估和生成上下文适当且策略优势的响应方面存在挑战。

<h3 id="630">[630] NeuPAN: Direct Point Robot Navigation with End-to-End Model-based Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.06828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.06828) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruihua Han, Shuai Wang, Shuaijun Wang, Zeqing Zhang, Jianjun Chen, Shijie Lin, Chengyang Li, Chengzhong Xu, Yonina C. Eldar, Qi Hao, Jia Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotics, Motion Planning, End-to-End Learning
- **Venue**: TRO 2025
- **Summary**: 非完整机器人在未知杂乱环境中导航需要精确感知和运动控制，现有方法存在误差传递问题。本文提出NeuPAN，一个端到端模型学习框架，直接将原始点云映射到潜在距离特征空间以生成无碰撞运动，并使用可插拔的近端交替最小化网络（PAN）。在多种机器人和环境中的实验表明，NeuPAN在准确性、效率和泛化能力上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="631">[631] Transcribing Bengali Text with Regional Dialects to IPA using District Guided Tokens</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.17407](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.17407) (replaced)
- **Authors**: S M Jishanul Islam, Sadia Ahmmed, Sahid Hossain Mustakim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Multilingual Learning, Sequence-to-Sequence
- **Summary**: 孟加拉语文本到国际音标（IPA）的准确转写面临方言、外来词等挑战。本文提出区域引导令牌（DGT）技术，通过在输入序列前添加地区令牌来指导模型生成IPA。在包含孟加拉国六个地区的数据集上微调多种Transformer模型，ByT5模型表现最佳，优于mT5等词级模型。

<h3 id="632">[632] A Generalization Bound for Nearly-Linear Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.06765](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.06765) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Eugene Golikov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Generalization Bound, Deep Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 现有的神经网络泛化界通常需要训练后评估，本文针对近线性网络提出了一种先验的泛化界，即无需实际训练即可评估。该界在接近线性的网络上非平凡，是首个具有此性质的神经网络的非平凡泛化界。

<h3 id="633">[633] Enhancing Cognitive Workload Classification Using Integrated LSTM Layers and CNNs for fNIRS Data Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2407.15901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15901) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mehshan Ahmed Khan, Houshyar Asadi, Mohammad Reza Chalak Qazani, Adetokunbo Arogbonlo, Siamak Pedrammehr, Adnan Anwar, Asim Bhatti, Saeid Nahavandi, Chee Peng Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: fNIRS Classification, Cognitive Load Classification, Deep Learning
- **Venue**: Computers 2025
- **Summary**: 功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）用于认知状态分类，传统机器学习方法预处理复杂且精度低，且多数仅区分两级认知负荷。本文在CNN中集成LSTM层以捕获时间依赖性，在fNIRS数据上将深度学习模型准确率从97.40%提升至97.92%。

<h3 id="634">[634] ARVO: Atlas of Reproducible Vulnerabilities for Open-Source Software</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2408.02153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.02153) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiang Mei, Jordi Del Castillo, Pulkit Singh Singaria, Haoran Xi, Abdelouahab Benchikh, Tiffany Bao, Ruoyu Wang, Yan Shoshitaishvili, Adam Doupé, Hammond Pearce, Brendan Dolan-Gavitt
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Vulnerability Detection, Research Reproducibility
- **Venue**: IEEE European Symposium on Security and Privacy (EuroS&P) 2026
- **Summary**: 现有漏洞数据集在可重复性、数量和多样性之间存在权衡，可重复性常被忽略。本文提出一种方法构建ARVO数据集，重现OSS-Fuzz中超过6100个真实世界漏洞，覆盖311个项目，提供可重复构建、触发和分析的漏洞形式。评估显示81%的漏洞可重现，补丁定位准确率达89.4%。

<h3 id="635">[635] Exploration of LLMs, EEG, and behavioral data to measure and support attention and sleep</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2408.07822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07822) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Akane Sano, Judith Amores, Mary Czerwinski
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: EEG Analysis, Sleep Staging, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 探索利用大型语言模型（LLM）从脑电图（EEG）和活动数据中检测和改善注意力和睡眠。结果表明LLM能基于人类文本行为特征估计睡眠质量并提供个性化建议，但基于EEG和活动数据检测注意力、睡眠阶段和质量需要更多训练数据和领域知识。

<h3 id="636">[636] Measuring Human Contribution in AI-Assisted Content Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2408.14792](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.14792) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yueqi Xie, Tao Qi, Jingwei Yi, Xiyuan Yang, Ryan Whalen, Junming Huang, Qian Ding, Yu Xie, Xing Xie, Fangzhao Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Content, Human-AI Collaboration, Information Theory
- **Summary**: 随着生成式AI普及，内容创作中人类贡献程度的衡量成为挑战。本文提出基于信息论的框架，通过计算人类输入与AI辅助输出的互信息相对于AI辅助输出自信息的比例，量化人类的信息贡献。实验表明该度量能有效区分不同创意领域中的不同程度的人类贡献。

<h3 id="637">[637] Brownian Motion with a Pulse: A Biostatistician's Guide to Diffusions, Bridges, Functional PCA, and First-Passage Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2408.16011](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.16011) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Eliuvish Han Cui
- **Subjects**: stat.AP; cs.AI; math.PR; math.ST
- **Tags**: Statistical Analysis, Biostatistics, Tutorial
- **Summary**: 布朗运动是连续时间不确定性的数学语言，本文为生物统计学家提供了关于布朗运动构造、性质及其在纵向生物标志物、退化建模等中应用的教程。包含Karhunen-Loève展开、功能主成分分析、布朗桥等工具，并通过文学和历史档案的跨领域示例使概念具体化。

<h3 id="638">[638] LAYUP: Asynchronous decentralized gradient descent with LAYer-wise UPdates</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.05985](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.05985) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cabrel Teguemne Fokam, Marcel Nieveler, Lukas König, Khaleelulla Khan Nazeer, David Kappel, Anand Subramoney
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Distributed Training, Communication Efficiency, Asynchronous Optimization
- **Summary**: 同步集中式分布式训练通信开销大，现有通信高效算法对掉队者敏感或存在参数漂移。本文提出LayUp，一种异步去中心化SGD方法，采用逐层更新和随机八卦通信，无需缓冲。理论证明了梯度偏差上界和收敛性，实验表明在视觉和语言任务上收敛速度比同步数据并行快约32%，比同类通信高效算法快约27%。

<h3 id="639">[639] CATCH: Channel-Aware multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Frequency Patching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2410.12261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12261) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xingjian Wu, Xiangfei Qiu, Zhengyu Li, Yihang Wang, Jilin Hu, Chenjuan Guo, Hui Xiong, Bin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Anomaly Detection, Anomaly Detection, Time Series Analysis
- **Venue**: ICLR 2025
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/decisionintelligence/CATCH)
- **Summary**: 多变量时间序列异常检测中，现有方法难以捕捉细粒度频率特征和通道相关性。本文提出CATCH框架，基于频率分块，并设计通道融合模块（CFM）通过补丁级掩码生成器和掩码注意力机制发现合适的通道相关性。在10个真实数据集和12个合成数据集上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="640">[640] GAPartManip: A Large-scale Part-centric Dataset for Material-Agnostic Articulated Object Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.18276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.18276) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Cui, Chengyang Zhao, Songlin Wei, Jiazhao Zhang, Haoran Geng, Yaran Chen, Haoran Li, He Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robotic Manipulation, 3D Vision, Dataset
- **Venue**: ICRA 2025
- **Summary**: 对透明盖子和反光手柄等具有挑战性的关节物体操纵，现有基于深度感知和姿态检测的方法易受不完美深度感知影响且缺乏部件级交互多样性。本文提出大规模部件中心数据集GAPartManip，包含照片级材质随机化和部件级动作交互姿态注释。集成多种深度估计和交互姿态预测方法，展示了在仿真和真实场景中的鲁棒性。

<h3 id="641">[641] TIF: Learning Temporal Invariance in Android Malware Detectors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.05098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.05098) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinran Zheng, Shuo Yang, Edith C.H. Ngai, Suman Jana, Lorenzo Cavallaro
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Malware Detection, Contrastive Learning, Temporal Invariance
- **Venue**: IEEE Transactions on Software Engineering (TSE)
- **Summary**: 针对Android恶意软件检测器随时间性能下降的问题，本文提出TIF（时间不变性训练框架），通过多代理对比学习和不变性梯度对齐来学习稳定特征。在长达十年的数据集上，TIF在早期部署阶段表现优异，优于现有方法。

<h3 id="642">[642] From Markov to Laplace: How Mamba In-Context Learns Markov Chains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.10178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.10178) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Marco Bondaschi, Nived Rajaraman, Xiuying Wei, Kannan Ramchandran, Razvan Pascanu, Caglar Gulcehre, Michael Gastpar, Ashok Vardhan Makkuva
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: In-Context Learning, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 为理解Mamba的上下文学习能力，本文研究其在马尔可夫链上的学习行为，发现单层Mamba能高效学习最优拉普拉斯平滑估计器。该工作首次建立了Mamba与最优统计估计之间的理论联系。

<h3 id="643">[643] AI-Augmented Thyroid Scintigraphy for Robust Classification of Disease</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.00366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.00366) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maziar Sabouri, Ghasem Hajianfar, Alireza Rafiei Sardouei, Milad Yazdani, Azin Asadzadeh, Soroush Bagheri, Mohsen Arabi, Seyed Rasoul Zakavi, Emran Askari, Atena Aghaee, Sam Wiseman, Dena Shahriari, Habib Zaidi, Arman Rahmim
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Flow Matching, Data Augmentation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MaziarSabouri/Stable-Diffusion-Scintigraphy-Augmentation)
- **Summary**: 针对甲状腺显像深度学习模型面临数据有限和不平衡的问题，本文比较了Stable Diffusion、Flow Matching和传统数据增强策略。Flow Matching方法在F1和AUC上表现最佳，生成图像质量最高（最低FID 0.66，KID 0.83）。

<h3 id="644">[644] ACTIVA: Amortized Causal Effect Estimation via Transformer-based Variational Autoencoder</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.01290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01290) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Andreas Sauter, Saber Salehkaleybar, Frank van Harmelen, Aske Plaat, Erman Acar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Variational Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 针对从观测数据预测干预分布的难题，本文提出ACTIVA，一个基于Transformer的条件变分自编码器，实现干预分布的摊销估计。在合成数据和基因表达模拟中，ACTIVA优于基线方法，减少了虚假的非后代效应。

<h3 id="645">[645] FairSAM: Fair Classification on Corrupted Image Data Through Sharpness-Aware Minimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.22934](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.22934) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yucong Dai, Jie Ji, Xiaolong Ma, Yongkai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Image Classification, Sharpness-Aware Minimization
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Summary**: 针对图像分类模型在数据损坏时性能下降且存在偏见的问题，本文提出FairSAM，将公平性策略融入Sharpness-Aware Minimization。实验表明，FairSAM在多个数据集上平衡了鲁棒性和公平性。

<h3 id="646">[646] Autonomous and Self-Adapting System for Synthetic Media Detection and Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.03615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.03615) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Aref Azizpour, Tai D. Nguyen, Matthew C. Stamm
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Open-Set Recognition, Image Forensics
- **Summary**: 针对现有合成图像检测系统静态且对新生成器退化的问题，本文提出自适应系统，采用开放集识别和可进化嵌入空间，通过无监督聚类识别新生成器。实验表明，该方法在生成环境变化下保持了鲁棒性能。

<h3 id="647">[647] Efficient Traffic State Prediction With Dynamic Joint Spatio-Temporal Relation Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.08061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08061) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhifeng Hao, Kai Hu, Juncai Zhang, Zhidan Zhao, Zhengming Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Traffic Forecasting, Graph Neural Network, Spatiotemporal Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Jessez2/STEI-PCN)
- **Summary**: 针对交通预测中复杂的时空交互问题，本文提出STEI-PCN，一种高效纯卷积网络，通过时空编码和关系推断捕获局部联合依赖。在多个数据集上，STEI-PCN以较低训练和推理成本实现竞争精度。

<h3 id="648">[648] Causally Fair Node Classification on Non-IID Graph Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.01652](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.01652) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yucong Dai, Lu Zhang, Yaowei Hu, Susan Gauch, Yongkai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Fairness, Causal Inference
- **Venue**: TMLR
- **Summary**: 针对图数据公平学习中忽略因果关系的不足，本文提出MPVA，一种消息传递变分自编码器，用于因果公平节点分类。在非IID设置下，MPVA有效近似干预分布，相比传统方法减少了偏差。

<h3 id="649">[649] Adaptive GoGI-Skip: Coupling Goal-Gradient Importance with Dynamic Uncertainty for Efficient Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.08392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.08392) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ren Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Token Compression
- **Summary**: 针对Chain-of-Thought推理中速度与精度的权衡，本文提出Adaptive GoGI-Skip框架，通过目标梯度重要性（GoGI）与自适应动态跳过的耦合，实现了超过45%的令牌减少和2倍加速，且无精度损失。

<h3 id="650">[650] Brain-Inspired Stochastic Joint Embedding Representation Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.11129](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11129) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Makoto Yamada, Kian Ming A. Chai, Ayoub Rhim, Satoki Ishikawa, Mohammad Sabokrou, Yao-Hung Hubert Tsai
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Representation Learning, Brain-Inspired Learning
- **Summary**: 针对自监督学习未充分利用生物视觉处理机制的问题，本文提出PhiNet v2，一种类脑架构，处理时间视觉输入，无需强数据增强。在多个基准上取得竞争性能，可有效从序列输入中学习。

<h3 id="651">[651] Towards Adaptive Categories: Dimensional Governance for Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.11579](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.11579) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zeynep Engin, David Hand
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: AI Governance, AI Safety, Agentic AI
- **Summary**: 针对传统静态分类治理框架对动态AI代理不足的问题，本文提出维度治理框架，跟踪决策权威、过程自主性和问责制。该框架支持自适应分类和阈值监测，为前沿AI治理提供了更灵活的方法。

<h3 id="652">[652] Render-FM: Feedforward Model for Real-time Photorealistic Volumetric Rendering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2505.17338](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.17338) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhongpai Gao, Benjamin Planche, Meng Zheng, Anwesa Choudhuri, Terrence Chen, Ziyan Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, 3D Gaussian Splatting, 3D Reconstruction
- **Venue**: ECCV 2026
- **Summary**: 针对CT体绘制中NeRF和3DGS需要大量逐扫描优化的问题，本文提出Render-FM，一个前馈模型，在2.8秒内直接回归6DGS参数，实现500倍加速。该模型泛化到未见解剖部位和新传输函数。

<h3 id="653">[653] Sarc7: Evaluating Sarcasm Detection and Generation with Seven Types and Emotion-Informed Techniques</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.00658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00658) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Raina Gao, Alyssa Jeong, Lang Xiong, Yicheng Fu, Sean O&#39;Brien, Vasu Sharma, Kevin Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sentiment Analysis, LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering
- **Venue**: EMNLP WiNLP and COLM Melt, Solar, PragLM, and Origen
- **Summary**: 针对讽刺检测和生成的挑战，本文引入Sarc7基准，分类7种讽刺类型，并提出基于情感的提示技术。Gemini 2.5在情感提示下F1达0.3664，人类评估者更偏好情感驱动生成。

<h3 id="654">[654] "Is This Really a Human Peer Supporter?": Misalignments Between Peer Supporters and Experts in LLM-Supported Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.09354](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09354) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kellie Yu Hui Sim, Roy Ka-Wei Lee, Kenny Tsu Wei Choo
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mental Health AI, Human-Computer Interaction, LLM Application
- **Venue**: CSCW 2026
- **Summary**: 针对AI支持的同伴支持系统，本文呈现并评估了一个LLM系统。研究发现，同伴支持者积极参与，但专家指出响应中缺少困扰提示和过早建议。这凸显了标准化、心理导向培训的必要性。

<h3 id="655">[655] "I Said Things I Needed to Hear Myself": Peer Support as an Emotional, Organisational, and Sociotechnical Practice in Singapore</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.09362](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.09362) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kellie Yu Hui Sim, Kenny Tsu Wei Choo
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Mental Health, User Study
- **Summary**: 针对新加坡数字同伴支持平台设计不足的问题，本文通过对20位同伴支持者的访谈，揭示其动机、情感劳动和社会文化维度。提出了文化响应型数字工具的设计方向。

<h3 id="656">[656] ToxSyn-PT: A Synthetic Fine-Grained Dataset of Minority-Targeted Toxic Language in Portuguese</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.10245](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.10245) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Iago Alves Brito, Julia Soares Dollis, Fernanda Bufon Farber, Diogo Fernandes, Arlindo R. Galvão Filho
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hate Speech Detection, Data Synthesis, Multilingual Learning
- **Venue**: LREC 2026
- **Summary**: 针对葡萄牙语仇恨言论检测缺乏细粒度数据集的问题，本文构建ToxSyn-PT，包含9个受保护少数群体的多标签数据及非毒性反例。实验发现社交领域模型泛化失败，宏观F1可能掩盖真实行为。

<h3 id="657">[657] From Empirical Evaluation to Context-Aware Enhancement: Repairing Regression Errors with LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.13182](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.13182) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anh Ho, Thanh Le-Cong, Bach Le, Christine Rizkallah
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Repair
- **Summary**: 该论文针对自动程序修复（APR）技术在回归错误上的有效性未充分探索的问题，引入了RegressionBug4APR基准（200个回归错误），评估了基于LLM的APR方法。实验表明，结合错误诱导更改信息的LLM方法修复成功率提升1.6倍。

<h3 id="658">[658] Measuring Intent Comprehension in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2506.16584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16584) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nadav Kunievsky, James A. Evans
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Intent Understanding
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM能否推断用户意图的问题，提出了一个方差分解评估框架，将模型输出变异归因于意图、表达和不确定性。在LLaMA和Gemma模型上实验表明，更大模型通常将更多方差归因于意图，但收益不均。

<h3 id="659">[659] Structure-Aware Compound-Protein Affinity Prediction via Graph Neural Networks with Group Lasso Regularization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.03318](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.03318) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zanyu Shi, Yang Wang, Pathum M. Weerawarna, Jie Zhang, Timothy I. Richardson, Yijie Wang, Kun Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Drug Discovery, Graph Neural Network, Molecular Property Prediction
- **Summary**: 该论文针对化合物-蛋白亲和力预测中关键基团识别困难的问题，提出了图神经网络框架，利用group lasso正则化增强可解释性。在六种酪氨酸蛋白激酶上实验，降低了均方根误差并提高了皮尔逊相关系数。

<h3 id="660">[660] Neural Concept Verifier: Scaling Prover-Verifier Games via Concept Encodings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.07532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07532) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Berkant Turan, Suhrab Asadulla, David Steinmann, Kristian Kersting, Wolfgang Stammer, Sebastian Pokutta
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Concept Bottleneck Model, Interpretability, Neural Network Verification
- **Summary**: 该论文针对Prover-Verifier Games在高维图像上难以应用的问题，提出了Neural Concept Verifier（NCV）框架，结合概念编码与PVG实现可验证分类。实验表明，NCV优于传统基于概念和像素的基线，并缓解捷径行为。

<h3 id="661">[661] DPO Unchained: Your Training Algorithm is Secretly Disentangled in Human Choice Theory (and its Loss' Convexity is Dispensable)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.07855](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07855) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenxuan Zhou, Shujian Zhang, Brice Magdalou, John Lambert, Ehsan Amid, Richard Nock, Andrew Hard
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, RLHF, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 该论文将DPO与人类选择理论框架全面连接，揭示了支持非凸损失和任意人类选择模型嵌入的通用视角。玩具实验验证了框架的有效性，为偏好优化提供了理论基础。

<h3 id="662">[662] CLAR: Learning 3D Representations for Robotic Manipulation by Fusing Masked Reconstruction with Multi-Level Contrastive Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.08262](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08262) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Cui, Chengyang Zhao, Yuhui Chen, Haoran Li, Zhizheng Zhang, Dongbin Zhao, He Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Robotics, 3D Vision, Contrastive Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有3D预训练方法在语义和空间几何特征间的权衡问题，提出了CLAR框架，融合掩码自编码与多级对比学习。在仿真和真实场景中，CLAR在视觉运动策略学习上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="663">[663] Detail++: Training-Free Detail Enhancer for T2I Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.17853](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.17853) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lifeng Chen, Jiner Wang, Zihao Pan, Beier Zhu, Xiaofeng Yang, Chi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 该论文针对文本到图像扩散模型处理复杂提示的挑战，提出了Detail++框架，采用渐进式细节注入策略。在T2I-CompBench等基准上，Detail++显著优于现有方法。

<h3 id="664">[664] FedSA-GCL: A Semi-Asynchronous Federated Graph Learning Framework with Personalized Aggregation and Cluster-Aware Broadcasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.18219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.18219) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhongzheng Yuan, Lianshuai Guo, Xunkai Li, Yinlin Zhu, Wenyu Wang, Meixia Qu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Graph Neural Network, Personalized Federated Learning
- **Venue**: Knowledge-Based Systems
- **Summary**: 该论文针对联邦图学习中同步通信低效且异步方法不适用的难题，提出了FedSA-GCL框架，利用ClusterCast机制高效训练。在多个图数据集上，方法在鲁棒性和效率上分别平均提升1.9%和3.0%。

<h3 id="665">[665] The Ratchet Effect in Silico: How Interaction Drives Cumulative Intelligence in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.21166](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.21166) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ren Zhuang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Collective Intelligence
- **Summary**: 该论文针对LLM训练缺乏内生积累的问题，提出了POLIS框架，通过异构代理交互实现知识文化积累。在数学推理基准上，1-4B参数模型平均提升8.8-18.9分，缩小了与70B+模型的差距。

<h3 id="666">[666] NeedleChain: Measuring Intact Context Comprehension Capability of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2507.22411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.22411) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hyeonseok Moon, Heuiseok Lim
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Long Context, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 该论文针对现有长文本基准高估模型能力的问题，提出了NeedleChain基准，测试模型忠实整合所有证据的能力。实验显示GPT-4o在200 token纯相关上下文中也常失败，并提出了ROPE收缩策略。

<h3 id="667">[667] VRPO: Rethinking Value Modeling for Robust RL under Noisy Supervision in LLM Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.03058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.03058) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dingwei Zhu, Shihan Dou, Zhiheng Xi, Senjie Jin, Guoqiang Zhang, Jiazheng Zhang, Junjie Ye, Mingxu Chai, Enyu Zhou, Ming Zhang, Yuhui Wang, Caishuang Huang, Chenhao Huang, Yunke Zhang, Yuran Wang, Tao Gui, Qi Zhang, Xipeng Qiu, Xuanjing Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, RLHF, Value Learning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对强化学习在噪声奖励下策略不稳定的问题，提出了VRPO框架，通过增强价值模型吸收噪声并产生可靠优势。在多轮对话等任务上，VRPO优于PPO和GRPO等基线。

<h3 id="668">[668] ProMed: Shapley Information Gain Guided Reinforcement Learning for Proactive Medical LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.13514](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13514) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongxin Ding, Baixiang Huang, Yue Fang, Weibin Liao, Xinke Jiang, Jinyang Zhang, Yinghao Zhu, Zheng Li, Liantao Ma, Junfeng Zhao, Yasha Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hxxding/ProMed)

<h3 id="669">[669] Mitigating Cross-Image Information Leakage in Multi-Image Understanding with Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2508.13744](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13744) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yeji Park, Minyoung Lee, Sanghyuk Chun, Junsuk Choe
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Multi-Image Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yejipark-m/FOCUS)
- **Summary**: 该论文针对视觉语言模型在多图像输入时性能下降的问题，发现跨图像信息泄露并提出了FOCUS方法。通过随机噪声掩盖和logits聚合，在多个多图像基准上一致提升性能，并泛化到视频理解。

<h3 id="670">[670] Meta-learning ecological priors from large language models explains human learning and decision making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.00116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.00116) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Akshay K. Jagadish, Mirko Thalmann, Julian Coda-Forno, Marcel Binz, Eric Schulz
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, Meta-Learning, Decision Making
- **Summary**: 该论文针对人类认知是否适应真实任务统计结构的问题，提出了生态理性分析框架，利用LLM生成任务并通过元学习推导ERMI模型。在15个实验中，ERMI超越了多个认知模型，在逐试次预测中更优。

<h3 id="671">[671] The Efficiency Frontier: Classical Shadows versus Direct Quantum Measurement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2509.06218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.06218) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuowei Ma, Junyu Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Quantum Measurement
- **Summary**: 该论文针对量子信息提取中经典阴影与直接测量效率比较的问题，进行了全栈资源分析，确定了效率前沿。表明在大量可观测量且低泡利权重时经典阴影更优，并分析了不同量子计算机上的资源消耗。

<h3 id="672">[672] Graph Alignment via Dual-Pass Spectral Encoding and Latent Space Communication</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Graph Learning, Graph Alignment
